The optimized framework projects 2026 remittances at USD 9,001 million under stable conditions. These findings highlight the structural dependence of remittances on global economies, emphasizing the need for robust exchange rate policies, skilled migration, and formal financial channels to enhance long-term economic resilience.

</details>


### [112] [COCOLogic-V2: Identifying Logical Inconsistencies via Truly Hard-Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28194)
*David Steinmann,Antonia Wüst,Kristian Kersting,Wolfgang Stammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 COCOLogic-V2 数据集，通过细分样本边界与逻辑设置，揭示了当前模型在复杂真实场景下视觉归纳推理的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释模型（如 CBM 和程序合成）评估主要局限于简单任务，在大规模真实图像的复杂推理能力方面尚缺乏深入研究。

Method: 构建了 COCOLogic-V2 数据集，这是一个针对真实世界图像的以物体为中心的视觉归纳推理数据集，覆盖了一阶逻辑的子集。通过将样本分类为正例、近边界（NB）负例和远边界（FB）负例进行细粒度诊断分析。

Result: 现有模型在区分正例和远边界（FB）负例时表现良好，但在处理近边界（NB）负例时容易失败；同时发现感知噪声和大规模规则诱导的搜索空间在少样本场景下显著增加了模型推理的难度。

Conclusion: 视觉归纳推理仍是一项尚未解决的挑战。COCOLogic-V2 为研究人员提供了评估和推动该领域方法发展的基准框架。

Abstract: While interpretable models such as concept bottleneck models (CBMs) and program synthesis methods enable verification of model decisions, their evaluation is typically limited to simple tasks, leaving complex reasoning on real-world images largely unexplored. We introduce COCOLogic-V2, an object-centric dataset for visual inductive reasoning on real-world images covering a broad subset of first-order logic. By categorizing samples into positive variants, near-boundary (NB), and far-from-boundary (FB) negatives, COCOLogic-V2 enables fine-grained diagnosis of model accountability. Our evaluations show that models tend to separate positive and FB samples well but fail on NB samples, while perceptual noise and large rule-induced search spaces pose additional challenges in few-shot settings. Together, these results highlight that visual inductive reasoning remains an open challenge and COCOLogic-V2 provides a concrete foundation for advancing methods in this direction.

</details>


### [113] [Physics-Informed Neural Network with Transfer Learning for State Estimation in Lithium-Ion Batteries using the Single Particle Model with Electrolyte](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28220)
*Gift Modekwe,Qiugang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种应用于电池SPMe模型物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的迁移学习框架，通过预训练和微调策略加速收敛，解决了不同工况下PINNs训练效率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法计算昂贵，而基于从零开始训练的PINNs在面对不同电池化学成分或工况时，存在训练需求高且收敛速度慢的问题。

Method: 开发了一个迁移学习框架。首先预训练模型以学习通用的电化学动力学，随后通过权重迁移、冻结部分层以及微调剩余参数（包括关键电化学变量的估计）来适配目标电池。

Result: 通过PyBaMM验证表明，该方法能够实现准确的电压预测，有效减少了训练时间，并实现了跨电池的高效泛化。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于迁移学习的SPMe-PINNs框架，在保证电化学一致性的前提下，显著降低了训练成本并提升了不同电池化学体系/工况下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful tool for solving nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs), including battery electrochemical models. They typically en-force conservation laws within the loss function to ensure physically consistent solutions. Tradi-tional numerical methods such as finite difference, finite volume, and finite element techniques, re-ly on discretization and can be computationally expensive for nonlinear systems. To address this challenge, PINNs offer improved scalability, particularly for reduced-order models like the single particle model with electrolyte (SPMe). The SPMe describes lithium-ion battery dynamics through coupled diffusion, transport, reaction kinetics, and voltage equations. Despite these advantages, training SPMe-based PINNs from scratch for different battery chemistries or operating conditions is demanding and often leads to slow convergence. To overcome this limitation, this work introduces a transfer learning framework for SPMe-PINNs. The model is first pretrained to learn general elec-trochemical dynamics and then adapted to a target battery by transferring weights, freezing se-lected layers, and fine tuning the remaining parameters, including estimating key electrochemical variables. Validation using PyBaMM demonstrates accurate voltage prediction, indicating that the proposed approach preserves electrochemical consistency while reducing training time and ena-bling efficient generalization across batteries.

</details>


### [114] [Estimation--Prediction Tradeoff in Causal Probabilistic Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28225)
*Aniq Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了时态链路预测中“估计-预测”的权衡问题，指出高预测精度可能掩盖了本质的不确定性，并提出了一种基于因果框架的评估方法，以区分模型误差与过程不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时态链路预测通常仅评估未见边的预测性能，这会导致模型误差（可约）与本质的不确定性（不可约）混淆。作者旨在研究这种隐含的“估计-预测”权衡，并指出当前评价指标的局限性。

Method: 1. 构建了一个具有瞬态边和已知因果结构的概率时态图生成因果框架；2. 对二元逻辑回归参数化推导了Cramér-Rao界；3. 通过实验验证了参数估计误差与不可约预测损失之间的权衡关系。

Result: 揭示了二元逻辑模型中存在一种内在权衡：最大化Fisher信息和提升参数恢复能力的区域，往往具有最高熵，使得预测难度增加；实验证实预测精度升高并不直接等同于更好地掌握了因果机制。

Conclusion: 单一的预测准确率无法衡量模型是否真正学到了潜在的因果机制。研究强调在时态链路预测中，必须区分可约的模型误差与不可约的过程不确定性，并建议使用包含因果参数恢复的综合基准。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction is usually evaluated by predictive performance on unseen edges, but in probabilistic temporal graphs this criterion can conflate model error with irreducible uncertainty. We study this issue by characterising an inherent estimation--prediction tradeoff in binary logistic models where regimes that maximise Fisher information and improve parameter recoverability are also those with the highest entropy, making individual predictions intrinsically harder even under perfect parameter recovery. We propose a probabilistic causal framework for generating temporal graphs with transient edges and known ground-truth causal structure, allowing temporal link prediction to be evaluated jointly with causal parameter recovery. For the proposed binary logistic parametrisation, we derive the Cramér--Rao bound and validate the tradeoff between parameter estimation error and irreducible predictive loss. Our results show that predictive accuracy alone may not reflect whether a model has learned the underlying causal mechanism, motivating benchmarks that distinguish reducible model error from intrinsic process uncertainty.

</details>


### [115] [Parameter Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT) for Network Traffic Prediction via Dynamic Urban Congestion Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28274)
*Abdolazim Rezaei,Mehdi Sookhak,Mahboobeh Haghparast*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PEHT 框架，通过引入 LoRA 和多模态融合策略，在低参数量下实现了对城市蜂窝网络流量的精准预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市蜂窝网络流量受复杂的移动性、拥堵动态和异构用户行为影响，传统的预测模型难以在保持高精度的同时有效处理多源异构数据与参数规模。

Method: 提出 PEHT (Parameter-Efficient Hybrid Transformer) 框架：1. 将网络特征与城市移动性/拥堵特征分离处理；2. 在 Transformer 编码器中集成 LoRA (Low-Rank Adaptation) 以减少训练参数；3. 使用多模态融合策略将外部特征注入解码器。

Result: 在 Telecom Italia Milan 数据集及合成拥堵场景下的实验结果表明，PEHT 在 RMSE、MAE 和 R² 指标上均优于现有的先进基准模型。

Conclusion: PEHT 通过 LoRA 技术实现了高效参数优化，并结合多模态数据融合有效提升了城市蜂窝网络流量预测的准确性，证明了将外部上下文（如移动性与拥堵数据）融入 Transformer 架构的显著价值。

Abstract: Accurate network traffic prediction is a critical element for efficient resource allocation in dynamic urban cellular networks. However, prediction remains challenging because network demand is influenced by complex mobility patterns, congestion dynamics, and heterogeneous user behavior. This paper introduces the Parameter-Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT), a network traffic prediction framework that integrates urban mobility and congestion information into a Transformer-based architecture. PEHT separates primary network communication features from secondary urban mobility features and incorporates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) into the Transformer encoder to reduce the number of trainable parameters while maintaining high predictive accuracy. A multimodal fusion strategy then injects external mobility and congestion features into the decoder to improve traffic forecasting. Experiments on the Telecom Italia Milan dataset and multiple synthetic congestion scenarios show that PEHT outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of RMSE, MAE, and $R^2$. The implementation is available in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [116] [Democratic ICAI: Debating Our Way to Steering Principles from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28294)
*Kevin Kingslin,Anish Natekar,Ashutosh Ranjan,Vivek Srivastava,Savita Bhat,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了 Democratic ICAI，通过结构化辩论提取多维偏好理性，显著提升了偏好对齐的解释性与决策预测精准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有偏好对齐方法无法捕捉人类复杂决策背后的深层逻辑、以往逆向宪法 AI (ICAI) 单一解释路径过于简化的问题。

Method: 提出“民主化逆向宪法 AI”（Democratic ICAI），通过结构化角色扮演辩论，从人类偏好标签中提取多种竞争性假设及其理据，并据此推导出更全面、细致的原则，进而指导 LLM 及决策树模型进行决策建模。

Result: 在 MuCE-Pref 和 LiTBench 基准测试中，Democratic ICAI 显著优于现有的推理性提示和基于原则的基线方法，在预测准确性上有所提升，且生成的宪法原则更受 LLM 标注人员认可。

Conclusion: Democratic ICAI 通过引入竞争性辩论机制，提升了人类偏好对齐的解释性与准确性，为构建更透明、更符合人类价值观的 AI 对齐提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Preference-based alignment often struggles to capture the reasoning that underlies human judgments. Many evaluations rely on multiple interacting criteria, yet pairwise labels reveal only the final choice rather than the considerations that shape preferences. Inverse Constitutional AI (ICAI) improves interpretability in decision making by summarizing preferences into natural-language principles, but its single-pass explanations miss much of the nuance involved in complex decisions. We introduce Democratic ICAI, a novel approach that gathers multiple competing rationales through structured persona debate, offering a broader and more expressive account of the factors influencing each comparison. From these richer signals, we derive clearer and more comprehensive steering principles and use them to guide decision modeling through both LLM-based and decision-tree judges. Experiments on creative preference benchmarks, MuCE-Pref and LiTBench, across multiple creative task categories show that Democratic ICAI yields a more faithful preference structure. It improves average preference prediction across tasks relative to deliberative prompting and principle-based baselines, while producing constitutions that LLM annotators prefer.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [How far does a random forest generalize from a 54-run LAMMPS+SPICA benchmark?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27695)
*Dennis Alves Pedersen,Paulo Henrique Leme Ramalho,Fábio Andrijauskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种基于随机森林的冷启动代理模型，仅需少量基准测试数据即可预测分子动力学软件在HPC集群上的混合执行配置性能，大幅节省了计算资源分配预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高性能计算（HPC）集群上为分子动力学负载寻找近乎最优的MPI+OpenMP混合配置，传统方法依赖穷举法，极其消耗计算资源及预算，亟需一种低成本、高效的性能预测方法。

Method: 基于54次LAMMPS+SPICA模拟数据构建冷启动随机森林（Random Forest）代理模型；使用9个拓扑与资源特征预测循环时间及四个内部计时指标；通过留一维度交叉验证（Leave-one-dimension-out）探究模型的泛化能力与硬件架构依赖性。

Result: 循环时间预测的样本内平均绝对误差为0.49秒（相对误差4.0%）；分析显示OpenMP线程数和MPI/OpenMP比例是影响性能的关键特征；模型不仅能准确表征单节点、多节点等各性能区域，还能为后续深度基准测试提供可靠的优化方向指示。

Conclusion: 该代理模型能有效预测MPI+OpenMP配置性能，特别是在同一硬件体系结构区域内表现出色。它提供了一种可解释的性能“地图”，帮助研究人员在大幅降低基准测试成本的前提下，评估配置并缩小最佳优化范围，尽管跨架构预测精度会下降。

Abstract: Selecting near-optimal hybrid MPI+OpenMP configurations for molecular dynamics workloads on modern HPC clusters has traditionally required exhaustive empirical benchmarking, consuming allocation budget proportional to the number of configurations evaluated. This work investigates whether a cold-start Random Forest surrogate, trained once on a small, structured benchmark dataset, can reliably predict execution performance and recommend high-performing configurations without further cluster runs. The training dataset comprises 54 LAMMPS+SPICA runs of the antimicrobial peptide Tritrpticin on a hydrated DOPC bilayer (4 354 coarse-grained beads), spanning 18 hybrid configurations on 1-8 AMD EPYC 7662 nodes of the Lovelace cluster at CENAPAD-SP, with three independent replications each. Nine topology and resource features feed five regressors that predict loop time and four internal LAMMPS timing fractions (Pair, Kspace, Comm, Modify). In-sample mean absolute error is 0.49 s on loop time (4.0 % relative). Feature importance localizes predictive signal in topology variables (OpenMP threads and MPI/OpenMP ratio dominate; raw node and core counts contribute under 3 %). Leave-one-dimension-out generalization reveals that accuracy is governed by hardware regime membership: within a common regime (single-node, multi-node, or shared threading tier) the surrogate ranks configurations correctly, and degrades when targets cross architectural boundaries. The result is an interpretable map of where the surrogate's recommendations can be trusted, useful for scoping further benchmark campaigns at a fraction of their nominal cost.

</details>


### [118] [Lightweight Multi-Vehicle Collaborative Perception Acceleration with Fusion Position Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27750)
*Wenzhao Zhang,Shujun Han,Haixiao Gao,Mengying Sun,Bizhu Wang,Xiaodong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为FALL的轻量化加速方案，通过调整深度神经网络线性层间的特征融合位置，在保证感知精度的前提下，显著降低了多车协作感知的计算与通信延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决自动驾驶中多车协作感知（MvCP）在有限计算与通信资源下的实时性挑战。

Method: 1. 提出针对中间融合（IF）MvCP的轻量化加速方案；2. 定义了“条件可加性”（conditional additivity）并分析其在DNN线性层中的应用；3. 提出层间特征融合位置调整（FALL）方案。

Result: 仿真结果显示，FALL方案在有限通信资源下可将MvCP延迟降低高达74.8%，在有限计算资源下降低高达30.3%。

Conclusion: 通过提出FALL方案，在理论上实现了无精度损失的MvCP加速，并经实验证明在有限计算和通信资源下能显著降低延迟。

Abstract: Multi-vehicle collaborative perception (MvCP) is considered as a key technology to facilitate automated driving (AD), where real-time MvCP under limited resources is significant for reliable AD. In this paper, we formulate a lightweight acceleration scheme for intermediate-fusion (IF) MvCP, which can adapt to both situations of limited computation and communication resources. We provide a relaxed definition conditional additivity and analyze the conditional additivity for various DNN linear layers. On this basis, we focus on the IF-MvCP based on additive feature fusion, and derive the MvCP precision consistency of the forward and backward feature fusion position (FP) adjustments among linear layers. Through experiments, we further validate the precision consistency of the FP adjustment method. Moreover, we propose an FP adjustment among linear layers (FALL) scheme for MvCP acceleration without precision loss theoretically. Simulation results show that the proposed FALL can reduce MvCP latency by up to 74.8% under limited communication resources and by up to 30.3% under limited computation resources.

</details>


### [119] [Optimizing Teacher-Student Partitioning for Scalable Knowledge Distillation on HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27797)
*Adrian P. Dieguez,Victor Conchello Vendrell,Alex Batlle,Vinnam Kim,Jordi Ros-Giralt,Harris Teague*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过优化高性能计算集群中教师与学生模型的非对称并行策略（解耦分区+垂直/水平并行），在知识蒸馏任务中实现了67%的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识蒸馏库（如TRL）在训练时将教师和学生模型对称处理，未能利用两者在显存和带宽需求上的巨大差异，导致资源利用效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种感知HPC的知识蒸馏方法，解耦了教师与学生模型的分区策略；结合了垂直与水平模型并行技术，并推导出用于确定最优分裂策略的分析表达式，以识别不同分裂方案的拐点。

Result: 在生产环境的HPC集群上，该方法在进行GKD训练时，相比TRL实现了高达67%的吞吐量提升（samples-per-second）。

Conclusion: 对于知识蒸馏任务，利用教师模型与学生模型在显存占用和通信需求上的非对称性，并通过感知高性能计算（HPC）拓扑的并行策略进行优化，能显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) enables training smaller student models under the guidance of larger teacher models, and the widely adopted TRL library implements it. Yet, TRL treats both models symmetrically, missing opportunities to exploit their pronounced asymmetry in memory footprint, and communication requirements. This paper presents an HPC-aware methodology for KD that decouples teacher and student partitioning efficiently. Our approach achieves up to 67% higher samples-per-second than TRL by avoiding unnecessary teacher-model data structures and selecting the best split strategy. We combine vertical and horizontal partitioning of models, deriving an analytical expression that identifies the existence of inflection points between splitting regimes. These results showed that exploiting teacher--student asymmetry through topology-aware parallelism notably accelerated GKD training on production HPC clusters at our company

</details>


### [120] [RAMSES: Secure high-performance computing for sensitive data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27919)
*Peter Heger,Lech Nieroda,Roland Pabel,Christoph Stollwerk,Stefan Borowski,Kamil Tokmakov,Michael Commer,Martin Peifer,Stefan Wesner,Viktor Achter*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RAMSES 是一个新型 HPC 系统，通过集成硬件级内存加密、先进存储加密及多层安全机制，成功解决了高性能计算与敏感数据安全性之间的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 HPC 系统倾向高性能而忽视安全，而生命科学等领域既需要高性能处理，又必须处理敏感数据，迫切需要一种兼顾两者的新型架构。

Method: 提出并构建了 RAMSES 系统，核心方法包括：整合 AMD 处理器硬件级内存加密、IBM Storage Scale 文件加密、Thales CipherTrust 密钥管理，以及实施操作系统加固、多层安全架构和强制多因素身份验证，实现数据全生命周期（静态、传输中、使用中）加密。

Result: 在生物医学领域的基准测试中，RAMSES 展现出极低的安全开销，成功证明了在保持系统灵活性和易用性的同时，能够满足严苛的安全合规要求。

Conclusion: RAMSES 证明了在 HPC 系统中实现高性能与高安全性的兼容是可行的，能够在满足严格数据保护标准（如 GDPR、ISO 27001）的前提下，支持大规模敏感数据的处理。

Abstract: Traditionally, the architecture of high-performance computing (HPC) systems is tailored for speed, while highly secure computer systems must sacrifice speed for security. However, a wide range of scientific domains, such as the life sciences, call for a combination of performance and security to allow processing sensitive data at scale. Here, we present RAMSES (Research Accelerator for Modeling and Simulation with Enhanced Security), an HPC system designed from the ground up to deliver high performance within a robust security framework. RAMSES integrates hardware-based memory encryption of AMD processors with state-of-the-art file encryption from IBM Storage Scale and the Thales CipherTrust manager, establishing an HPC platform that ensures continuous encryption throughout the data life cycle - at rest, in transit, and in use - in compliance with major data protection standards (European General Data Protection Regulation, ISO/IEC 27001 certification, and Federal Information Processing Standards). In addition, we implemented advanced operating system hardening, a multi-layered security architecture, and mandatory multi-factor authentication to adapt the HPC environment to increased security demands. Benchmark results from the biomedical sector demonstrate that the performance impact of the secure environment is limited and that integration of the conflicting requirements speed and security can be achieved while preserving a coherent, flexible, and user-friendly system.

</details>


### [121] [DataStates-LLM: Scalable Checkpointing for Transformer Models Using Composable State Providers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16956)
*Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Franck Cappello,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DataStates-LLM 是一种新型异步检查点架构，通过抽象模型状态解耦及非阻塞I/O优化，解决了超大规模LLM训练中检查点存储的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模LLM检查点方案将模型视为不透明的二进制块，忽略了异构性（内存位置、分片逻辑、数据类型），导致严重的阻塞式传输、数据无关序列化和I/O争用问题，限制了超大规模训练的效率。

Method: 提出“DataStates-LLM”架构，引入“状态提供者(State Providers)”机制将状态抽象与数据传输解耦；利用模型参数在训练期间的不可变性，实现“惰性”非阻塞异步快照；通过合并碎片化数据并重叠元数据序列化与张量I/O来优化性能。

Result: 在256个A100-40GB GPU上，针对高达70B参数的模型进行评估；结果表明检查点吞吐量提升至最高4倍，端到端训练时间缩短至最高2.2倍。

Conclusion: DataStates-LLM 通过状态提供者(State Providers)架构成功解决了大规模LLM训练中检查点存储的高开销问题，实现了更高效、非阻塞的异步快照，显著提升了检查点吞吐量并缩短了端到端训练时间。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Large Transformer-based models, specifically Large Language Models (LLMs), now scaling to trillions of parameters, has necessitated training across thousands of GPUs using complex hybrid parallelism strategies (e.g., data, tensor, and pipeline parallelism). Checkpointing this massive, distributed state is critical for a wide range of use cases, such as resilience, suspend-resume, investigating undesirable training trajectories, and explaining model evolution. However, existing checkpointing solutions typically treat model state as opaque binary blobs, ignoring the ``3D heterogeneity'' of the underlying data structures--varying by memory location (GPU vs. Host), number of ``logical'' objects sharded and split across multiple files, data types (tensors vs. Python objects), and their serialization requirements. This results in significant runtime overheads due to blocking device-to-host transfers, data-oblivious serialization, and storage I/O contention. In this paper, we introduce DataStates-LLM, a novel checkpointing architecture that leverages State Providers to decouple state abstraction from data movement. DataStates-LLM exploits the immutability of model parameters during the forward and backward passes to perform ``lazy'', non-blocking asynchronous snapshots. By introducing State Providers, we efficiently coalesce fragmented, heterogeneous shards and overlap the serialization of metadata with bulk tensor I/O. We evaluate DataStates-LLM on models up to 70B parameters on 256 A100-40GB GPUs. Our results demonstrate that DataStates-LLM achieves up to 4$\times$ higher checkpointing throughput and reduces end-to-end training time by up to 2.2$\times$ compared to state-of-the-art solutions, effectively mitigating the serialization and heterogeneity bottlenecks in extreme-scale LLM training.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Kimball's Data Warehouse Architecture: Evaluating the Challenges of Conformed Data against the Inmon Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27571)
*Júlio Rocha,Salviano Soares,António Valente,Filipe Cabral Pinto*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文对比了数据仓库领域的两大巨头Inmon与Kimball的架构差异，指出两者虽侧重点不同，但共同推动了企业数据存储技术的发展，且双方在专业上互相认可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对由于数据量快速增长而引发的企业级存储框架重构需求，以实现更高效的数据查询与管理。

Method: 通过对比分析Inmon的“集散中心式”（hub-and-spoke）架构与Kimball的基于一致维度（conformed dimensions）的“数据总线”（data bus）架构，从信息质量、系统质量、个人影响和组织影响四个维度进行了综合评估。

Result: 研究表明，Kimball架构更倾向于满足用户需求，而Inmon架构则更侧重于企业级统一数据视图；两者在企业数据管理领域均具有极高的权威性和参考价值。

Conclusion: Inmon与Kimball的架构虽方法迥异，但两者并非敌对关系，而是相辅相成，共同为构建高效企业数据存储框架提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: In recent decades, driven by rapid data growth, organisations have faced the need to restructure their storage frameworks to efficiently handle queries requested by employees through available enterprise applications. Investigating this need involves examining the classic approaches of William H. Inmon, widely known as the father of Data Warehousing, and Ralph Kimball. Although both shared the same core concerns, Kimball later suggested an alternative architecture focused primarily on user needs. According to Ariyachandra and Watson, Inmon's "hub-and-spoke" architecture and Kimball's data bus framework featuring conformed dimensions stand out among alternative approaches. A comparison across these architectures highlights four key aspects: Information Quality, System Quality, Individual Impacts, and Organisational Impacts. Although Kimball and Inmon proposed contrasting solutions, they did not view each other as rivals. For instance, in one of the editions of the book "The Data Warehouse Toolkit", published by Kimball in 1996, the back cover features a note by Inmon stating that it is "one of the definitive books of our industry. If you take time to read only one professional book, make it this book.

</details>


### [123] [DiStash: A Disaggregated Multi-Stash Transactional Key-Value Store](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27979)
*Yiming Gao,Hieu Nguyen,Jun Li,Shahram Ghandeharizadeh*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DiStash 是一个跨多种存储介质的事务性键值存储系统，它通过扩展 FoundationDB，使用户能够通过单一事务管理分散在不同类型存储池中的数据，从而简化应用逻辑并提供强一致性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代存储架构中存在多种存储介质，但缺乏一种机制能够以事务方式统一管理跨不同存储池的键值对，导致应用开发者在处理不同类型存储介质间的数据一致性和故障恢复时面临巨大的逻辑复杂性。

Method: 基于 FoundationDB 进行扩展，通过事务机制统一纳管多种存储介质（DRAM, SSD, HDD, NVM），支持用户定义数据是跨池复制（inclusive）还是分层存储（exclusive）。

Result: 通过微基准测试（microbenchmarks）和 eBay 的实际生产负载验证了 DiStash 的设计决策和性能权衡，证明了其在简化应用逻辑和保障数据可靠性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: DiStash 通过扩展 FoundationDB 有效实现了跨多种存储介质的事务性 KV 管理，简化了应用逻辑并保证了数据的一致性与持久性，适用于异构存储池场景。

Abstract: A stash is a storage medium such as Dynamic Random Access Memory (DRAM), Solid State Disk (SSD), Hard Disk Drive (HDD), or Non-Volatile Memory (NVM). This paper presents a disaggregated transactional key-value (KV) store, DiStash, that governs KVs cross pools of stash types. It enables an application to use a single transaction to read and write different copies of one or more key-value pair across the different pools of stashes. It simplifies the application logic by (a) preventing undesirable race conditions that may cause copies of data across different stash pools to reflect different values and/or (b) failures that may result in loss of key-value pairs. A configuration of DiStash may use a pool of stashes as either ephemeral or durable storage. The application dictates whether the content of its participating stashes are inclusive (replicated) or exclusive (tiered). We implement a DiStash by extending FoundationDB. We quantify the tradeoffs with its design decisions using microbenchmarks and eBay's production workload. We open source our implementation at https://github.com/ebay-USC/DiStash.

</details>


### [124] [Single and Multi Truth Data Fusion using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28062)
*Hira Beril Kucuk,Norman W Paton,Jiaoyan Chen,Zhenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了将大语言模型应用于表格数据融合（即真值发现）任务的有效性，证明了LLM在多种提示策略下优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统真值发现方法在处理复杂、冲突数据时的局限性，旨在挖掘大语言模型（LLM）处理表格数据融合任务（涵盖单真值和多真值场景）的能力与潜力。

Method: 通过实证研究，探讨了在表格数据融合任务中应用大语言模型（LLM）的策略，对比评估了领域相关、领域无关、零样本（Zero-shot）及单样本（One-shot）提示工程策略。

Result: 实验表明，基于LLM的方法在三个基准数据集上均超越了传统无监督学习算法（DART和LTM），验证了LLM在处理数据一致性问题上的优越性。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型的框架在处理表格数据融合任务时，其准确性显著优于传统无监督真值发现算法（如DART和LTM），且在单真值与多真值场景下均表现出较强的通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Data fusion, also known as truth discovery, is a data integration problem that aims to determine the correct value or set of values for each attribute of an object when presented with potentially conflicting values from multiple sources. Data fusion tasks belong to two main categories: single-truth scenarios, where each attribute has only one correct value, and multi-truth scenarios, where multiple values can be valid simultaneously. This paper investigates the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in data fusion tasks for tabular data. Various prompting strategies, encompassing both single-truth and multi-truth scenarios, are investigated empirically. Domain-dependent, domain-independent, zero-shot and one-shot prompts are evaluated on three different benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that LLM-based approaches outperform traditional unsupervised truth discovery methods, such as DART and LTM, across all datasets. The codebase of this study has been made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>

### [125] [Directed Graph Topology Inference via Graph Filter Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27455)
*Rasoul Shafipour,Andrei Buciulea,Santiago Segarra,Antonio G. Marques,Gonzalo Mateos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从受噪声激发的线性扩散动力学节点测量中推断有向图结构的算法，通过联合优化扩散滤波器与图拓扑，解决了非同时可对角化系统的网络辨识难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决从线性扩散动力学产生的节点测量值中推断有向网络结构的问题；克服了以往研究仅局限于无向图或白噪声输入信号的局限性，特别是在图移位算子与观测协方差矩阵无法同时对角化的复杂情境下。

Method: 1. 提出一种基于输出信号测量值和输入信号统计先验，通过解决二次矩阵方程识别扩散滤波器的方法，并将其转化为Stiefel流形约束下的平滑二次最小化问题；2. 基于滤波器估计以识别拓扑结构，将其构建为寻找与滤波器交换（commute）的稀疏且结构允许的移位算子的优化问题；3. 提出一种交替优化算法，实现滤波器与拓扑结构的联合识别。

Result: 证明了在输入协方差满足谱多样性假设下的可辨识性；数值实验表明该算法在合成有向图和真实数据集（城市移动性、投资组合优化）上能够有效恢复网络拓扑结构，且具有较优的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一套能够稳健推断有向图结构及扩散滤波器的框架，即使在输入信号存在相关性且移位算子不可对称化的复杂条件下，该方法依然展现出良好的拓扑还原性能，并可应用于城市移动性分析和投资组合优化等实际场景。

Abstract: We address the problem of inferring a directed network from nodal measurements generated by linear diffusion dynamics on the sought graph. Observations are modeled as the outputs of a graph convolutional filter, i.e., a polynomial (with unknown coefficients) of a local diffusion graph-shift operator encoding the latent graph topology, excited with an ensemble of independent graph signals with arbitrarily-correlated nodal components. Unlike prior efforts that considered undirected graphs and white signal excitations, here the graph-shift operator and the observations' covariance matrix are not simultaneously diagonalizable. In this challenging context, we first rely on measurements of the output signals along with prior statistical information on the inputs to identify the diffusion filter. Such system identification problem involves solving a system of quadratic matrix equations, which we show is identifiable under spectral-diversity assumptions on the input covariances. For algorithmic purposes we recast it as a smooth quadratic minimization subject to Stiefel manifold constraints. Subsequent identification of the network topology given the graph filter estimate boils down to finding a sparse and structurally admissible shift that commutes with the given filter, thus, forcing the latter to be a polynomial in the sought graph-shift operator. A joint graph filter and topology identification algorithm is also proposed, which alternates between the aforementioned steps in a mutually reinforcing fashion to offer improved sample complexity. Numerical tests corroborate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms in recovering synthetic digraphs and real-data case studies, and illustrate their potential utility on urban mobility analyses as well as portfolio optimization.

</details>


### [126] [The Decision Geometry of Covariance Estimation for the Global Minimum-Variance Portfolio under Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27462)
*Xavier Fonseca*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过解析协方差估计误差如何影向最小方差投资组合（GMVP）的性能，证明了仅凭传统的矩阵范数评估方法是不够的。作者提出了以决策为中心的评价框架，揭示了后悔（regret）的几何特性，并提供了在重尾分布条件下的性能收敛边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的协方差估计方法通常使用矩阵范数损失（matrix-norm loss）进行评估，但这与最终的决策目标（最小方差投资组合 GMVP）并不直接相关。作者旨在解决统计估计评价标准与实际最优决策目标之间的偏差。

Method: 1. 理论推导：推导出GMVP后悔与协方差估计误差的精确恒等式及非渐进界；
2. 几何分析：表征GMVP后悔在估计误差高维空间中的不变性（invariant projection）；
3. 极端值分析：将框架应用于重尾分布收益率（尾部指数kappa在2到4之间）；
4. 仿真验证：使用skew-t/t-copula仿真设计对理论结果进行预注册验证。

Result: 1. 确立了GMVP后悔不仅依赖于估计误差本身，还受限于投资组合权重的几何性质；
2. 发现GMVP后悔对误差矩阵的(p-1)维投影具有不变性；
3. 在重尾分布情形下，推导出了具体的后悔收敛速率；
4. 证明了“决策导向”的方法虽未改变速率的阶，但提供了更敏锐的常数估计和针对特定条件数下的性能约束。

Conclusion: 本文通过揭示协方差估计误差如何映射到GMVP的决策后悔（decision regret），证明了传统的矩阵范数评估方法在投资组合优化中存在局限性。研究表明，决策质量并不取决于估计误差的所有维度，而是受制于投资组合集中度和真实协方差条件数。这种决策导向的视角提供了更精准的性能分析框架，优于传统的统计估计量范数分析。

Abstract: The global minimum-variance portfolio (GMVP) is the canonical decision built from an estimated covariance matrix, yet covariance estimators are universally evaluated by matrix-norm loss, which is not the object the decision depends on. We characterise exactly how covariance-estimation error maps into GMVP suboptimality. We prove an exact regret identity and a non-asymptotic bound showing decision regret depends on the estimation error only through its action on the portfolio weights, scaled by portfolio concentration and the conditioning of the true covariance. From this we derive the decision geometry: GMVP regret is invariant to a (p-1)-dimensional projection of the p^2-dimensional error matrix, with invariance to the covariance-scale direction as an exact special case. We then apply the framework to heavy-tailed returns (tail index kappa in (2,4)), establishing the regret convergence rate implied by the centred operator-norm rate, and confirm the theory on a skew-t/t-copula simulation design with pre-registered analysis. The decision-focused advantage is a sharper constant and a concentration discount rather than a faster rate; we report an honest high-conditioning boundary of the rate prediction. The results complement recent decision-focused learning approaches by supplying the exact estimation geometry and consistency theory they lack.

</details>


### [127] [Adversarial Contamination Meets Hard Thresholding: An Iterative Algorithm with Signal Adaptivity and Minimax Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27685)
*Shixiang Liu,Hanming Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AC-IHT算法，用于解决高维回归中的鲁棒性问题，实现了近最优的参数估计和有效的支持集恢复，并证明了其统计推断的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维回归中普遍存在的数据污染问题（测量误差、异常值或对抗性干扰），旨在开发一种稳健的统计方法，以实现对回归系数的精确估计及支持集恢复。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段“对抗性污染鲁棒迭代硬阈值”（AC-IHT）非凸优化算法。该算法通过在不同阈值尺度下交替更新回归系数向量和污染向量来实现估计。

Result: 算法达到了极小极大近优估计速率（仅相差对数项），在特定信号条件下展现了信号自适应性（更快的收敛速率与更准的支持集恢复），并证明了其具备强预言机性质。数值实验验证了其在有限样本下的优越表现。

Conclusion: AC-IHT算法为高维回归中的数据污染问题提供了一种鲁棒且高效的解决方案，具备信号自适应性与强预言机性质，且可扩展至广义线性模型及重尾噪声场景，理论价值与实际性能均表现优异。

Abstract: Pervasive data contamination -- stemming from measurement errors, outliers, or adversarial corruption -- has motivated the development of robust statistical methods. In this context, we propose a two-stage Adversarial Contamination-resistant Iterative Hard Thresholding (AC-IHT) algorithm for high-dimensional regression with contamination. Our nonconvex algorithm achieves minimax near-optimal (up to logarithmic terms) estimation by iteratively updating the coefficient vector and the contamination vector with different thresholding scales. We further demonstrate that our AC-IHT estimator is signal-adaptive: under proper signal conditions, it adaptively attains a sharper estimation rate and more accurate support recovery. Moreover, it enjoys the strong oracle property, laying a theoretical foundation for asymptotic inference. Numerical experiments confirm its superior finite-sample performance. Finally, we discuss theoretical extensions of the proposed procedure to generalized linear models and to heavy-tailed noise settings.

</details>


### [128] [Local Fokker--Planck Geometry for Score Estimation: Heat-Ball Mean-Value Representations and Exact High-Dimensional Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27954)
*Jiayao Bai,Lang Deng,Yi Du,Yifei Jia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种处理扩散模型中分数函数估计的新方法，通过局部Fokker-Planck几何框架与受控的热球平均法，有效解决了传统方法在低密度区域估计误差较大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于分数的生成模型在处理非线性漂移时，依赖全局平均（如DSM方法），这会导致低密度区域的估计误差显著膨胀，且该区域往往是采样准确性最关键的地方。

Method: 1. 时间变换：将变系数Fokker-Planck方程转化为标准非齐次热方程；2. 局部平均：利用Evans的热球单调性方法导出分数函数、密度、对数密度及熵密度的局部均值表示；3. 采样策略：引入κ-测度（κ-measure）及其因子化采样器，以高效处理高维热球积分。

Result: 在数学上证明了局部框架的一致性，通过热球残差的极限关系证明了其对现有方法的泛化性；实验表明该方法在2D结构化数据及256维MNIST数据上表现良好，验证了理论推导的浓度定律。

Conclusion: 本文提出的局部Fokker-Planck几何框架，通过引入热球（heat-ball）平均法，为分数函数估计提供了一个更精确、可控的替代方案。该方法不仅统一了Denoising Score Matching (DSM) 与全局Fokker-Planck残差方法，且在处理低密度区域时具有更高的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Score-based generative models and Langevin samplers rely on estimating the score function $\nabla_x\log p_t(x)$ of a forward diffusion. Classically this is tractable when the drift is linear: the marginal density is Gaussian and the score is a global conditional expectation. For a general nonlinear, state-dependent drift the marginal density has no closed form, and existing methods--denoising score matching and global Fokker--Planck residual penalties--resort to global averaging that inflates estimation error in low-density regions precisely where accuracy is most critical. We address this by developing a local Fokker--Planck geometric framework that replaces global conditioning with local parabolic averaging. Our approach rests on three contributions. First, a time change to the cumulative-variance coordinate reduces the variable-coefficient Fokker--Planck equation to a standard inhomogeneous heat equation, on which we extend Evans' classical heat-ball monotonicity method to derive exact local mean-value representations for the score $\nabla_x\log p$ together with the density, log-density, and entropy density; local well-posedness is established under an explicit dimension-dependent drift budget. Second, for high-dimensional Monte Carlo evaluation of the resulting heat-ball integrals, we introduce the $κ$-measure and derive its exact factorized sampler with unit per-sample weight, $χ^2_2$ radial concentration. Third, the $r\to0$ limit of the heat-ball residual recovers the pointwise Fokker--Planck residual, showing that the local framework is a one-parameter generalization of global FP-residual methods, and that the DSM population minimizer is feasible for the heat-ball constraint at every scale. We validate the framework on 2D structured data on 256-dimensional MNIST, and on a dedicated sampler study confirming the concentration laws.

</details>


### [129] [Surprises in Proper Positive-Only Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28309)
*Shai Ben-David,Farnam Mansouri,Anay Mehrotra,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文解决了PU学习中“恰当学习”的长期开放问题，通过引入“一致外部可分性”条件，确立了其与VC维共同作为恰当学习的判据，并揭示了PU学习与标准PAC学习之间深刻的理论脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自NATARAJAN（1987）提出PU学习模型以来，长期未得到定论的“恰当正样本唯一学习”（proper positive-only learning）的特征刻画问题。

Method: 通过引入“一致外部可分性”（uniform exterior separability）这一新的组合条件，结合VC维理论，从理论上分析并证明了概念类在正样本唯一设置下实现恰当学习的充分必要条件。

Result: 证明了概念类可恰当学习的充要条件是具有有限VC维且满足一致外部可分性；发现了该模型下恰当学习与非恰当学习、确定性与随机性算法、ERM算法适用性之间的复杂差异。

Conclusion: 该研究完全刻画了正样本唯一（PU）学习中“恰当学习”（proper learning）的条件，揭示了其与标准PAC学习在本质上的差异，并提出了一套新的组合维度理论，丰富了学习理论的研究基础。

Abstract: Binary classification from positive-only samples is a variant of PAC learning in which the learner receives i.i.d. samples from the positive region of an unknown target concept, but is evaluated under the original distribution (which places mass on both positive and negative regions). This model dates back to Natarajan [1987, STOC], and the characterization of improper learning is well-known -- it even appears in textbooks. The characterization of proper positive-only learning, however, has long remained open. In this work, we revisit and settle this question: a concept class is properly learnable from positive-only samples if and only if it has finite VC dimension and satisfies a new combinatorial condition, which we call uniform exterior separability. Together with several separation results, this characterization reveals a surprisingly rich landscape that differs sharply from standard PAC learning: proper and improper learning are separated, randomized and deterministic proper learning are separated, there are classes for which no ERM is a learner, and finite VC dimension does not suffice even for non-uniform learning. Along the way, we introduce new combinatorial dimensions that we believe can be of broader interest in learning theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Cluster, Route, Escalate: Cascaded Framework for Cost-Aware LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27457)
*Yasmin Moslem,Magdalena Kacmajor,Vasudevan Nedumpozhimana,Ammar Abbas,Solmaz Panahi,David Lynch,Zhuangzhuang Nie,Alexandros Agapitos,Aleksandar Milenovic,Hongmeng Song,Yucheng Shi,Yue Pan,Patricia Buffini,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段级联的大模型部署框架，通过智能路由和动态质量评估，在大幅降低推理成本与延迟的同时，近乎完美地维持了最强模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产环境中部署大模型时，单一模型难以平衡“成本”与“精度”之间的矛盾，即昂贵模型处理简单查询时资源浪费，而轻量模型处理复杂查询时表现不足。

Method: 提出一种两阶段级联方案：第一阶段通过聚类将查询分发至性价比最高的模型；第二阶段引入质量评估（QE）机制，对第一阶段输出质量不佳的查询进行升级，交由更强大的模型处理。

Result: 在测试数据集上，该系统保留了最强模型97%-99%的准确率，同时降低了每输出Token的时间消耗（TPOT）。该方法仅需任务正确性标签，且能适应模型池的变化，无需手动重新配置。

Conclusion: 该两阶段级联架构通过智能路由与质量评估，在保持模型精度的前提下，显著降低了大语言模型的推理成本与延迟，且具备高通用性与自适应性。

Abstract: Efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs) in production forces a trade-off between accuracy and cost. Operators often default to a single model that is either expensive for easy queries or insufficient for hard ones. To address this challenge, we propose a two-stage cascaded solution. Stage 1 clusters incoming queries and assigns each cluster to its most cost-effective model. The cost budget for this routing process is set by an interpretable hyperparameter, tuned offline. Stage 2 adds a quality estimation (QE) cascade; when an output from Stage 1 is judged low-quality, the query is escalated to a stronger model. This ensures only hard or low-confidence cases reach the expensive models. On the test datasets, the cascaded system retains 97-99% of the strongest model's accuracy while reducing Time Per Output Token (TPOT). It requires only task-correctness labels and adapts to changes in the model pool without manual reconfiguration.

</details>


### [131] [Mixed-Precision For Energy Efficient Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27949)
*Gülçin Gedik,Robert Schöne,Roman Iakymchuk*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本研究通过在科学仿真中应用混合精度计算，成功在保持高精度的前提下，显著降低了计算耗时与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着仿真技术日益逼真，计算资源消耗（时间与功率）急剧增加，亟需优化方案以缓解计算压力。

Method: 利用计算机算术工具评估混合精度计算策略，并在 Reactor Simulator 和 LULESH 基准测试中进行实验验证。

Result: 在 Reactor Simulator 中，计算时间和能耗均降低了 30%；在 LULESH 中，计算时间优化了 30%，能耗降低了 25%，且均未牺牲计算精度。

Conclusion: 混合精度计算在科学仿真中具有显著的节能提效潜力，能够实现性能与能耗的优化，同时保持计算结果的准确性。

Abstract: As simulations grow more realistic, the pursuit of higher accuracy results in extended computation times and substantial power consumption. This study explores mixed-precision computing as a promising strategy to address these challenges, leveraging computer arithmetic tools to optimize performance. Using Reactor Simulator and LULESH benchmarks as case studies, we evaluated the potential of mixed-precision strategies to reduce both time-to-solution and energy-to-solution. For Reactor Simulator, we achieved a 30% reduction in both metrics without compromising accuracy. Similarly, for LULESH, results demonstrated up to a 30% improvement in time-to-solution and a 25% reduction in energy-to-solution.

</details>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [A Sensitivity-Aware Test Collection for Search Among Personal Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27559)
*Jack McKechnie,Graham McDonald,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文为敏感信息感知搜索（SAS）领域发布了一个基于Enron语料库的全新测试集，包含人工标注与LLM扩展的敏感性标签及相关性评估，并提供了标准化的实验基准和开源工程支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前敏感信息感知搜索（SAS）的研究受限于缺乏包含必要敏感性标签、查询语句及相关性评估的公开测试集，导致该领域研究难以开展和对比。

Method: 1. 基于Enron电子邮件语料库，通过众包方式构建了包含50个主题、150个查询及11,471个相关性评估的基准。2. 利用大语言模型（LLM）对语料库进行扩展，补充了相关性评估和敏感性标签。3. 提供了稀疏和稠密检索的基准测试，并通过ir_datasets和Huggingface发布资源以供社区使用。

Result: 构建了一个包含真实商业邮件的敏感性感知检索测试集，并提供了涵盖相关性检索、敏感性分类及敏感性感知搜索的基准模型性能指标。

Conclusion: 通过引入并公开该数据集以及基准测试，填补了敏感信息感知搜索研究中高质量标准测试集的空白，为评估和开发更安全的检索系统提供了基础架构。

Abstract: Traditional search tasks aim to satisfy user information needs by returning a subset of a collection of documents, ranked by the documents' relevance to a user query. However, some collections that contain useful information also contain sensitive personal information. Recently, there has been increasing interest in the development of Sensitivity-Aware Search (SAS) retrieval models to provide users with effective retrieval results without revealing such sensitive information. To develop such systems, test collections containing both sensitive and non-sensitive information, a set of queries, and query-document relevance assessments are required. The Enron email corpus contains real business-related emails, where some emails also contain sensitive personal information. However, the original Enron collection does not contain queries or query-relevance assessments. To this end, we crowdsource 150 query formulations for 50 different topics and 11,471 query-relevance assessments for a subset of the Enron documents that have been manually labelled for sensitivity. We follow best practices for using large language models (LLMs) in Information Retrieval evaluation to extend the collection further with additional LLM judged query-relevance assessments and sensitivity labels. We present baseline performances for relevance, sensitivity classification, and sensitivity-aware search on the collection. We make the collection available, including through the popular ir_datasets package, and provide pre-built sparse and dense indices on Huggingface to facilitate easy experimentation.

</details>


### [133] [Intuition-Guided Latent Reasoning for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27684)
*Chang Liu,Yimeng Bai,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Qifan Wang,Fuli Feng,Wenge Rong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: IntuRec是一种将认知科学中的“直觉”引入大模型推荐系统的框架，通过以Top-K候选集生成的直觉嵌入作为潜推理的起点，优化了推理路径并增强了推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的推荐系统潜推理方法，往往从无约束的潜空间起始点开始，导致初始隐藏表示与目标项目嵌入不对齐，产生次优推理轨迹。受人类多步推理受直觉引导的启发，旨在通过引入直觉来锚定和优化潜推理轨迹。

Method: 提出了一个两阶段框架IntuRec：1. 提取阶段，LLM基于用户历史生成Top-K候选集作为“直觉”来源；2. 注入阶段，利用自注意力和交叉注意力机制将候选集转化为与偏好对齐的“直觉嵌入”，并将其作为潜推理的起始点引导后续推理。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，IntuRec持续优于现有的先进基线模型（SOTA）。

Conclusion: IntuRec框架通过引入推荐直觉，有效地解决了现有潜变量推理方法中起始点偏离和推理路径不准确的问题，在多个真实数据集上表现优越，为LLM推荐系统提供了一种高效的潜变量推理方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive reasoning capabilities in complex problem-solving tasks, motivating their use for preference reasoning in recommender systems. Latent reasoning, which operates in continuous hidden spaces rather than discrete tokens, has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for LLM-based recommendation. However, existing methods often start from unconstrained reasoning points, where hidden representations are misaligned with target item embeddings, leading to suboptimal reasoning trajectories.
  Inspired by cognitive neuroscience, which suggests that human multi-step reasoning is guided by intuition as a latent prior, we propose \emph{IntuRec}, a two-stage framework that anchors latent reasoning with \emph{recommendation intuition}. In the extraction stage, the LLM-based recommender generates a top-$K$ candidate set based on users' histories as the source of intuition. In the injection stage, the candidate set is transformed into a preference-aligned intuition embedding using self- and cross-attention mechanisms, which initializes the reasoning start point and guides subsequent latent reasoning. By providing a semantically grounded starting point, IntuRec efficiently explores the preference space along more accurate reasoning trajectories. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that IntuRec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. We release our code at https://github.com/Ten-Mao/IntuRec.

</details>


### [134] [Bifocal Diffusion Language Models: Asymmetric Bidirectional Context for Parallel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27732)
*Yuhang Chen,Xianfeng Wu,Jinhao Duan,Mingfu Liang,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Parish Aggarwal,Frank Shyu,Luke Simon,Sandeep Pandey,Xi Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 为了解决 dLLM 生成质量与推理吞吐量的矛盾，本文提出了 R2LM 模型。通过结合支持 KV 缓存的因果注意力机制与轻量级的反向 Mamba，在实现高效并行解码的同时保持了高质量的上下文感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型（dLLM）存在架构困境：双向注意力虽能保证生成质量但不支持 KV 缓存（推理慢），因果注意力虽支持高效 KV 缓存但缺失右侧上下文（生成质量下降）。旨在寻找一种既能保留双向上下文优势又能实现高效缓存推理的方法。

Method: 提出 Bifocal dLLMs 范式，具体实现为 R2LM（Right-to-Left Mamba）。该模型结合了：a) 用于提供精准左侧上下文并支持 KV 缓存的因果注意力机制；b) 用于提供压缩右侧上下文的轻量级反向 Mamba 状态空间模型（SSM）侧边网络，且不破坏缓存能力。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B 模型上进行 60B tokens 的持续预训练实验显示：R2LM 在批量推理中的吞吐量比双向 dLLM 高出 2.4-12.9 倍，比自回归（AR）基线快 1.9-2.9 倍；在生成质量上，平均水平超越双向 dLLM，并在多数基准测试中优于因果基线。

Conclusion: R2LM 证明了通过不对称双向上下文设计（结合因果注意力与反向 Mamba），可以有效解决离散扩散语言模型在生成质量与推理吞吐量之间的架构矛盾。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs) recover masked tokens in parallel, offering significant speedups over autoregressive (AR) generation. However, such promising frameworks face a fundamental architectural design dilemma: \ding{182} Adopting bidirectional attention achieves strong generation quality by allowing each position to access the full context, but is inherently incompatible with KV caching, limiting inference throughput in batch-serving scenarios; \ding{183} Conversely, causal attention enables efficient cached inference but loses all right-side context, substantially degrading generation quality. This paper introduces Bifocal dLLMs, a new paradigm that resolves this dilemma through \emph{asymmetric bidirectional context}. Analogous to bifocal lenses, we instantiate the paradigm as \textbf{R2LM} (Right-to-Left Mamba), which combines two complementary mechanisms: $a$) standard causal attention providing precise left-context with full KV cache compatibility, while $b$) a lightweight reverse Mamba SSM sidecar supplying compressed right-side context without breaking cacheability. Comprehensive experiments on continued pretraining of Qwen3-1.7B with 60B tokens demonstrate that R2LM achieves $2.4\times$ to $12.9\times$ higher throughput than bidirectional dLLMs and $1.9\times$ to $2.9\times$ speedup over AR baselines in batch serving through parallel decoding with KV caching, while exceeding the causal baseline on most benchmarks and surpassing the bidirectional dLLM on average.

</details>


### [135] [End-to-End Dynamic Sparsity for Resource-Adaptive LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27743)
*Yuhang Chen,Jinhao Duan,Ruichen Zhang,Mingfu Liang,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Parish Aggarwal,Frank Shyu,Luke Simon,Sandeep Pandey,Tianlong Chen,Xi Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出L2A框架，通过动态调控计算足迹（层跳跃、头剪枝、推理Token精简），使LLM能够在复杂的云资源环境下根据实时预算和输入动态自适应，实现了性能与计算开销的最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型推理通常假设资源静态，但在真实的云基础设施中资源波动频繁，静态模型难以适应，容易导致崩溃或资源浪费，缺乏根据运行时预算动态调整计算的能力。

Method: 提出L2A架构，在LLM中集成轻量级、预算条件化及输入感知的门控网络（Gating Networks）；通过联合目标函数同时优化任务性能、逻辑一致性和资源开销；实现了层跳跃（层深度控制）、头剪枝（吞吐量控制）和推理Token减少（延迟控制）的三维自适应。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B和Qwen-3-4B模型上进行验证，具备高达34%的层稀疏度时性能损失仅为0.6%，优于传统需要为每个配置单一调整的模型，且保持了极佳的泛化能力。

Conclusion: L2A实现了在资源波动环境下的自适应推理，单一模型即可在不同资源约束下达到最优的性能与成本权衡（Pareto frontier），显著优于现有的静态模型或启发式方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) inference is typically deployed under a static resource assumption, where models execute a fixed computational graph regardless of the runtime environment. However, real-world cloud infrastructure is inherently dynamic, characterized by fluctuating availability (e.g., spot instance preemption) and tiered Quality-of-Service requirements. In such volatile settings, static models are inflexible: they either crash under resource constraints or waste compute on redundant operations. To bridge this gap, we propose Learning to Allocate (L2A), an end-to-end framework for resource-adaptive inference. Unlike prior methods that condition only on input difficulty, we formulate inference as a constrained allocation problem conditioned on both the input and the runtime resource budget itself. We introduce lightweight, budget-conditioned and input-aware gating networks integrated into the LLM. These gates are trained via a unified objective that jointly optimizes task performance, logical consistency, and resource costs along three axes matching how real-world dynamics manifest: layer skipping for memory and depth pressure, head pruning for throughput contention, and reasoning-token reduction for latency tightening. This lets the model learn a budget-aware policy beyond input difficulty alone: it adaptively configures its computational footprint with respect to real-time resource dynamics, maximizing reasoning depth when resources permit while enforcing strict frugality when budgets tighten. A single L2A model traces the entire compute-accuracy Pareto frontier on Llama-3-8B and Qwen-3-4B: at up to 34% realized layer sparsity, it stays within 0.6% of the dense baseline on GSM8K, with the same gap holding zero-shot on out-of-distribution tasks, while every static or heuristic baseline requires a separately tuned model and still drops by 5-10% at comparable inference time.

</details>


### [136] [From Bootstrapping to Sequence Modeling: A Unified Generative Framework for Personalized Landing-Page Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27865)
*Fan Li,Chang Meng,Jiaqi Fu,Shuchang Liu,Tianke Zhang,Xueliang Wang,Xiaoqiang Feng,Yongqi Liu,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GLAN的序列建模框架，旨在优化多页架构下的个性化登陆页导航（PLPM），通过深度结合全局跨日动态与局部会话反馈，显著提升了在线平台的用户留存与参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于保守Q学习（CQL）的登陆页导航方法存在两大局限：1. 马尔可夫假设无法捕捉用户行为中显著的非马尔可夫时间依赖；2. 基于TD学习的自举方法导致长周期环境下的累积误差和信誉分配问题。

Method: 提出GLAN（生成式登陆页自适应导航器）框架：1. 基于Decision Transformer模型处理PLPM任务；2. 设计L-RTG（长周期回报增长）模块，通过序列建模捕捉跨日用户消费动态，提供全局指导；3. 设计HRM（分层奖励调制）模块，将会话级反馈转化为细粒度信号，实现对单次页面分配的精确局部监督。

Result: 在快手平台的在线实验中，GLAN相比基准方法，DAU提升了0.158%，用户生命周期（LT）提升了0.108%。

Conclusion: GLAN通过结合全局序列建模（L-RTG）与局部细粒度监督（HRM），克服了传统基于RL的登陆页导航方法在处理非马尔可夫依赖和长周期延迟奖励方面的缺陷。在大规模线上实验中，GLAN显著提升了DAU和用户时长，验证了其在实际应用场景的优越性。

Abstract: Modern online platforms increasingly adopt multi-page architectures to accommodate diverse user needs. On these platforms, page navigation (the process of directing users to specific functional pages upon app entry) serves as a critical gateway that shapes user's first impression and significantly influences subsequent engagement. To optimize this process, Kuaishou formulated the task of Personalized Landing Page Modeling (PLPM) and proposed KLAN, a reinforcement learning framework built upon Conservative Q-Learning (CQL). However, CQL-based approaches suffer from two fundamental limitations: (1) the Markov assumption fails to capture the strong non-Markovian temporal dependencies inherent in real-world user behaviors, and (2) TD learning with bootstrapping incurs severe cumulative errors and credit assignment difficulties under delayed rewards, particularly in long-horizon settings where users enter the app multiple times daily. To address these limitations, we propose GLAN (Generative Landing-page Adaptive Navigator), a sequence modeling framework built on Decision Transformer to tackle PLPM from a unified global-local perspective. Specifically, GLAN incorporates two key modules. First, we design the L-RTG module that captures users' inter-day consumption dynamics to provide accurate global guidance for all page assignments within a day. Furthermore, we propose the HRM module that decomposes session-level feedback into fine-grained signals, enabling precise local supervision for each page assignment. Extensive online experiments conducted on the Kuaishou platform demonstrate the effectiveness of GLAN, achieving +0.158\% and +0.108\% improvements on Daily Active Users (DAU) and user Lifetime (LT) respectively.

</details>


### [137] [An LLM-Powered Semantic Alignment Framework for Journal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27930)
*Yanglin Yan,Zicheng Xie,Tianchen Gao,Rui Pan,Hansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型进行期刊推荐的无训练语义对齐框架，实现了高效、稳定且可解释的学术论文匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有期刊推荐方法过度依赖监督学习、手工特征工程或历史交互数据，存在泛化能力弱且解释性不足的问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的语义对齐框架，将期刊推荐转化为手稿内容与期刊范围描述之间的语义匹配问题，直接利用文章元数据（标题、摘要、关键词）和期刊信息进行推理，无需专门的监督学习训练。

Result: 在包含49个统计学及相关领域期刊的23,609篇文章数据集上，DeepSeek-V3模型的Top-3、Top-5、Top-10准确率分别达到40.23%、53.67%和70.05%；此外，引入参考文献可提升性能，且模型稳定性高（Top-5 Jaccard相似度达84%），并能输出解释性推理过程。

Conclusion: LLMs提供了一种无需训练、可扩展且具备高度可解释性的学术期刊推荐方案，证明了利用大模型进行语义对齐在学术决策支持中的有效性。

Abstract: Journal recommendation is an important task in scholarly information systems. Existing approaches typically rely on supervised learning models, manually engineered features, or historical interaction data, which may limit their generalizability and interpretability. We propose an LLM-powered semantic alignment framework that formulates journal recommendation as a semantic matching problem between manuscript content and journal scope descriptions. The framework enables large language models (LLMs) to infer journal suitability directly from article titles, abstracts, keywords, and candidate journal information without task-specific training. Experiments are conducted using DeepSeek-V3 on a dataset of 23,609 articles from 49 journals in statistics and related fields. The proposed framework achieves Top-3, Top-5, and Top-10 accuracies of 40.23\%, 53.67\%, and 70.05\%, respectively. Additional analyses show that incorporating reference information generally improves recommendation performance and that recommendations remain highly stable across repeated runs, with an average Top-5 Jaccard similarity of 84\%. The framework also generates interpretable reasoning outputs that provide insights into the recommendation process. These findings demonstrate the potential of LLMs as a training-free and scalable paradigm for journal recommendation and scholarly decision support.

</details>


### [138] [Listwise Explanation of Embedding-Based Rankings via Semantic Chunk Grouping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27980)
*Hyunkyu Kim,Yeeun Yoo,Youngjun Kwak*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对密集排名器与传统词级解释方法之间的特征粒度不匹配问题，本文提出了 ChunkGroupSHAP 方法，通过语义块聚类实现了更符合模型特征表示的列表式解释，并证明了最佳解释粒度取决于排名器类型与数据语料结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有列表式解释方法（通常基于孤立词）与密集嵌入排名器（基于语境化的句子/段落级表示）之间存在特征粒度不匹配的问题，减少词级特征的碎片化。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ChunkGroupSHAP 的列表式 Shapley 值评估方法。该方法通过将语义相关的文本块聚类为共享的跨文档特征，在掩盖特征组时同时扰动所有相关文档，从而将属性分配的粒度与密集表示对齐。

Result: 在 MS MARCO、FinanceBench、AILACaseDocs 和 FinQA 数据集上的实验表明：BM25 模型最适合词级解释，密集向量模型更适合语料库级分组解释，而异构 Web 检索更适合查询局部化分组。

Conclusion: 解释特征的粒度应根据检索器的表示粒度（如密集表示需要语料库级分组）和检索语料库的结构（如查询局部性）进行动态调整，而非一刀切地使用词级特征。

Abstract: Dense embedding rankers score documents through contextual sentence- and passage-level representations. Yet many listwise explanation methods still attribute rankings to isolated words. This feature-unit mismatch leaves word-level features too fragmented for dense semantic ranking. We introduce ChunkGroupSHAP, a listwise Shapley method that clusters semantically related chunks into shared cross-document features. Masking a group perturbs all documents with related evidence, attributing rankings at a granularity closer to dense representations while preserving the listwise setup. Our findings across MS MARCO, FinanceBench, AILACaseDocs, and FinQA with E5 rankers and BM25 show that the best explanation unit is setting-dependent: word features for lexical BM25, corpus-level groups for dense rankers, and query-local grouping for heterogeneous web retrieval. Feature units should thus follow both the ranker's representational granularity and the structure of the retrieved corpus.

</details>


### [139] [Fast and Feasible: Permutation-based Constrained Reranking for Revenue Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28059)
*Svetlana Shirokovskikh,Anastasiia Soboleva,Ekaterina Solodneva,Aleksandr Katrutsa,Roman Loginov,Egor Samosvat*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对电商搜索重排序任务的收益与用户体验权衡难题，提出了一种名为PermR的轻量级排序算法，通过启发式置换策略在满足约束条件下实现收益最大化，已成功应用于大规模生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在搜索与推荐系统中，为了提升电商平台的广告收益，需要对搜索结果进行重排序，但单纯追求收益往往会牺牲用户体验（如相关性降低或欺诈风险增加），因此需要一种能同时权衡收益与性能约束的实时排序方案。

Method: 1. 将重排序问题建模为整数线性规划（ILP），以最大化收益为目标，同时约束相关性等指标；2. 提出一种基于置换（Permutation-based）的轻量级近似算法PermR，通过在搜索结果中对相邻项进行交换，以迭代方式快速达到优化目标或满足约束条件。

Result: 1. 离线评估中，PermR能达到ILP算法63%的收益增量，且满足生产环境的延迟要求；2. 在14天的线上A/B测试中，处理了5600万次搜索请求，使平台收益提升了2%。

Conclusion: PermR是一种轻量级、高效且实用的重排序算法，能够在保证用户体验（相关性等准则）的前提下，实现平台广告收益的显著提升，成功解决了大规模商用场景下的实时重排序难题。

Abstract: Search and recommender systems have produced highly relevant search results. A natural next step in the development of such systems in e-commerce is to rerank these results to increase the platform's revenue from paid promotion products. However, maximizing revenue alone may degrade the user experience by reducing relevance or increasing fraud risk. To avoid this, we state the reranking problem as an integer linear program ($ILP$) that maximizes revenue subject to per-query constraints on other metrics, e.g., relevance. Since solving $ILP$ exactly for every query is slow for deployment to the online service, we propose a lightweight permutation-based reranking approximation algorithm PermR. At each step, the algorithm selects a pair of neighboring items and swaps them to either improve the objective or repair a violated constraint. We evaluate PermR across multiple categories of a large classified platform in offline and online settings. PermR achieves about 63\% of the ILP revenue improvement, within production latency limits, preserving all constraints. In a 14-day online A/B test over 56 million search queries, PermR increased revenue by $2$\%.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [MultModLM: A multi-modal benchmark for Large-Language Model based hardware schematic generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27666)
*Dhruv Kulkarni,Sai Manoj Pudukotai Dinkarrao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于RTL转原理图的任务基准MultModLM，并揭示了现有大模型在硬件生成任务中的局限性，指出LLM评估模型在该领域中表现不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前LLM与硬件生成的结合局限于文本任务，缺乏针对RTL到原理图（schematic）生成的多模态大模型基准及有效的评估机制。

Method: 1. 构建数据集：MultModLM（包含99个涵盖算术、控制和状态机的RTL模块及对应原理图）；2. 评估框架：结合基于准则评分、自评估、跨模型评估、盲测及人工验证的多阶段综合评价体系。

Result: 1. LLM虽能生成视觉上可解释的原理图，但功能准确性欠佳；2. LLM作为评估器（judge）与人类评分员几乎无一致性，证明其在结构精密领域不可靠。

Conclusion: 提出“LLM-as-a-judge”在结构化精密领域（如硬件设计）不可靠，呼吁开发更健壮、领域感知强的评估方法及支持形式化验证的结构评估工具。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language models (LLMs) find application in several fields. This extends to hardware definition and synthesis. However, most works at the intersection of LLMs and hardware generation focus on text-based tasks, creating a gap for multi-modal LLMs for RTL design. In this work, we introduce MultModLM, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on the task of generating hardware schematics from RTL (Register Transfer Level) descriptions. The dataset consists of 99 diverse RTL modules spanning arithmetic, control, and state-based designs. To address the challenges of non-unique schematic representations, we propose a multi-stage evaluation framework combining rubric-based scoring, self-evaluation, cross-model assessment, blind evaluation, and human validation to enable exhaustive evaluation.
  Through experiments on state-of-the-art LLMs, we observe that while models can generate visually interpretable schematics, their functional correctness remains constrained. Furthermore, we find that LLM-based evaluators exhibit near-zero agreement with human raters, revealing, as a key finding, that LLM-as-a-judge paradigms are unreliable in structurally precise domains. These findings suggest that reliable evaluation of multi-modal hardware outputs remains an open challenge, motivating the need for more robust and domain-aware evaluation methodologies, as well as tools for structural evaluation, so as to enable formal equivalence checkers.

</details>


### [141] [SEADA: An efficient methodology for optimizing mixed-precision DNNs on multi-precision spatial architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27884)
*Leandro Fiorin,Marco Ronzani,Cristina Silvano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SEADA 是一种针对多精度空间架构的协同设计框架，通过系统级分析模型、快速映射工具及基于熵的精度选择策略，实现了高效的混合精度神经网络部署与架构探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度计算能有效降低 DNN 的延迟、能耗和内存占用，但将其高效映射到多精度空间架构在精度选择、硬件异构平衡及系统级代价评估方面面临严峻挑战。

Method: SEADA 由四部分组成：(1) 可配置的系统级多精度空间加速器架构分析代价模型；(2) 用于将 DNN 工作负载映射到整数加速器的快速映射工具；(3) 用于评估浮点层混合精度执行收益的分析模型；(4) 基于位级熵（bit-level entropy）的逐层精度选择方法。

Result: 该方法能够平衡各层的精度敏感度、体系结构异构性和系统约束，实现近乎最优的映射，显著提升了设计的效率与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SEADA 是一套针对多精度空间架构的强大设计空间探索框架，能够高效地解决混合精度深度神经网络在硬件映射中的复杂挑战。

Abstract: Mixed-precision computation has been introduced in deep neural networks (DNNs) as an effective approach to reduce latency, energy consumption, and memory footprint. However, efficiently mapping mixed-precision networks onto multi-precision spatial architectures poses several challenges. These include determining the appropriate precision for each layer, balancing layer-wise accuracy sensitivity to quantization against architectural heterogeneity and system-level constraints, and accurately estimating the system-level cost of heterogeneous precision assignments. This work presents SEADA, an efficient methodology designed to address these challenges. SEADA comprises: (i) a configurable system-level analytical cost model of a multi-precision spatial accelerator architecture; (ii) a fast mapping tool that identifies near-optimal mappings of DNN workloads onto the target integer accelerator; (iii) analytical models for floating-point layers to estimate the overall benefits of mixed-precision execution; and (iv) a per-layer precision selection methodology based on bit-level entropy, enabling efficient assignment across multiple numerical precisions. SEADA's efficiency provides designers with a robust framework for the design-space exploration of multi-precision architectures.

</details>


### [142] [Co-Optimization of Analog Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Low-Power Function Approximation in Flexible Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27892)
*Paula Carolina Lozano Duarte,Georgios Zervakis,Mehdi Tahoori,Sani Nassif*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对柔性电子设备实现低功耗复杂函数计算的需求，本文提出了模拟柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络（AKANs），通过软硬件协同剪枝与误差建模，在保持高精度逼近的同时显著降低了系统功耗和硬件面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴与IoT设备处理复杂非线性函数（如神经网络激活、传感器校准等）时，传统数字实现成本过高；柔性电子平台（FE）虽有灵活性但对能耗和电路密度限制极严，迫切需要高效的模拟函数逼近方案。

Method: 该论文提出了 AKANs 方法，通过软硬件协同优化进行设计：在训练阶段引入电路级误差建模以应对硬件缺陷，并同时在软件和硬件层面应用剪枝技术以降低功耗和面积。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，该方法实现了高达 55% 的面积节省和 50% 的功耗降低，平均节省率接近 30%，同时通过正则化样条参数提升了逼近精度。

Conclusion: 模拟柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络 (AKANs) 是一种稳健且具有通用性的框架，能够显著提高柔性电子设备中模拟函数逼近的能效，是实现低功耗可穿戴处理的有效方案。

Abstract: Wearable devices and Internet of Things (IoT) sensors require on-sensor processing of biosignals and environmental data, including computationally demanding operations such as nonlinear activation functions for neural network inference, sensor calibration curves to map raw readings to physical units, and signal preprocessing functions like logarithmic compression and power operations for feature extraction. These functions exhibit significant complexity, often involving transcendental operations and multivariate dependencies that are costly to implement digitally. Analog function approximation provides a power-efficient alternative by performing these computations in the analog domain, thereby reducing the energy overhead associated with analog-to-digital conversion and subsequent digital processing. Flexible Electronics (FE) present a particularly attractive platform for wearable applications due to mechanical flexibility and low-cost fabrication, but impose strict constraints on circuit density and power consumption, making efficient analog implementations critical but challenging. This work introduces Analog Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (AKANs), developed via hardware-software co-optimization, to approximate these complex multivariate functions accurately under hardware imperfections. Our method incorporates circuit-level error modeling during training and applies pruning at both software and hardware levels to reduce area and power. Validation across multiple benchmarks demonstrates that our proposed pruning methodology not only reduces hardware cost but can also improve approximation accuracy by regularizing spline parameters. Results show up to 55% area and 50% power savings, with average reductions of nearly 30% across datasets, highlighting AKANs as a robust and generalizable framework for low-power analog function approximation in FE.

</details>


### [143] [Phase Matters: Characterizing Heterogeneous Vision-Language Inference on a Mobile SoC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27906)
*Aryama V Murthy,Yashas N Kotre,Prathmesh Sharma,Pragya Mishra,Sanjith Ganapathi,Priyesh Shukla*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文深度评测了骁龙8 Elite平台上的VLM推断性能，验证了NPU在不同推断阶段（预填充/解码）的异构加速差异，并提出了一种通过图重写优化使非原生模型高效适配移动端NPU的实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前手机SoC已提供NPU执行路径以支持设备端VLM推断，但开发者缺乏将VLM管道映射到异构硬件后端时的细粒度（阶段级）性能指导。

Method: 对高通SM8750（骁龙8 Elite）进行硬件在环（HIL）的深度特性分析，包含吞吐量、缓存状态、热稳定性、功耗及异构计算配置等维度；并提出了一套包含四步图重写技术（Graph Rewrite）的移植方案，使此前不支持的视觉编码器能在QNN上高效运行。

Result: 通过对FastVLM-0.5B及四类架构编码器的测试，发现NPU在预填充（prefill）阶段带来1.64倍加速，但在解码（decode）阶段仅提升1.18倍；视觉编码器在NPU上可获得20-45倍加速，并实现10.47°C的温降和2.52倍的能效提升；图重写技术使Phi-3.5-V等模型在QNN上实现最高22倍加速。

Conclusion: 针对移动端VLM部署，NPU推断具有明显的阶段依赖性；合理的计算负载映射和后端优化（如算子图重构）能显著提升能效并降低稳态温度，为移动端部署提供了切实可行的工程指导。

Abstract: Recent phone-class mobile SoCs expose practical NPU execution paths for on-device vision-language model (VLM) inference, but developers still lack phase-level guidance for mapping VLM pipelines across heterogeneous backends. We present a hardware-in-the-loop characterization of VLM inference on the Qualcomm SM8750 (Snapdragon 8 Elite), covering phase throughput, cache-state effects, 100-run thermal stability, energy, heterogeneous CPU/NPU pipeline configurations, and visual-token-budget sensitivity. Using FastVLM-0.5B as an end-to-end case study, together with encoder-only measurements across four architecture families, we show that phase matters: NPU execution is highly phase-dependent, delivering 1.64x speedup for prefill but only 1.18x for decode, while vision encoders achieve 20-45x speedups over CPU. These gains translate into 10.47 degrees C lower steady-state temperature and 2.52x lower energy, avoiding thermal throttling in always-on settings. Finally, we show that a four-step graph rewrite enables previously unsupported encoders, such as Phi-3.5-V, to reach the QNN path with up to 22x speedup, providing a practical porting recipe for mobile VLM deployment.

</details>


### [144] [Agentic Hardware Design as Repository-Level Code Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28279)
*Cunxi Yu,Chenhui Deng,Nathaniel Pinckney,Brucek Khailany*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HORIZON是一个专为硬件设计打造的自动化智能体框架，它将硬件开发转化为自动化的仓库级代码演进过程，并在多个主流基准测试中取得了全自动化的满分表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着硬件设计复杂度的提升，传统的EDA（电子设计自动化）辅助流程难以满足快速迭代需求。研究者旨在将已在通用软件系统中成功的“仓库级代码自演进”范式迁移到硬件设计（如Verilog/RTL开发）中，以实现硬件设计的闭环自动化。

Method: 提出HORIZON框架，将硬件设计视为“仓库级代码演进”过程。该框架利用Markdown作为蓝图，构建包含领域知识、评估器、验收机制及Git/运行时策略的项目包；通过完全自动化的智能体循环，在隔离的Git工作树中进行状态管理、追踪和回退，实现硬件制品的迭代设计。

Result: HORIZON在ChipBench、RTLLM、Verilog-Eval及9个CVDP类别中均实现了100%的基准测试完成率，实现了完全无需人类干预的自动化智能体循环。

Conclusion: HORIZON实现了硬件设计代理的完全自动化演进，并在多项基准测试中达到100%完成率，证明了仓库级代码演进方法在硬件设计领域的有效性。同时，研究者保持理性，强调当前基准测试仅为受控代理，距离解决真实的工业级芯片设计挑战仍有距离。

Abstract: We present HORIZON, a self-evolving agent framework that treats hardware design as repository-level code evolution. A Markdown harness is compiled into a project pack containing domain knowledge, an executable evaluator, an acceptance predicate, and a git/runtime policy; a hands-free agent loop then evolves an isolated git worktree, using repository operations for state management, tracing, and replay. This extends prior works of repository-scale self-evolution from EDA software systems, to hardware-design artifacts themselves. We evaluate our approach on ChipBench, RTLLM, Verilog-Eval, and nine CVDP categories, achieving 100\% benchmark completion across all suites with a fully hands-free agentic loop. However, we do not claim that agentic AI for hardware design is solved: these benchmarks are controlled proxies for a much broader engineering problem in chip design. Section~\ref{sec:discuss} examines the limitations of the current study and highlights open research challenges.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [AI-Model Network: Concept, Current State and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27382)
*Li Zhetao,Zeng Xiyu,Wang Jianhui,Xiao Yong,Liu Zhongren,Wu Junru,Lai Junjie,Huang Jijun,Long Saiqin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有 AI 大模型异构且碎片化的问题，本文提出了 AI-ModelNet，通过构建一种类似互联网的模型互联与协同推理网络，实现模型间的资源共享和协作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型虽发展迅速，但因训练成本高、部署复杂，正向轻量化、私有化和领域化转变，导致异构模型碎片化。如何实现这些分散模型的有效交互与协作成为亟待解决的瓶颈。

Method: 提出 AI-ModelNet 的概念与分层架构，构建了原型系统，并通过多种应用案例对该框架的可行性进行了验证。

Result: 提出了 AI-ModelNet 的系统视野与分层架构，并初步证实了该框架的实现可行性。此外探讨了该领域的关键未来研究方向。

Conclusion: AI-ModelNet 为人工智能模型提供了一种互联互通、能力共享与协同推理的新范式，有望解决大模型孤岛问题，推动 AI 向网络化、协作化方向演进。

Abstract: While the primary function of computers lies in computation and processing, the core value of the Internet is rooted in sharing and collaboration. Computers create the Internet, and the Internet empowers the value of computers. The rapid development of the Internet, cloud computing, and big data is pushing artificial intelligence into the era of large models (LMs). However, the practical application of LMs is currently hindered by high training costs and deployment complexities, driving a shift toward lightweight, private, and domain-specific models. With the rapid proliferation and wide distribution of heterogeneous models, enabling effective interaction and collaboration among them has emerged as a critical bottleneck that urgently needs to be addressed in LM development. Drawing inspiration from the development of the Internet, this paper proposes the concept, vision, and system architecture of world wide AI-model network (AI-ModelNet). It is a novel paradigm that achieves interconnection, capability sharing, and collaborative reasoning by establishing pathways between models. We first briefly review the current state of single-model and multi-model research. Subsequently, the systemic vision and hierarchical architecture of AI-ModelNet are articulated, followed by validation of the framework's feasibility through a prototype system and diverse application cases. Finally, key directions for future research are discussed preliminarily.

</details>


### [146] [When Does Personality Composition Matter for Multi-Agent LLM Teams?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27443)
*Aryan Keluskar,Amrita Bhattacharjee,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人格提示对LLM多智能体团队绩效的影响，发现人格对绩效的影响取决于任务性质：在结构化任务中影响有限，但在协作与博弈任务中，低宜人性人格会显著降低表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前关于人格提示如何影响大模型通信风格的研究较多，但缺乏关于这种行为转变如何具体影响客观任务结果的系统性研究，特别是在多智能体协作场景下。

Method: 通过提示词（Prompting）操纵多种前沿大语言模型（LLMs）的人格特质（重点考察宜人性），并在三种不同类型的任务中进行实证测试：结构化编程、开放式研究协作以及竞争性谈判。

Result: 在编程任务中，低宜人性会导致通信方式显著改变，但对任务完成度影响微乎其微；而在开放式协作和博弈任务中，相同的低宜人性人格配置会严重损害团队的协作效率和任务产出。

Conclusion: 人格提示对大模型多智能体团队表现的影响高度依赖于任务结构：在规则明确的任务（如编程）中影响较小，但在需要合作或博弈的任务（如研究协作和谈判）中，低宜人性人格会导致绩效显著下降。这表明人格定制在多智能体系统设计中应视任务需求而定，而非通用策略。

Abstract: Personality prompting shapes how large language models communicate, yet whether these behavioral shifts affect objective task outcomes remains under-explored. Prior work shows that agents prompted with low agreeableness produce adversarial language, while those prompted with high agreeableness become cooperative, but the relationship between communication style and task performance has not been systematically examined across multiple domains. In this work, we investigate whether personality composition matters for multi-agent team performance by manipulating personality traits across frontier LLMs on three task domains: structured coding, open-ended research collaboration, and competitive bargaining. We find that personality effects depend critically on task structure. In coding tasks, low agreeableness leads to large communication shifts that have little effect on milestone completion. In open-ended collaboration and bargaining, the same manipulation substantially degrades performance. We discuss implications for multi-agent system design and the limits of personality manipulation.

</details>


### [147] [Internalizing the Future: A Unified Agentic Training Paradigm for World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27483)
*Xuan Zhang,Zhijian Zhou,Lingfeng Qiao,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使LLM具备内部世界模型的方法，通过三阶段训练流程解决代理在长程任务中缺乏“假设推理”的问题，从而实现更精准的前瞻性决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代理在长程任务中过于依赖被动反应，缺乏人类特有的“假设推理”（what-if reasoning）和内部世界模型来评估未来结果，且直接微调预测轨迹往往会导致缺乏根基的表面模仿（格式-能力差距）。

Method: 提出了一个由三阶段组成的训练框架：(i)世界模型代理中间训练（WM-AMT）：注入潜在的预测能力；(ii)格式诱导监督微调（FE-SFT）：结构化输出预测过程；(iii)前瞻条件强化学习（FC-RL）：校准模拟轨迹并提升动作效用（将预测结果转化为类似于Q值的评估）。

Result: 在搜索和数学推理任务中，该方法显著优于现有的训练基准，证明了通过“能力优先”的方法可以实现更具扎实性和校准性的前瞻预测能力。

Conclusion: 通过“能力优先”的三阶段训练流水线（WM-AMT -> FE-SFT -> FC-RL），LLM能够具备真正的预测性思维，从而在长程任务中实现更稳健的决策，而非单纯的模仿。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capability in sequential decision-making, yet they remains fundamentally reactive in long-horizon tasks. Unlike humans who employ "what-if" reasoning to evaluate potential plans before commitment, standard agents lack an internal world model to simulate future outcomes. Therefore, we propose to internalize future-aware planning by training a single autoregressive model to verbalize both a prospective state rollout and a plan-conditioned success estimate-a textual analogue of the Q-value. Crucially, we identify a format-capability gap: simply fine-tuning agents on look-ahead traces during post-training leads to superficial mimicry of foresight without genuine predictive grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce a three-stage training paradigm: (i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT) to inject latent predictive capabilities into the policy; (ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT) to structure this injected capability; and (iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL) to refine the calibration and utility of the generated simulations. Evaluated on search and mathematical reasoning tasks, our approach consistently outperforms other training baselines. Our results demonstrate that effective internal world modeling in LLM agents requires a capability-first training pipeline to achieve grounded and calibrated foresight.

</details>


### [148] [Odyssey: Constructing Verifiable Local Truth-Preserving Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27593)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ODYSSEY 是一种基于范畴论（层与 Kan 扩张）的基座模型构建框架，旨在通过模块化的 Foundry 组件实现可验证、可组合且具备局部真值保持能力的 AI 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服当前基座模型在可验证性、局部上下文保持及复杂知识集成方面的不足，提供一种基于范畴论的严谨框架，实现可控、可信且模块化的模型构建。

Method: 提出了 ODYSSEY 框架，引入 Foundry（铸造厂）作为组合式的架构组件。利用“通用铸造学习”（UFL）技术，通过左 Kan 扩张（聚合局部信息）和右 Kan 扩张（强制约束与胶合规则）实现模型的组合。开发了 Foundry SQL (FSQL) 及 TICKET 认证机制用于数据查询与模型验证。

Result: 成功实现了多种具体的 Foundry（如科学挑战、市场含义、操作决策等）。测试表明，该框架能够有效支持领域构建、制品重播、层诊断、局部 LLM 审查及基于 TICKET 的因果认领提取。

Conclusion: ODYSSEY 框架证明了范畴论（特别是层论与 Kan 扩张）可以有效构建具可验证性、局部真值保持的基座模型，为异构知识源的集成与论证提供了一套严谨的数学与工程基础设施。

Abstract: We introduce a categorical framework called ODYSSEY for constructing verifiable, local truth-preserving foundation models as compositions of foundries: building-block architectural components that specify a cover of local contexts, local representation families, restriction maps, gluing rules, obstruction policies, update obligations, and human-facing views. A foundry is an organized sheaf of knowledge that carries within it an argumentation component. Concrete foundries are built from generic foundries such as evidence/argument, operational decision, institutional/financial, market meaning, scientific challenge, research-program, assistant-build, and evaluation-harness foundries. Universal Foundry Learning (UFL) formalizes foundry construction as a composition of left and right Kan extensions, with left Kan extension rolling local artifacts into candidate foundries and right Kan extension enforcing the restriction, gluing, obstruction, and argumentation conditions required for promotion. Foundry SQL (FSQL) is a small typed query surface for slicing maintained foundry artifacts that uses TICKET (Topos Integration using Causal Kan Extension Transformers) certification for admitting external or pre-built models into durable ODYSSEY state. ODYSSEY is fully implemented and tested across a wide spectrum of concrete foundries, showing that the same categorical machinery supports domain construction, artifact replay, sheaf diagnostics, grounded Toulmin/local-LLM scrutiny, residual-obstruction ledgers, and optimized TICKET-compatible causal-claim extraction across heterogeneous sources. This paper is to be presented as a 2.5 hour tutorial at ICML 2026. The tutorial home page is at https://bit.ly/4ajS0nA.

</details>


### [149] [DysLexLens: A Low-Resource LLM Framework for Analysing Dyslexic Learners Insights from Online Forums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27619)
*Dana Rezazadegan,Atie Kia,Phongpadid Nandavong,Dominique Carlon,Jeremy Nguyen,Abhik Banerjee,James Marshall,Anthony McCosker,Yong-Bin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 DysLexLens，这是一个端到端的低资源 LLM 框架，专门用于分析阅读障碍者在社交论坛中分享的 AI 使用体验。该架构通过字典过滤、KG 推理及多维评估，实现了从噪声数据中挖掘洞察并确保结果可追溯，为理解特殊群体的技术体验提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管阅读障碍群体日益依赖 AI 工具，但目前缺乏针对该群体在现实生活中使用 AI 体验的深入研究，且现有数据往往源于噪声较大的低资源论坛环境，难以直接分析。
