Method: 提出了 DysLexLens 框架，包含四个模块：1. 字典驱动的过滤方法，构建专注的 Reddit 语料库；2. LLM 辅助的语义分析结合知识图谱 (KG) 的查询推理；3. 基于 RAGAS 和查询稳健性 (Query Robustness) 的量化评估；4. 针对幻觉和证据对齐的结构化定性验证指南。

Result: 通过对 Reddit 论坛数据及 30 个问题的验证测试，证明了 DysLexLens 在提取有意义模式方面的有效性，并展示了其在其他低资源社交媒体语境下的通用性。相关资源已开源以支持复现。

Conclusion: DysLexLens 是一个针对低资源语境下分析阅读障碍者人工智能（AI）使用体验的有效框架。其结合语料库构建、知识图谱推理与多维度评估的方法，不仅提升了数据处理的精准度，还增强了结果的可追溯性，证明了该架构在处理论坛数据时的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Dyslexic learners increasingly use artificial intelligence (AI) tools to support reading, writing, organisation, and study-related tasks. However, their lived experiences with these tools remain largely underexamined. This paper proposes DysLexLens, a low-resource LLM framework, designed to analyse dyslexic learners experience with AI through online forum discussions. DysLexLens is designed as an end-to-end, evidence-traceable architecture which transforms noisy social media posts into a dictionary-driven corpora, provides knowledge-graph (KG)-based question reasoning, generates verifiable query responses, and enables response evaluation through quantitative and human-grounded assessment. DysLexLens has four key features. First, it employs a dictionary-driven filtering method to construct a more focused Reddit corpus on dyslexia and AI, filtering out noisy and weakly related posts to improve the relevance of data collected from low-resource forum contexts. Second, it integrates LLM-assisted semantic analysis with KG-based query reasoning to uncover meaningful patterns. Third, it has quantitative evaluation metrics (RAGAS and Query Robustness) to measure LLM-generated response performance. Fourth, it provides structured qualitative validation guidelines for assessing response quality, with a specific focus on hallucination and evidence alignment. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DysLexLens using dyslexia-related Reddit forum data and 30 questions. The results show its potential generalisability to other low-resource forum data contexts. DysLexLens, sample data, questions and evaluation results are available at Github to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [150] [AI-Driven Synthesis for High-Tech System Design: Automating Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28126)
*Luuk Oerlemans,Steven Westerhof,Theo Hofman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算设计综合（CDS）框架，利用生成式AI实现高科技系统的自动化设计，通过两个案例验证了其在减少人工干预、提升设计智能化水平方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现代高科技系统设计中日益复杂的组合优化难题。

Method: 提出计算设计综合（CDS）框架，结合深度学习与生成式人工智能（Generative AI）技术，并通过电子驱动系统设计和空间尺寸标注两个案例进行验证。

Result: 通过案例证明了CDS在自动化生成新颖系统设计方面的有效性，实现了从辅助设计向自主设计模式的跨越。

Conclusion: 计算设计综合（CDS）标志着从基于模拟的优化向自主设计的重大范式转变，能够以最小的人为干预实现复杂系统的高效设计。

Abstract: This article addresses the combinatorial complexity inherent in modern high-tech system design by presenting automation-in-design (AiD) as a transformative paradigm. We propose computational design synthesis (CDS), a framework utilising deep learning and generative AI to automate the creation of novel systems. Two case studies (e-drive system design and spatial dimensioning problem) serve as proof-points for this approach. The AI-driven methods used in the case studies represent a fundamental shift in engineering, advancing from simulation-based optimisation towards autonomous design with minimal human supervision.

</details>


### [151] [MER-R1: Multimodal Emotion Reasoning via Slow-Fast Thinking Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27652)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Chengwei Qin,Xinyi Wang,Jiayu Zhang,Jiangnan Chen,Hewei Guo,Dongchuan Ran,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现多模态情感识别中“慢推理”不一定优于“快直觉”。作者提出MER-R1框架，通过强化学习将偏向召回的快思考与偏向精度的慢思考进行协同优化，成功实现二者优势互补并取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示了推理型多模态大模型（MLLMs）在情感识别中并非推理深度越深效果越好，并发现“快思考”（直觉）偏向召回，“慢思考”（推理）偏向精度，两者存在天然互补性但难以直接转化。

Method: 提出了MER-R1强化学习框架，核心机制包括：1. 双目标解耦（Dual-objective disentanglement），分别优化召回与精度；2. 快慢思考置信度校准（Slow-fast confidence calibration），利用快思考直觉引导慢思考推论；通过理论证明减少优化过程中的方差干扰。

Result: 在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion基准测试中达到了SOTA（性能最优）水平，不仅提升了情感识别准确率，还真正实现了基于推理的性能优化。

Conclusion: 提出MER-R1框架，证明通过强化学习整合快思考（直觉、召回能力）与慢思考（推理、精度）的互补性，能有效提升多模态情感识别（MER）的准确性，并实现性能最优化。

Abstract: We find that explicit reasoning does not necessarily translate into better multimodal emotion recognition (MER) accuracy, even though it makes predictions more interpretable. Specifically, for reasoning-based MLLMs, fast thinking by triggering direct answers often outperforms slow thinking after deliberative reasoning. Our empirical analyses show that fast thinking improves recall with broader and more confident predictions, whereas slow thinking favors precision through conservative filtering of incorrect categories. Building on these insights, we propose MER-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that turns slow-fast complementarity into explicit optimization. Dual-objective disentanglement separates recall and precision into two optimization signals, allowing them to be jointly optimized rather than traded off against each other. Slow-fast confidence calibration further aligns the final slow-thinking answer with fast-thinking intuition, strengthening correct emotions while suppressing incorrect ones. In this way, MER-R1 unifies the recall-oriented intuition of fast thinking with the precision-oriented selectivity of slow thinking. We further provide theoretical justification for this synergy, showing that it mitigates variance-induced interference during optimization. Extensive experiments on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion show that MER-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance and makes reasoning genuinely benefit emotion recognition.

</details>


### [152] [ToE: A Hierarchical and Explainable Claim Verification Framework with Dynamic Multi-source Evidence Retrieval and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27736)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Kun Zheng,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Chunlei Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Tree of Evidence (ToE) 框架，通过引入基于强化学习的证据推理与树状聚合机制，有效对抗 AI 生成的伪造信息及检索系统毒化攻击，显著提升了自动事实核查的准确度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对生成式引擎优化（GEO）带来的AI生成式虚假信息传播，以及现有检索系统易受对抗性投毒攻击污染的问题，旨在提升LLM在大规模事实核查任务中的准确性与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了“证据树”（Tree of Evidence, ToE）框架，由三个核心模块构成：强化学习驱动的多源检索智能体、证据评估智能体，以及证据树聚合算法。该方法通过动态分解、获取并迭代验证声明，构建可解释的证据链。此外，文中还通过理论分析证明了检索策略的收敛误差边界。

Result: 在多个数据集及不同大语言模型基座上进行了验证，ToE 较主流基线提升了 4 到 24 个百分点，且在面对对抗性中毒输入时表现出显著增强的效果。

Conclusion: ToE 通过分层证据推理和强化学习，在处理常规误导信息及对抗性中毒攻击时，在准确性和鲁棒性方面均优于现有基线模型。

Abstract: The rapid spread of fake news poses increasing threats to information ecosystems, especially as AI-generated misinformation under Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) poisoning allows adversarially crafted content to be systematically surfaced by retrieval systems, contaminating LLM reasoning. In this paper, we propose Tree of Evidence (ToE), a hierarchical evidence reasoning framework for automated fact-checking that models each claim as a dynamically expanding argument tree. ToE integrates a reinforcement learning-driven multi-source retrieval agent, an evidence evaluation agent, and an argument tree aggregation algorithm to iteratively decompose, retrieve, and verify claims through an explainable evidence chain. We further provide a theoretical analysis of the retrieval process, deriving a formal error bound that guarantees the learned policy converges to a neighborhood of the information-theoretically optimal policy. Experiments across multiple datasets and backbone LLMs demonstrate that ToE achieves improvements ranging from 4 to 24 percentage points over competitive baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on adversarially poisoned inputs.

</details>


### [153] [Towards Reliable and Robust LLM Planning: Symbolic Feedback-Driven Iterative Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27757)
*Jiajing Zhang,Jiamei Jiang,Chenyang Zhang,Feifei Mo,Linjing Li,Daniel Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种符号反馈驱动的迭代自精炼框架，通过符号验证和自然语言指导，有效解决了大型语言模型在长程规划中存在的鲁棒性和可靠性不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在长程决策任务中常因任务复杂度高而产生不可行或错误的方案，引发了关于其鲁棒性和可靠性的安全担忧，缺乏有效的长程规划能力。

Method: 提出了一种符号反馈驱动的迭代自精炼框架，包含三个核心组件：1. 自然语言提示机制（将逻辑符号映射为自然语言以增强约束理解）；2. 符号验证器（识别错误并生成可解释的纠正指令）；3. 计划识别器（推断目标可达性以提供有效引导）。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提框架能够持续提升LLM在长程规划任务中的方案可行性（feasibility）和正确性（correctness）。

Conclusion: 该框架通过引入符号反馈驱动的自精炼机制，显著提升了大型语言模型（LLM）在长程规划任务中的鲁棒性与可靠性，为构建更可信的AI系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have attracted widespread attention from academia and industry, yet their deployment raises critical security concerns regarding robustness and reliability. Planning, a core component of intelligent behavior, remains challenging for LLMs, which often produce infeasible or incorrect solutions in long-horizon decision-making tasks due to inherent complexity. In this paper, we propose a symbolic feedback-driven iterative self-refinement framework to enhance the robustness and reliability of LLMs in long-horizon planning. Specifically, a natural language prompting mechanism is introduced to map logical symbols into natural language descriptions, enabling LLMs to better capture task constraints and semantics. We further design a symbolic verifier that identifies errors and converts them into corrective instructions interpretable by the LLM, thereby guiding self-refinement. In addition, we leverage a plan recognizer to infer goal reachability, facilitating more effective guidance toward desired goals. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves both feasibility and correctness in long-horizon planning tasks. This highlights its effectiveness in enhancing the reliability of LLM-based planning and potential to enable more trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [154] [Understanding Rollout Error in Graph World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27780)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析图世界模型（GWMs）在长程规划中的误差放大机制，提出了Error-Aware GWM框架，通过谱正则化与关键节点加权等策略，有效解决了动态图环境下的长程预测发散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型多针对向量或图像数据，而在以代理、工具、技能等构成的图结构环境中，预测误差会随图结构蔓延甚至放大，尤其在边动态演化的场景下，长程规划极易失效，缺乏严谨的理论分析和应对机制。

Method: 1. 建立统一的固定边和动态边GWM框架（引入动作节点）；2. 推导图值展开界（Graph-valued rollout bounds），区分拓扑诱导与模型诱导的误差放大；3. 提出联合节点-边算子以应对动态边展开；4. 引入Error-Aware GWM：结合谱正则化、展开一致性及关键节点加权。

Result: 实验表明，长程规划中误差和遗憾随视界增长；动态边训练对于结构演化场景至关重要；Error-Aware GWM显著优于baseline，能防止长程预测发散。

Conclusion: 图世界模型（GWMs）在动态图规划中表现优异，但在处理静态或稀疏预测任务时，专用图模型仍具优势；提出的Error-Aware GWM能有效抑制长程预测的发散性并保持高预测精度。

Abstract: World models are often used for planning by rolling learned dynamics forward. Many planning environments, however, are not vectors or images; they are graphs of agents, tools, skills, routes, and dependencies. In these settings, a local prediction error may stay local or spread through the graph, and the failure mode changes again when edges are predicted rather than fixed. This paper studies long-horizon rollout error in Graph World Models (GWMs). We formulate a unified fixed-edge and dynamic-edge GWM framework with action nodes for node-, edge-, and graph-level decisions. We develop graph-valued rollout bounds that separate topology-induced amplification from model-induced amplification, and we introduce a joint node-edge operator for dynamic-edge rollouts. Guided by the analysis, we propose Error-Aware GWM, which combines spectral regularization, rollout consistency, and critical-node weighting. Across synthetic topologies and heterogeneous agent-graph testbeds, rollout error and planning regret grow with horizon, dynamic-edge training is needed when structure evolves, and Error-Aware GWM prevents long-horizon divergence while preserving prediction accuracy. Real-world graph benchmarks clarify the scope of GWMs: they are most useful for dynamic graph rollout and agent planning, while specialized graph models remain strong on static or sparse prediction tasks.

</details>


### [155] [Grounded Iterative Language Planning: How Parameterized World Models Reduce Hallucination Propagation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27806)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GILP 框架创新性地融合了参数化世界模型的可靠性与 LLM 代理的推理灵活性，通过一致性校验机制显著降低了智能体的状态幻觉并提升了复杂的图结构规划任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语言智能体世界模型面临二元困境：基于代理的模型（Agent-based）推理灵活但容易产生难以量化的状态幻觉；参数化模型（Parameterized）易于度量但规划能力较弱。研究旨在弥补这两类模型的缺陷。

Method: 提出 Grounded Iterative Language Planning (GILP) 框架：使用一个小型的参数化预测器（backbone）提供状态变化、风险和价值预测，辅助 LLM 进行决策；引入一致性门控机制（consistency gate），当 LLM 推理与参数化预测不一致时触发修正。

Result: 在真实 GPT-4o-mini 调用测试中，状态幻觉率从 0.176 降至 0.035；在模拟器消融任务中，规划成功率从 0.668 提升至 0.838，且仅增加约 22% 的 LLM 调用成本。

Conclusion: 通过结合基于代理的语言推理和参数化预测模型，GILP 有效缓解了语言智能体在世界建模中的幻觉问题，并在提升规划成功率的同时保持了较高的计算效率。

Abstract: World models for language agents come in two useful forms. An agent-based world model calls an LLM API and reasons flexibly in language, but its errors appear as hallucinated state changes that are hard to score with ordinary regression losses. A parameterized world model is a trained transition predictor; its errors are easier to measure with quantities such as NodeMSE, delta accuracy, and validity accuracy, but it is usually weaker as a standalone planner. We compare these two families on four graph-structured planning benchmarks and introduce operational hallucination metrics for the agent-based case. The comparison motivates \textbf{Grounded Iterative Language Planning} (GILP), which trains only a small parameterized backbone and combines it with API-based agent reasoning. The backbone supplies valid actions, predicted state deltas, risk, and value; the LLM drafts an action and imagined delta; and a consistency gate asks for revision when the two disagree. On real GPT-4o-mini calls, GILP reduces hallucinated-state rate from 0.176 to 0.035. In calibrated simulator ablations, it raises success from 0.668 to 0.838 while adding only ~22% extra LLM calls.

</details>


### [156] [ATOD: Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation for Multi-turn Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27814)
*Qitai Tan,Zefang Zong,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长周期交互任务，本文提出了 ATOD 算法，通过退火调度平衡模仿学习与强化学习，并利用 T-DUR 机制优化监督信号，实现了小模型在交互任务中超越教师模型性能的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决小语言模型在长周期交互任务中面临的两难困境：在线蒸馏（OPD）学习速度快但存在性能上限，而强化学习（RL）虽有更高上限但初期学习效率低且难以收敛。

Method: 1. 采用了一种退火式的 OPD-RL 调度策略，即在训练初期以 OPD 为主以快速对齐教师行为，随后逐渐增强 RL 以实现奖励驱动的探索；2. 提出了 Turn-level Disagreement-Uncertainty Reweighting (T-DUR) 机制，通过软加权高实用性轮次，增强了长序列任务中的监督信号。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 Search-QA 等基准测试中，ATOD 相比 OPD 平均成功率提升了 3.03 个百分点，相比 GRPO 提升了 23.62 个百分点，并超越了教师模型 2.16 个百分点。

Conclusion: ATOD 成功结合了在线蒸馏与强化学习的优势，能够打破教师模型的性能限制，为训练高效的小型交互式智能体提供了一种优越的范式。

Abstract: Training small language-model agents for long-horizon interactive tasks requires both fast imitation and reward-driven improvement. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher guidance and typically improves rapidly in the early stage, but its gains saturate once the student approaches the teacher, limiting the final performance ceiling. Reinforcement learning (RL) directly optimizes environment rewards and encourages exploratory improvement toward a higher reward-defined ceiling, but sparse and delayed feedback makes early-stage learning much less efficient than OPD. In this paper, we propose ATOD (Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation), a hybrid online distillation algorithm that explicitly exploits this complementarity. (1) ATOD uses an annealed OPD-RL schedule: OPD dominates early training to approach teacher-level behavior, while RL is gradually strengthened to drive reward-based exploration. (2) ATOD introduces Turn-level Disagreement-Uncertainty Reweighting (T-DUR), which softly amplifies high-utility turns and improves dense supervision in long trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA show that ATOD consistently outperforms competing post-training baselines: across the three student sizes, ATOD improves average success rate by 3.03 points over OPD and 23.62 points over GRPO, while surpassing the corresponding teacher models by 2.16 points.

</details>


### [157] [NormAct: A Benchmark for Hidden Social Norm Compliance in Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27826)
*Shiyun Zhao,Xinwei Song,Tianyu Guo,Xiaomeng Gao,Mingyuan Liu,Xu Han,Yuanyuan Zhang,Zhenliang Zhang,Xue Feng,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对具身智能体在现实场景中忽视隐含社会规范的问题，本文提出了NormAct基准测试和NormPerceptor框架，通过增强模型对场景规范的感知与提示能力，大幅提升了智能体社会化决策的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身智能模型通常只关注明确的目标任务，忽略了现实世界中隐性社会规范的约束。目前缺乏有效的基准来评估模型是否能理解并应用这些未提及的规范，从而导致模型在复杂的现实交互中表现出行为不当或违反社会准则。

Method: 1. 构建了NormAct基准测试，用于评估具身智能体在隐性社会规范下的任务能力。2. 使用GPT-4o/Claude 3 Opus/Gemini 1.5 Pro等模型进行评估。3. 提出NormPerceptor架构，通过在规划前推断并注入场景相关的规范提示，改进模型的决策过程。

Result: 1. 实验显示SOTA模型在明确目标达成率上为67.3%，但隐性规范遵循率仅为26.4%。2. 在应用NormPerceptor后，综合任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。3. 实验证明模型具备相关社会知识，但缺乏将规范整合进决策循环的机制。

Conclusion: 现有的多模态大模型在执行具体任务时，往往能完成明确指令，但在处理隐含的社会规范时表现欠佳。研究证明问题的核心在于模型缺乏在特定场景下激活和落地（Grounding）规范的能力，通过引入上下文条件提示生成器（NormPerceptor），可以显著提升智能体在复杂环境下的综合任务成功率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed as embodied planners in egocentric environments, where task success requires not only achieving instructed goals but also acting in socially appropriate ways. While explicit goals may render certain actions optimal, implicit social norms often impose hidden constraints. Existing evaluations typically focus on explicit goal achievement or direct norm knowledge, seldom assessing whether planners can infer and apply these hidden constraints within action sequences. We introduce NormAct, a benchmark for embodied social-norm interactions that evaluates plans on Goal Achievement, Norm Compliance, and overall Task Success. NormAct uniquely embeds hidden norms within ordinary tasks, testing whether models can realize them without explicit instruction. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemini 3 Pro) reveal a significant gap: models achieve explicit goals in 67.3\% of cases, but comply with hidden norms in only 26.4\%. Cue-condition experiments indicate that this gap stems not from a lack of general social knowledge, but from challenges in activating and grounding relevant norms in context. To address this, we propose NormPerceptor, a context-conditioned cue generator that infers scene-relevant norms prior to planning, increasing Task Success from 24.2\% to 46.7\%. Our results underscore the importance of enabling embodied agents to proactively detect hidden norms, ground them in visual evidence, and integrate them as action-planning constraints. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Caleb196x/NormAct.

</details>


### [158] [Verifiable Geometry Problem Solving: Solver-Driven Autoformalization and Theorem Proposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27926)
*Can Li,Ting Zhang,Junbo Zhao,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SD-GPS 是一个求解器驱动的几何问题求解框架，通过将符号求解器作为核心执行预言机，解决自动形式化与定理预测中的瓶颈，实现了感知与推理的闭环，大幅提升了几何逻辑推理的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经符号几何求解器存在两大瓶颈：1. 自动形式化阶段缺乏与下游求解器的耦合；2. 定理预测阶段受限于固定的规则库，易陷入演绎僵局。

Method: 提出 SD-GPS 框架，包含两个核心模块：1. 求解器驱动的自动形式化（Solver-Driven Autoformalization），将可执行性作为强化学习信号；2. 验证式定理建议（Verified Theorem Proposing），引入 impasse-aware 智能体提出并验证局部辅助引理。系统基于 QwenVL3-2B 构建。

Result: 在 Geometry3K 和 PGPS9K 数据集上，SD-GPS 在标准补全、多项选择及跨模态引用任务中均优于现有的 MLLM、神经及神经符号方法。

Conclusion: 通过将多模态感知与符号执行闭环，SD-GPS 证明了神经智能体可以通过形式化系统进行接地，从而显著提升几何问题的可验证推理能力。

Abstract: Geometry Problem Solving have increasingly adopt the neuro-symbolic paradigm, combining neural intuition with symbolic rigor. However, current frameworks suffer from severe bottlenecks in two core stages: autoformalization, which treats multimodal translation as a static task decoupled from downstream solver compatibility, and theorem prediction, where solvers frequently hit a deductive impasse due to fixed rule libraries. To address these, we propose SD-GPS, a solver-driven framework that treats the symbolic solver as an execution oracle throughout both formalization and deduction. First, Solver-Driven Autoformalization unifies supervised formal-language adaptation and solvability-guided reinforcement learning into a single module built on QwenVL3-2B, making executability the central training signal. Second, Verified Theorem Proposing introduces an impasse-aware agent that proposes local auxiliary lemmas from current proof states, ensuring soundness by filtering all proposals through symbolic verification. Empirical evaluations on Geometry3K and PGPS9K demonstrate that SD-GPS consistently outperforms existing MLLM, neural, and neuro-symbolic methods across standard completion, multiple-choice, and cross-modal reference regimes, proving that closing the loop between multimodal perception and symbolic execution significantly improves geometric reasoning, offering profound insights into how neural agents can be grounded by formal systems to achieve verifiable problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [159] [RelBall: Relation Ball with Quaternion Rotation for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27967)
*Yike Liu,Peijia Xie,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RelBall 提出通过模量变换建模语义层次，并利用尾实体球体结构处理复杂的多向关系，弥补了现有模型在关系模式覆盖、多对多映射及语义表达上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KGC 模型（如 RotatE, Rotate3D）在处理复杂的非交换性组合模式、语义层次结构以及多向关系映射（1:N, M:N）时存在局限性，难以全面捕获知识图谱中的多样化关系模式。

Method: 提出 RelBall 模型，引入两项核心创新：1. 模量变换（Modulus Transformation）：通过调整模量大小映射概念的抽象程度（抽象概念模量小，具体实例模量大）；2. 以尾实体为中心的球体（Tail-centric relation ball）：通过球体结构建模复杂的多对一、一对多及多对多逻辑关系。

Result: 在多个标准数据集上的链路预测实验表明，RelBall 相比现有基线模型具有更强的性能，能够覆盖所有常见的关系模式，并提供了具有可解释性的语义分层表示。

Conclusion: RelBall 通过结合模量变换与以尾实体为中心的球体建模，成功解决了现有知识图谱补全模型在处理非交换性变换、语义层次结构及多对多关系方面的局限性，在链路预测任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Real-world knowledge graphs are often incomplete, lacking many valid facts. Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims to predict missing links using known triples, thereby enhancing graph coverage. A key challenge is modeling diverse relational patterns such as symmetry, antisymmetry, inversion, composition and semantic hierarchy. Existing models such as RotatE can capture symmetric, antisymmetric, inverse, and commutative composition patterns, yet struggle with non-commutative composition. Rotate3D addresses this by introducing non-commutativity via three-dimensional rotations, but still fails to capture the semantic hierarchies prevalent in knowledge graphs. Moreover, both models cannot effectively model one-to-many relations. To overcome these limitations, we propose RelBall, which extends Rotate3D with two innovations. First, our model introduces modulus transformation to model hierarchies, driving abstract concepts toward smaller moduli and concrete instances toward larger ones. Second, it introduces a tail-centric relation ball to model one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. RelBall offers the following advantages: (1) coverage of all relational patterns, including the ones mentioned above; (2) an interpretable hierarchical representation where the modulus directly reflect semantic levels; (3) support for one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate RelBall's competitive link prediction performance against various baselines.

</details>


### [160] [Lifted Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28024)
*Malte Luttermann,Tanya Braun,Ralf Möller,Marcel Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将提升推理技术引入因果推断，提出了参数化因果因子图（PCFG）和LCI算法，在保证精确性的同时大幅提高了关系域中因果效应的推断效率，并进一步通过PD-PCFG支持部分因果知识的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果推理多依赖于命题级模型，在关系推理任务中效率低下。本文旨在利用提升推理技术，通过挖掘概率图模型中对象的同质性（Indistinguishability），在保证推理精确性的前提下，加速关系域中的因果效应计算。

Method: 1. 提出参数化因果因子图（PCFG）对因果知识进行建模并定义干预语义；2. 设计提升因果推理（LCI）算法，在提升的层面执行干预与推理；3. 提出部分有向参数化因果因子图（PD-PCFG）以处理部分因果知识，并扩展LCI算法以适用于该通用模型。

Result: 提出的LCI算法相比传统的命题级因果推理算法（如基于因果贝叶斯网络的方法）具有显著的计算速度提升；同时，PD-PCFG的引入使得该方法能够处理因果关系先验知识不足的情况，扩展了算法的适用范围。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入参数化因果因子图（PCFG）及其泛化形式（PD-PCFG），成功将提升推理（Lifted Inference）技术应用于因果推断领域，显著提升了在关系域中计算因果效应的效率，同时保持了推理的精确性，并增强了对不完整因果知识的处理能力。

Abstract: Lifted inference exploits indistinguishabilities in probabilistic graphical models by using a representative for indistinguishable objects, thereby speeding up query answering while maintaining exact answers. In this article, we show how lifting can be applied to efficiently compute causal effects in relational domains. More specifically, we introduce parametric causal factor graphs (PCFGs) to incorporate causal knowledge in lifted models and give a formal semantics of interventions therein. We further present the Lifted Causal Inference (LCI) algorithm to compute causal effects on a lifted level, thereby drastically speeding up causal inference compared to propositional inference, e.g., in causal Bayesian networks. In addition, we present partially directed parametric causal factor graphs (PD-PCFGs) as a generalisation of PCFGs to handle partial causal knowledge and extend LCI to perform lifted causal inference in a PD-PCFG, thereby extending the applicability of lifted causal inference to a broader range of models requiring less prior knowledge about causal relationships.

</details>


### [161] [JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC) V1: An Industrial-Scale LLM/VLM-Centric Solution for Item Understanding, Management, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28070)
*Oxygen AIIC,Chan Long,Chao Liu,Chaofan Chen,Chaohui Dong,Chunyuan Guo,Danping Liu,Debin Liu,Deping Xiang,Fulai Xu,Guangyue Liu,Hao Li,Huichun Hu,Jian Yang,Jianan Wang,Jianbo Zhao,Jiaoyang Li,Jiaxing Wang,Jinglong Li,Jinjin Guo,Jun Fang,Jun Liu,Kai Zhou,Li Wang,Lili Gao,Liying Chen,Luning Yang,Mengdi Zhou,Pengzhang Liu,Qi Lv,Qianyun Wang,Qixia Jiang,Ruyue Li,Shimu Liang,Shuxing Wang,Sijie Zhang,Siqi Li,Tianhao Gao,Wang Ke,Weihu Huang,Wencan Lai,Wenjie Zhang,Xiaohui Zhang,Xiaojing Dong,Ya Liu,Yifeng Zhang,Yixiang Wang,Yongtai Zhang,Yongyi Liao,Zhaoru Chen,Zhen Chen,Zhiyong Ma,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhongwei Liu,Ziyan Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JD.com 推出了 Oxygen AIIC 平台，利用 LLM/VLM 技术解决了超大规模电商环境下的商品知识生产与服务难题，实现了高效率、高精度的知识资产化与业务赋能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对 JD.com 海量 SKU（百亿级）和复杂业务场景，解决概念快速演变、大规模高质量知识生产以及多样化下游需求带来的工业挑战。

Method: 构建了四个核心架构支柱：（1）人机协作的本体工程；（2）“先检索后判别”（S2D）的知识识别架构；（3）自演进的 LLM/VLM 模型；（4）统一的商品数据服务枢纽（数据隧道）。采用华为昇腾 NPU 进行大规模部署。

Result: 知识生产精度达 94.2%，召回率达 82.8%。在业务层面：搜索流量覆盖率达 80.4%，信息质量问题减少 37%，核心属性自动填充率超 80%。

Conclusion: Oxygen AIIC 证明了在大规模电商环境下，利用 LLM/VLM 构建工业级实时知识中心的可行性与有效性，实现了知识生产的自动化、高精度与规模化应用。

Abstract: JD.com, one of the world's largest e-commerce platforms, serves over 700 million active users and millions of merchants, with a catalog of tens of billions of SKUs. At this scale, high-quality, structured item knowledge underpins a better consumer experience, lower management costs, and higher operational efficiency-yet producing and serving it poses three industrial-scale challenges: fast-emerging concepts, high-quality knowledge production for massive SKUs, and diverse downstream requirements. To address these challenges, we present the JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC), an industrial-scale platform built on LLMs/VLMs for item-knowledge production and service. Oxygen AIIC is built around four core pillars: (i) ontology engineering driven by efficient human-AI collaboration, which supports the dynamic evolution and agile expansion of an ontology with millions of entries; (ii) a "Semantic Search then Discrimination"(S2D) knowledge identification architecture that, combined with throughput improvement strategies, enables scalable, extensible, and high-throughput AI Item Library production for tens of billions of SKUs; (iii) self-evolving item-understanding LLMs/VLMs that improve in a stable and controllable manner, enabling knowledge production with 94.2% precision and 82.8% recall; and (iv) a unified item tunnel that serves as the data and service hub. Oxygen AIIC now covers tens of thousands of JD categories and processes hundreds of millions of item updates per day on Huawei Ascend NPUs. It has accumulated hundreds of billions of item-knowledge assets. Deployed across core business scenarios-including search, recommendation, operations, category planning-Oxygen AIIC has delivered measurable gains at scale. Search-traffic coverage reaches 80.4%, item-information quality issues drop by 37%, the automated fill rate of core attributes during item listing exceeds 80%.

</details>


### [162] [Ontology-Guided Evidence Path Inference for Multi-hop Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28076)
*Yongxue Shan,Meihan Wu,Cundi Fang,Jie Peng,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPI 是一种本体引导的多跳 KGQA 框架，通过利用关系中心本体图和双向检索机制，在减少搜索空间的同时显著提高了复杂问题推理的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于主题扩展的 KGQA 方法面临搜索空间激增、噪声路径多以及难以满足复杂问题语义约束的挑战。

Method: 提出 OPI 框架：1) 构建以关系为中心的本体图以捕捉头尾类型约束；2) 设计双向检索机制（主题前缀扩展 + 答案端最终跳匹配）；3) 采用迭代精炼策略，结合问题上下文过滤不相关路径。

Result: 在 WebQSP、CWQ 和 MetaQA 数据集上均取得了显著提升，其中 WebQSP 的 Hit@1/F1 分别提升了 4.6/5.0 个百分点，MetaQA 上实现了近乎饱和的 Hit@1。

Conclusion: OPI 通过引入本体引导的证据路径推理，大幅优化了多跳 KGQA 的搜索效率与推理准确性，有效解决了搜索空间膨胀与噪声路径干扰问题。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) aims to answer natural-language questions by reasoning over structured facts. Existing multi-hop KGQA methods mainly rely on topic-centered expansion, which faces two key challenges: the search space rapidly grows with noisy mixed-type paths, and retrieved paths may fail to satisfy the semantic constraints of complex questions. To address these challenges, we propose OPI, an ontology-guided evidence path inference framework for multi-hop KGQA. OPI introduces a relation-centric ontology graph to capture the head-tail type constraints of relations, providing a compact interface for answer-side constraints. Based on this ontology graph, OPI first introduces a bidirectional retrieval mechanism by mapping the predicted answer type to compatible final-hop relations and combining topic-side prefix expansion with answer-side final-hop matching, thereby suppressing noisy mixed-type expansion. OPI further adopts an iterative refinement strategy to reassess retrieved paths and candidate answers under the question context, filtering type-compatible but question-irrelevant evidence for more reliable answer prediction. Experiments on WebQSP, CWQ, and MetaQA show that OPI substantially reduces the search space, improves Hit@1/F1 by 4.6/5.0 points on WebQSP and 8.9/3.3 points on CWQ over the strongest prior results, and achieves near-saturated Hit@1 on MetaQA with the retrieval module alone.

</details>


### [163] [Tandem Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28166)
*Difan Jiao,Raghav Singhal,Robert West,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对RLVR导致的推理模式“特异化”问题，研究提出了串联加强化学习（TRL），通过Senior与Junior的协同训练，在保持专家级推理性能的同时，大幅提升了模型间推理的可交流性与可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能显著提升大模型推理能力，但常导致模型产生难以被弱模型或人类理解的“特质化”推理模式。现有串联训练（Tandem Training）仅限于原型验证，需拓展至现代RLVR的长思维链规模以提升模型间的兼容性。

Method: 提出串联强化学习（Tandem Reinforcement Learning, TRL）：Senior模型与固定的Junior模型通过随机交替方式共同生成推理步骤，利用GRPO算法对Senior进行优化，从而引导Senior学习更符合Junior逻辑习惯的推理路径。

Result: 在竞赛级数学任务中，TRL的独立推理能力（Solo Reasoning）与常规GRPO持平；同时实现了更强的交接稳健性，减少了对Junior的分布漂移，并显著提升了思维链对Junior的可读性。

Conclusion: TRL成功将串联训练范式引入RLVR，在保持模型独立推理能力的同时，显著增强了模型间的思维链协作能力、降低了分布漂移，并提升了对较弱模型（junior）的可读性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved the reasoning capability of large language models, reaching expert or even superhuman performance in domains such as competition math. However, whether weaker agents and humans can actually harness this capability is far less certain, with RLVR documented to drift reasoning toward idiosyncratic patterns such as poor readability and language mixing. Tandem training is a recently introduced paradigm that targets this compatibility problem: a trained, stronger senior co-generates each rollout with a frozen, weaker junior, and the two are rewarded as a team, so the senior is pushed to reason in ways the junior can follow. Yet this paradigm has so far been demonstrated only in proof-of-concept settings, leaving open whether it scales to the long chains of thought of the modern RLVR pipeline. In this work, we propose Tandem Reinforcement Learning (TRL), which carries the tandem training paradigm into RLVR. In TRL, the senior and a frozen junior alternate stochastically to co-generate the reasoning, the resulting generation is rewarded, and the standard GRPO loss is applied to the senior. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct on competition math, we find that TRL matches vanilla GRPO on solo reasoning capability while three properties emerge together from the same rollout structure: stronger handoff robustness with the junior, reduced distributional drift from the junior, and a chain-of-thought more legible to the junior. Our results demonstrate a promising route for RLVR with practical payoffs in multi-model communication and human compatibility.

</details>


### [164] [Agent-Native Immune System: Architecture, Taxonomy, and Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28270)
*Bo Shen,Lifeng Chang,Tianyuan Wei,Yunpeng Li,Feng Shi,Yichen Han,Peijie Gao,Shiyi Kuang,Xin Chang,Dehui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为解决自主智能体运行时易受攻击的问题，本文提出了“智能体原生免疫系统（ANIS）”，通过将防御机制内嵌至智能体的认知循环中，实现了类似于生物免疫系统的动态自适应安全防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 安全机制（如边界安全和对齐训练）在面对具备持久记忆、工具调用和多智能体协作的“自主智能体”时，因处于推理循环外部而失效，导致智能体易受运行时攻击（如记忆中毒、工具链篡改）。

Method: 提出了“智能体原生免疫系统”（ANIS），包含：1. 六层“免疫塔”（L0-L5）架构，重点引入了非认知的阻断免疫层；2. 智能体病毒与疫苗的分类法；3. “Harness”三元组（Meta, Self, Auto）监测引擎，支持持续免疫学习（CIL）。

Result: 构建了首个内嵌于智能体认知循环的防御框架，将静态对齐转变为动态的运行时“执法”机制，并定义了衡量安全效能的新评估指标（如自动免疫率）。

Conclusion: ANIS 提出了生物启发式防御架构，强调了动态运行时“免疫”与静态训练期“对齐”在 AI 智能体安全中的本质区别，为主流 AI 安全防御方案开辟了新研究方向。

Abstract: The transition from static chat bots to autonomous agents--equipped with persistent memory, tool-use protocols, and multi-agent collaboration--has fundamentally expanded the AI threat landscape. Current defense mechanisms, such as perimeter security and training-time alignment, remain external to the agent's active reasoning loop. Consequently, they fall short: a fully aligned agent remains highly vulnerable to runtime hijacking via memory poisoning, tool-chain manipulation, or multi-agent protocol attacks. To address this critical gap, we introduce the Agent-Native Immune System (ANIS), the first biologically inspired, endogenous defense architecture embedded directly within the agent's cognitive loop. Our framework presents four primary contributions. First, we design a six-layer Immune Tower (L0-L5), distinctly incorporating Barrier Immunity (L1) as a non-cognitive, physical-and-logical isolation layer. Second, we establish a unified taxonomy of Agent Viruses and Agent Vaccines, formalizing the critical distinction between superficial non-parametric defenses and robust parametric vaccines. Third, we conceptualize the Harness Triad--Meta, Self, and Auto--a self-monitoring, meta-cognitive automation backbone that drives Continual Immune Learning (CIL), enabling vaccines to dynamically adapt to novel threats. Finally, we establish a rigorous theoretical demarcation between model alignment and agent immunity: while alignment provides a static "constitutional" value foundation during training, ANIS serves as the dynamic "law enforcement" mechanism during runtime. We conclude by framing open challenges for the field, including immune protocol standardization, novel evaluation metrics such as the Autoimmunity Rate (false-positive intervention rate), and the co-evolutionary dynamics between pathogens and vaccines within collective intelligence ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Support-Constrained RL Enables Real-World Policy Improvement without Real-World Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27475)
*Raymond Yu,William Huey,Mustafa Mukadam,Anusha Nagabandi,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决机器人仿真训练中的不可迁移问题，SCORE 框架通过“流引导”将模拟器中的强化学习限制在真实数据预训练策略的动作支撑集内，在不损失策略改进空间的前提下，大幅提升了复杂灵巧手任务的真实世界操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在真实世界使用 RL 成本昂贵且效率低；直接在仿真中进行无约束 RL 会利用物理模拟漏洞（SIM-to-Real gap），导致策略无法迁移到硬件；而现有正则化方法往往因过度约束导致策略改进受限。

Method: 提出 SCORE (Support-Constrained Off-Domain REinforcement) 框架：1. 使用真实世界数据预训练生成式策略；2. 在仿真环境中使用强化学习进行改进；3. 引入“流引导”（flow steering）约束机制，将模拟环境下的策略改进限制在预训练策略能够执行的动作空间内，从而避免利用仿真与现实间的动力学差异，确保策略的可迁移性。

Result: 在八项复杂的灵巧手多指操纵任务中，SCORE 将平均成功率从 37.8% 提升至 89.9%（对比最佳基线为 59.5%），且所需训练步数比基础策略减少了 36.8%。

Conclusion: SCORE 框架证明了通过在仿真中对策略优化施加适当的约束，可以显著提升真实世界的机器人操作策略。通过将强化学习限制在预训练策略的支撑集（Support）内，SCORE 实现了一种高效、可迁移且稳健的“从实到仿再到实”的改进范式。

Abstract: Robots trained on real world data tend to be imprecise, slow, and brittle to perturbations. Improving these policies with reinforcement learning (RL) is an appealing alternative, but this process often requires expensive training in the real world. Performing policy improvement in simulation instead provides a far cheaper alternative, but unconstrained RL in simulation can exploit contact and dynamics mismatches, resulting in unsafe behaviors that do not transfer to hardware. Common forms of regularization can furthermore limit improvement by overconstraining to an imperfect behavior prior. In this work, we propose Support-Constrained Off-Domain REinforcement (SCORE), a real-to-sim-to-real framework that constrains RL in simulation to the support of a generative policy pretrained on real data. We instantiate this constraint through flow steering, restricting SCORE to actions the base policy can already produce, which ensures transferable behaviors while maximizing policy improvement. Improving a policy with SCORE requires minimal effort: it learns from sparse rewards, avoids distillation, and leaves the base policy untouched. Across eight real-world dexterous multi-fingered robotic manipulation tasks, SCORE improves average success rate from 37.8% to 89.9%, compared to 59.5% for the best baseline, and reaches success in 36.8% fewer steps than the base policy. Ultimately, through extensive experiments and ablations, we show that simulation can substantially improve real-world manipulation policies when policy optimization is appropriately constrained, introducing a new paradigm for real-to-sim-to-real policy improvement. Videos and code are available at https://weirdlabuw.github.io/score/.

</details>


### [166] [AO-ARC: Almost-Surely Asymptotically Optimal Multi-Robot Motion Planning with ARC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27495)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AO-ARC 是一种新型随时多机器人运动规划算法，它结合了 ARC 方法与 AO-x 框架，显著提升了大规模多机器人系统下路径规划的求解效率、收敛速度及计算可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有 MRMP 方法在多机器人数量增加时，初始求解耗时长或收敛速度慢且不稳定（非随时）的问题。

Method: 采用 AO-x 元算法，将原有的 ARC 方法转化为随时算法（Anytime Algorithm）。通过基于 makespan（完工时间）成本度量对有界 MRMP 实例进行迭代求解，利用 ARC 的自适应解耦特性并保持成本界限的一致性。

Result: 在 2D 多机器人场景和复杂的 3D 机械臂场景中，AO-ARC 展现出与最先进可行性求解器相当的初始求解速度，且在收敛速度和可靠性上明显优于现有的随时 MRMP 方法。

Conclusion: AO-ARC 是一种高效的随时多机器人运动规划（MRMP）算法，通过将 ARC 方法与 AO-x 元算法结合，实现了快速的初始规划与收敛，并在理论与实验上验证了其在不同复杂场景下的有效性与渐近最优性。

Abstract: We present AO-ARC, an anytime multi-robot motion planning (MRMP) method that achieves initial solution times on par with state-of-the-art MRMP feasibility solvers while converging faster and more reliably than existing anytime MRMP methods as the number of robots increases. AO-ARC adapts the AO-x meta-algorithm for converting feasibility solvers into anytime algorithms by iteratively calling the original ARC method on bounded MRMP instances under a makespan cost metric. This exploits the adaptive (de)coupling of ARC while maintaining the consistent cost bound across robot (de)compositions needed for AO-x. We provide theoretical analysis proving the asymptotic optimality properties of AO- ARC and conduct empirical evaluation on a set of 2D scenarios with different levels of coordination complexity and a 3D manipulator scenario representative of real-world applications.

</details>


### [167] [P-ARC: Exploiting Subproblem Independence for Parallel Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27625)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 P-ARC 算法，通过对 ARC 运动规划框架进行阶段性并行化及多起始点策略优化，显著提升了大规模多机器人系统的运动规划速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人运动规划（MRMP）在高并发场景下计算复杂度极高，传统的串行 ARC 算法无法充分利用多核计算资源，导致在大规模机器人团队中的规划效率瓶颈。

Method: 1. 组件级并行化：针对 ARC 的三个主要阶段分别设计并行实现；2. 架构复合：引入 OR-parallel 多起始点策略，形成混合并行策略 OR-P-ARC；3. 实验验证：在 2D 移动机器人和 Panda 机械臂场景（最多 128 个机器人）中评估并行效率。

Result: 在高规模多机器人场景（如 128 个机器人）中，利用 16 CPU 核心，在真实场景启发的 Panda 机械臂规划任务中，实现了近 4 倍的规划时间加速。

Conclusion: 通过将 ARC 算法的三个关键阶段（初始解生成、冲突检测、冲突解决）解耦并进行并行化，以及引入多起始点（OR-parallel）策略，成功实现了多机器人运动规划的显著加速。研究表明，在 16 核 CPU 上，对于大规模机械臂团队，规划时间可实现接近 4 倍的加速。

Abstract: This paper presents Parallel ARC (P-ARC), a parallel variant of the Adaptive Robot Coordination (ARC) approach to multi-robot motion planning (MRMP). P-ARC proposes a parallel variant for each of the three main stages in ARC: initial individual solutions, conflict detection, and conflict resolution, exploiting the independence created by ARC's decomposition of the MRMP problem. Additionally, we employ an OR-parallel multi-start strategy to both ARC and P-ARC, creating a hybrid parallel strategy OR-P-ARC. We evaluate the impact of the different parallel strategies for ARC using a set of scaling 2D mobile and planar manipulator scenarios with up to 128 robots to control for conflicts and work distribution across the stages of ARC. Additionally, we demonstrate planning time speedups approaching 4X over the sequential version for large Panda multi-manipulator teams in real-world inspired scenarios when deploying 16 CPU cores.

</details>


### [168] [Spacecraft Fiducial Marker for Autonomous Rendezvous, Proximity Operations, and Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27566)
*Ravi Kumar Thakur,Matouš Vrba,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AstraTag是一种针对空间机器人研制的多尺度递归视觉标记，通过Spidron图案设计与TPS几何校正，显著提升了目标在近距离及曲面飞行器表面上的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准标记多为单尺度设计，在近距离接近与对接阶段极易移出视场，且在处理空间机器人常见的曲面几何形状时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 1. 设计基于方形Spidron（螺旋）图案的递归自相似标记模板，实现多尺度检测；2. 利用48位签名结合广义里德-所罗门码（GRS）进行识别；3. 流程包含轮廓定位、透视归一化和签名匹配；4. 针对曲面畸变引入薄板样条（TPS）重映射方案。

Result: 在平板和曲面太空飞行器模型上的测试表明，AstraTag在曲面上的检测率优于Fractal ArUco和AprilTag，有效解决了近距离深度探测及曲面失真的挑战。

Conclusion: AstraTag是一种针对空间机器人任务设计的高效、鲁棒的视觉基准标记系统，特别解决了传统标记在近距离观测和曲面附着时性能下降的问题，在空间操作任务中具有显著的技术优势。

Abstract: Robotic operations in space are challenging due to the harsh environment and the high cost of failure. Fiducial markers provide visual references that aid autonomous rendezvous, proximity operations, and docking for space robots. However, existing fiducial markers are mostly single-scale and largely designed for terrestrial robotics. Such markers leave the camera's field of view at close range, precisely during the proximity and docking phases where reliable tracking is most critical. This paper presents AstraTag, a fiducial marker designed for autonomous on-orbit robotic operations. The marker template is based on a square Spidron pattern whose recursive, self-similar structure enables detection across multiple spatial scales. Marker identification uses a 48-bit signature derived from triangular sub-regions of the template and encoded with a Generalised Reed-Solomon (GRS) code. The detection pipeline performs contour-based quadrilateral localisation, perspective normalisation, and signature matching against a pre-computed dictionary. To handle markers affixed to curved spacecraft surfaces, it incorporates a Thin-Plate Spline (TPS) re-warp fallback that exploits the marker's internal rectangular borders as additional geometric correspondences. We benchmark AstraTag against three-layer Fractal ArUco and AprilTag on spacecraft mockups with flat and curved surfaces. On curved surfaces, AstraTag achieves a higher detection rate than both baselines, offering a robust recursive-marker option for space robotics.

</details>


### [169] [SceneBot: Contact-Prompted General Humanoid Whole Body Tracking with Scene-Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27581)
*Sirui Chen,Shibo Zhao,Zhen Wu,Jiaman Li,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SceneBot是一个统一的人形机器人运动跟踪框架，通过引入接触标签和后见之明场景重建技术，解决了机器人难以完成复杂接触密集型任务的问题，实现了自由运动与物理交互的协同控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人强化学习策略虽擅长自由空间运动，但在面对复杂的接触密集型任务（如操作物体或行走于不平坦地形）时，缺乏处理物理交互模糊性的能力。

Method: 提出一种统一的运动跟踪框架，通过在单策略中同时引入参考运动和各肢体接触标签（contact labels）；开发“后见之明场景重建”（hindsight scene reconstruction）方法，从重定向的人体运动中推断场景交互图，以解决交互数据标注匮乏的问题。

Result: 在7.5小时的重建接触密集型数据上进行训练，SceneBot成功泛化至未见过的运动与环境，并实现了如搬运箱子上楼等复杂、长跨度任务的无缝执行。

Conclusion: SceneBot是首个能够统一实现自由空间运动与接触密集任务的通用人形机器人框架，验证了接触条件化作为人形机器人控制接口的有效性。

Abstract: Current humanoid reinforcement-learning policies excel at free-space motions but struggle with contact-rich tasks, as pure kinematic tracking cannot resolve the physical ambiguities of interacting with objects and uneven terrain. To address this, we introduce SceneBot, a unified motion-tracking framework capable of handling freespace locomotion, terrain traversal, and whole-body manipulation. SceneBot conditions a single policy on both reference motions and per-link contact labels, explicitly defining expected environmental interactions. To overcome the lack of annotated interaction data, we propose a hindsight scene reconstruction approach that infers scene-interaction graphs from retargeted human motion. Trained on 7.5 hours of this reconstructed, contact-rich data, SceneBot successfully generalizes to unseen motions and environments. Our results demonstrate that SceneBot is the first general framework to seamlessly unify free-space and contact-rich behaviors executing complex, long-horizon tasks like carrying a box upstairs and establishing contact conditioning as a powerful interface for humanoid control. All code and data will be open-sourced. More demos and information are available at: https://ericcsr.github.io/scenebot/

</details>


### [170] [Learning to Throw: Agile and Accurate Cable-Suspended Payload Delivery with a Quadrotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27603)
*Yifan Zhai,Elia Raimondi,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yannick Armati,Jiaxu Xing,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将四旋翼物理模型与绳索动力学仿真相结合的混合训练框架，通过深度强化学习实现了四旋翼载荷的高精度动态投掷，在硬件端显著优化了敏捷性与投掷准确率的均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机在悬挂载荷任务中表现出极高的敏捷性需求，但现有的基于轨迹优化的传统模型因绳索动力学建模困难、跟踪误差大及保守约束限制，导致载荷释放任务中无法兼顾效率与精度。

Method: 1. 构建混合仿真框架：通过耦合高保真解析四旋翼模型与物理引擎（处理复杂绳索动力学）；2. 深度强化学习：利用该环境训练RL策略以实现精准目标投掷；3. 零样本迁移：将训练好的RL策略直接部署于硬件，并对比分析状态空间策略与视觉观察策略。

Result: 实验表明，RL策略在硬件端实现了将落点误差降低高达 50%、投掷时间缩短高达 30% 的性能提升，全面超越了基于模型的基线方法；同时验证了基于视觉的策略同样具备高度实用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于混合仿真环境训练的深度强化学习策略，成功实现了四旋翼无人机高精度、高敏捷性的悬挂载荷投掷。实验证明，该方法在真实硬件上的零样本部署性能显著优于传统基于模型的控制方法，并验证了仅依靠视觉输入也能达到相近的精确度，为动态空中作业研究提供了有效的仿真与训练框架。

Abstract: Quadrotors offer the agility needed to rapidly transport suspended payloads during time-critical applications, including search-and-rescue and medical delivery. While suspended-payload transport and traversal for these missions are well studied, the highly dynamic targeted release of the payload remains comparatively underexplored. State-of-the-art approaches typically rely on model-based trajectory optimization and tracking; however, these methods often yield sub-optimal performance due to conservative feasibility constraints, tracking errors, and the inherent difficulty of analytically modeling flexible rope dynamics. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hybrid simulation framework that couples a high-fidelity analytical quadrotor model with a physics solver for complex rope and payload interactions. By exchanging forces between the two domains at every step, we obtain a physically accurate simulation of the suspended-payload system. Leveraging this environment, we train a deep reinforcement learning (RL) policy that executes agile, accurate payload throws to designated targets. Deployed zero-shot on hardware, our RL policy pushes the boundary of the agility-accuracy trade-off, outperforming the model-based baseline by reducing the landing error by up to 50% and the throw duration by up to 30%. Ablation studies confirm that the coupled simulation is the key enabler of these gains. We further show that the same pipeline trains a policy driven by visual observations rather than an explicit state estimate, achieving accuracy comparable to that of the state-based policy. To accelerate future research in dynamic aerial manipulation, we open-source the simulator to the community upon acceptance.

</details>


### [171] [Physics-Guided Robotic Radiation Source Localization along Arbitrary Measurement Paths in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27624)
*Hojoon Son,Kai Tan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种不受路径限制的机器人辐射源定位（RSL）框架，利用物理启发式机器学习（PIML）处理复杂辐射衰减，实现了在未知环境下的高精度空间感知与持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的辐射源定位（RSL）方法往往依赖于规划机器人接近辐射源以提高精度，这增加了辐射损害机器人的风险；同时，单一目标的路径规划限制了机器人在复杂放射性环境中的任务灵活性。因此，需要开发一种不受路径限制且能更安全、高效进行源定位的框架。

Method: 提出了一种结合物理信息的机器学习（PIML）模型，核心包括：1. 设计物理启发模型张量以处理复杂障碍物引起的伽马射线通量衰减；2. 采用并行多模型计算以增强鲁棒性；3. 利用蒙特卡罗粒子输运进行高保真仿真训练与验证，并辅以物理实验验证；4. 引入持续学习技术，支持实际机器人部署中的在线性能优化。

Result: 通过在多尺度、多种源类型及多种障碍物材料/几何结构的随机化高保真仿真环境以及物理实验中验证，该方法不仅实现了在未知环境下的高精度定位，且无需预设特定路径，展示了极强的通用性和适应性。持续学习技术的应用进一步增强了系统在真实环境中的实用价值。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的基于物理信息机器学习（PIML）的自动化框架，成功将辐射源定位从单纯的“点探测”升级为“空间感知”，在无需特定路径规划的情况下实现了高精度、鲁棒的源定位，并具备在实际部署中的持续学习能力，提升了机器人在未知放射性环境中的作业安全性和灵活性。

Abstract: Using robots to estimate the location of the radiation source is an effective way to improve efficiency and safety. Existing methods focus on planning the robot's path to achieve precise estimation, typically approaching the source. However, approaching the source increases the risk of radiation damage to a robot. In addition, a path-planning algorithm designed solely for radiation source localization (RSL) limits the flexibility of missions that deploy robots into radioactive environments. This study presents an automation framework for robotic RSL that leverages a physics-informed machine learning (PIML) model to precisely estimate the source location, regardless of measurement paths, in unknown environments. Physics-inspired model tensors have been designed for PIML to handle attenuated gamma-ray flux signals from unknown obstacles, and multiple models are computed in parallel to improve the robustness and precision of the RSL. The proposed method is evaluated in high-fidelity simulation environments using Monte Carlo particle transport across diverse randomized domains, including spatial scales, radiation source types, obstacle materials and geometries, and robot trajectories. The method is also validated through physical experiments on configurations that are not included in the simulation-based evaluation. The continuous learning technique is applied in real-robot deployment to enhance the practical applicability of the online robotic RSL system. The proposed method advances robot radiation perception from pointwise flux detection to spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [172] [Direct Action-Head Injection of A Grounded 3D Point Unlocks Spatial and Task Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27663)
*Shiang-Feng Tsai,Jin-Cheng Jhang,Yen-Ling Tai,Jia-Hong Lai,Shih-Yun Wong,KangTung-Hsu,Yi-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了VLA模型泛化能力的瓶颈在于接地信号表示方式，提出了一种将3D空间嵌入直接注入动作头的轻量级模块，大幅提升了机器人在任务与空间上的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在面对训练时未见过的目标位置（空间泛化）或与旧场景配对的新指令（任务泛化）时表现脆弱，且现有的语言/视觉提示方法难以根本解决此局限性。

Method: 提出一个轻量级、模型无关的模块：通过两层MLP将接地信号（Grounding signal）表征为3D空间坐标，计算其与末端执行器的相对位移，并通过自适应层归一化（Adaptive Layer Normalization）直接注入动作头。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO基准测试中，GR00T-N1.6在任务扰动下的成功率从31.2%提高到77.5%（+46.3），位置扰动下从28.1%提高到60.2%（+32.1）；该方法在不同骨干模型（如π0.5）上均表现出显著增益。

Conclusion: 在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中，将接地信号表示为3D空间信息并作为嵌入直接注入至动作头，是提升模型在空间和任务泛化能力上的关键，且无需修改预训练骨干网络。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage large-scale vision-language pretraining for flexible robot manipulation, yet at test time they remain brittle along two axes: spatial generalization, when object positions differ from those seen during training, and task generalization, when a familiar scene is paired with a different language instruction than the one seen in training. A growing family of methods addresses this brittleness by endowing a policy with the spatial and task-aware information such as 2D pixel-coordinate for object localization and placement. However, we find that existing representation through language prompting or visual prompting does not address the limitations; in contrast, exploiting a 3D point-based representation and feeding it directly to the action head leads to substantial improvements-revealing that how the grounding signal is represented and injected into the VLA is the true game changer. Thus, we propose a lightweight, model-agnostic module that represents the grounding signal in 3D, computes its relative displacement to the gripper, and injects the resulting spatial embedding directly into the action head through adaptive layer normalization. The entire module is a two-layer MLP that requires no changes to the VLA backbone or pretraining pipeline. On LIBERO-PRO, our method improves the average success rate of GR00T-N1.6 from 31.2 to 77.5 points under task perturbation and from 28.1 to 60.2 points under position perturbation (gains of 46.3 and 32.1 points). Comparable gains are achieved for $π_{0.5}$ as well, demonstrating that the mechanism is backbone-agnostic. Together, these results support our central finding: given adequate grounding lifted into 3D, injecting it directly into the action head is what unlocks both spatial and task generalization in VLAs-achievable with nothing more than a lightweight module on top of a pretrained backbone.

</details>


### [173] [CWI: Composite Humanoid Whole-Body Imitation System for Loco-manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27676)
*Wenqi Ge,Junde Guo,Zhen Fu,Shunpeng Yang,Jiayu Chen,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CWI框架解决人型机器人全身运动协调难题，通过解耦上肢模仿与下肢对齐运动，成功实现了在无需全身动捕数据的情况下，完成高质量的全身协调操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身控制器在处理复杂任务时存在局限：纯指令采样方法缺乏人体运动学特征且难以训练；而直接模仿全身MoCap数据则因数据集不平衡和动作侵略性导致稳定性差，且需要繁琐的手动过滤。因此，需要一种既能保持操作灵活性又能确保稳定 locomotion 的综合方案。

Method: 提出“组合全身模仿”（CWI）框架，核心思想是决策解耦：利用多样化的MoCap数据集进行上肢操作学习，并利用采用AMP（对抗运动先验）的“双判别器”架构来指导下肢稳定行走与下蹲。采用“多批评家”（Multi-critic）架构平衡多重任务冲突，并结合师生蒸馏（Teacher-Student Distillation）技术，最终实现仅依赖双臂位姿和速度/高度指令的全身策略。

Result: 在仿真实验和全尺寸人形机器人（LimX Oli）的真实场地部署中，CWI展现出极具竞争力的运动操控能力，具备良好的全身协调性，并在无需全身动捕的情况下实现了高效的遥操作。

Conclusion: CWI方法通过解耦上肢操作与下肢运动的训练，成功实现了人形机器人在无需全身动捕数据的情况下，完成稳定、连贯的全身操控任务。该方法在仿真和真实人形机器人（LimX Oli）上验证了其鲁棒性和实用价值，证明了无需昂贵动捕设备进行全身协调控制的可行性。

Abstract: Achieving everyday tasks with humanoid robots requires coordinating stable locomotion with versatile manipulation. However, existing whole-body controllers still face significant challenges. Methods trained solely via command sampling, without motion-capture (MoCap) data, often struggle with sparse rewards and require carefully tuned curricula to converge. This is especially problematic for upper-body control, where the resulting motions deviate from human-like statistics and degrade whole-body coordination. Conversely, approaches that imitate full-body MoCap data suffer from dataset imbalance, as many locomotion trajectories are overly aggressive for stable-locomotion scenarios, necessitating extensive data filtering and augmentation. To address this, we present Composite Whole-Body Imitation (CWI), a framework that decouples the use of MoCap data for upper-body manipulation and lower-body locomotion. This decoupling allows us to exploit the full MoCap dataset of diverse manipulation references, while stable, command-conditioned lower-body locomotion is guided by dual discriminators trained on curated expert-quality walking and squatting clips via an Adversarial Motion Prior (AMP). A multi-critic architecture reduces conflicts among locomotion, manipulation, and motion-style objectives, and a teacher--student distillation stage yields a whole-body policy conditioned only on bimanual hand poses and velocity/height commands. We evaluate CWI through simulation experiments and real-world deployment on a full-size LimX Oli humanoid. The results show competitive loco-manipulation performance, robust whole-body coordination, and practical teleoperation without full-body motion-capture equipment. A project page with supplementary material can be found at https://cwi-ral.github.io/CWI-RAL-Webpage.

</details>


### [174] [DIM-WAM: World-Action Modeling with Diverse Historical Event Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27677)
*Kai Wang,Zhaopeng Gu,Yixiang Chen,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Peng Su,Zhaowen Li,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决长程机器人操作任务中的“长时遗忘”和“全局状态感知差”问题，本文提出了DiM-WAM模型，通过引入多尺度记忆存储与进度监督机制，显著提升了机器人执行复杂任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型（World-action models）多依赖于短时历史和短距离预测，难以应对需要跨阶段依赖和全局任务意识的长程机器人操作任务，导致长时遗忘及任务状态感知失效。

Method: 提出了一种记忆增强的世界-动作模型（DiM-WAM），包含：1. 视觉事件特征提取器；2. 基于相似度合并的多库内存系统（用于存储长期上下文）；3. 结合时间与身份嵌入的读取机制，用于引导视频与动作预测；4. 进度监督目标（Progress-supervision objective），以显式建模任务阶段及进度。

Result: 在RMBench基准测试上，平均成功率从28.4%提升至69.8%；在四个真实世界的Franka机器人任务中，平均阶段成功率从70.7%提升至91.5%，完整任务成功率从52.5%提升至80.0%。

Conclusion: DiM-WAM通过引入多尺度记忆增强机制和进度监督目标，有效解决了机器人模型在长程任务中的遗忘问题和对全局任务状态感知不足的缺陷，显著提升了机器人操作性能。

Abstract: World-action models have shown promising robot-manipulation performance by jointly predicting future visual states and actions. However, existing methods mainly rely on short-term history and short-horizon future prediction, which is insufficient for long-horizon tasks whose correct execution depends on earlier observations and task progress. Such temporally dependent tasks require effective use of complementary temporal information, including recent local context, cross-stage historical events, immediate future dynamics, and global task progress. To address long-term forgetting and poor awareness of the global task state, we introduce DiM-WAM, a memory-augmented world-action model that integrates multi-scale historical context, local future dynamics, and global task progress. The memory extracts compact visual event information from real observations, updates multiple memory banks through independent similarity-based merging, and then reads the bank-identity- and time-embedded long-term context to condition video and action denoising. A progress-supervision objective further encourages memory tokens to encode not only completed historical events but also the current task stage and its implications for the remaining task. On RMBench, DiM-WAM raises average success from 28.4% with LingBot-VA to 69.8%, exceeding the explicit-memory Mem-0 baseline at 42.0%. On four real-world Franka tasks, it improves average stage success from 70.7% to 91.5% and full-task success from 52.5% to 80.0%. Project page: https://wangkai-casia.github.io/dim-wam/{\texttt{https://wangkai-casia.github.io/dim-wam/}}.

</details>


### [175] [Drop-Then-Recovery: How Redundant Are Vision-Language-Action Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27755)
*Guoheng Sun,Kaixi Feng,Shwai He,Xiaochuan Gong,Yexiao He,Ziyao Wang,Zheyu Shen,Wanghao Ye,Ramana Rao Kompella,Gaowen Liu,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文揭示了VLA模型中语言主干存在严重冗余，通过提出DTR协议和GateProbe指标发现，目前主流的机器人控制任务无需深度语言模型，删减语言层后性能仍可保持甚至提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型盲目借用大型预训练VLM的语言主干，导致模型计算资源过剩，且尚不明确处理短指令的机器人闭环控制任务到底需要多少模型表达能力。

Method: 提出了“Drop-Then-Recovery (DTR)”分析协议，结合“GateProbe”（一种单次虚拟门控敏感度指标）来评估和剔除冗余的Transformer层，并通过微调验证模型性能恢复能力。

Result: 实验表明，移除VLA模型中一半甚至绝大部分语言层（仅保留2层）后，不仅不会降低性能，反而能通过微调提升任务成功率；相比之下，视觉和动作路径对结构删减的容忍度极低。

Conclusion: VLA模型中当前的语言主干存在显著的架构冗余，对于典型的机器人操作任务而言，语言能力并不需要深度模型支撑，而视觉和动作路径对模型容量更敏感。未来研究应更审慎地在各模态间分配计算资源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable instruction-driven robotic manipulation, but they inherit oversized language backbones from pretrained VLMs whose capacity far exceeds what is needed for short robotic instructions. This raises a basic question: how much of a VLA model is actually necessary for closed-loop control? In this work, we study architectural redundancy in VLA models by using transformer block removal as a controlled intervention. We introduce \textbf{Drop-Then-Recovery (DTR)}, an analysis protocol that removes selected blocks from a pretrained VLA model and then fine-tunes the resulting model to measure whether the removed capacity was necessary for downstream control. To make this intervention reliable, we propose \textbf{GateProbe}, a one-shot virtual-gate sensitivity metric that ranks blocks by their contribution to the downstream action loss. Across multiple VLA architectures, manipulation benchmarks and even real-robot industrial scenarios, we find a strong asymmetry in post-removal recoverability: \ul{\textit{language backbones are highly redundant for standard robotic manipulation tasks, whereas vision and action pathways are substantially less tolerant to removal}}. On LIBERO, removing half of the LLM blocks even improves OpenVLA-OFT from 95.0% to 98.3% under the same downstream fine-tuning budget, and retaining only two language blocks still recovers baseline-level performance. These results suggest that current VLA benchmarks may exert limited pressure on deep language grounding and compositional instruction understanding, and that future VLA architectures should allocate capacity more deliberately across language, vision, and action components. The code is available at https://github.com/s1ghhh/VLADrop.

</details>


### [176] [SpikeVLA: Vision-Language-Action Models with Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27807)
*Ruiqi Song,Dujun Nie,Siyu Teng,Baiyong Ding,Xiaotong Zhang,Dong Li,Chenming Zhang,Yuchen Li,Hangbin Wu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SpikeVLA 是一种新型脉冲视觉-语言-动作架构，通过引入脉冲神经网络和事件驱动推理，大幅降低了具身智能模型的能效负担，实现了低功耗场景下的高效实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型多基于大型 Transformer，导致高推理延迟和高能耗，限制了在低功耗、实时具身智能场景下的部署应用。

Method: 提出了一种端到端的脉冲视觉-语言-动作（SpikeVLA）架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) Spike-V：利用事件驱动脉冲层替代密集连续层的视觉编码器；(2) Spike-L：通过脉冲动力学和令牌级稀疏性进行多模态推理的脉冲大语言模型；(3) Spike-A：采用拉普拉斯核群体编码的多层全连接SNN动作策略网络。

Result: 在导航和机器人控制任务中的实验表明，SpikeVLA 在显著降低能耗和计算复杂度的同时，依然保持了与现有主流模型相当的性能表现。

Conclusion: SpikeVLA 证明了脉冲神经网络（SNN）在具身智能领域的可行性，通过显著降低能耗和计算成本，同时保持竞争性的任务性能，为低功耗、实时机器人控制提供了一种高效的架构方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a dominant paradigm for embodied intelligence. However, most existing approaches are built on large-scale transformers, resulting in substantial inference latency and energy consumption that limit their practical deployment in low-power, real-time scenarios. We propose SpikeVLA, a spiking VLA architecture for embodied navigation with energy-efficient inference, consisting of three key components. (i) A spiking vision encoder, Spike-V, that replaces dense continuous layers with event-driven spiking layers to reduce the energy consumption of visual representation learning. (ii) A multi-modal spiking large language model, Spike-L, that reformulates cross-modal reasoning with spiking dynamics and token-level event-driven sparsity to further lower computational cost. (iii) A spiking action policy network, Spike-A employs Laplacian-kernel population coding with a multi-layer fully connected SNN, and decodes spiking activities into stable and robust continuous control with energy-efficient inference under low-power constraints. Experiments on navigation and robotic control tasks show that SpikeVLA significantly reduces energy consumption and computational cost while maintaining competitive performance, highlighting its potential for low-power, real-time embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [177] [LXD-SLAM: LiDAR+X Dense SLAM with $\sum_{i=0}^{5}C_5^i$ Configurable Sensor Combinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27811)
*Zhong Wang,Lin Zhang,Linfei Li,Ying Shen,Shaoming Zhang,Pengcheng Shi,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LXD-SLAM 是一种模块化、高通用性的激光雷达多传感器融合 SLAM 系统，通过高斯过程建模和统一的卡尔曼滤波，实现了复杂环境下的高精度实时稠密建图与全局一致性优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SLAM 系统在复杂几何退化环境下的全局一致性和传感器漂移问题上表现不足，且缺乏针对多平台集成的模块化设计以及数学一致性。

Method: 提出了一种基于激光雷达为主、支持多种传感器（相机、IMU、轮速计、GNSS）的统一融合框架。核心方法包括：1. 采用数学统一的迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波 (IESKF)；2. 利用多层高斯过程 (GP) 子网格进行环境建模以实现深度恢复；3. 引入扩展扫描上下文 (ESC) 描述符和双向 PnP 优化，实现了稳健的多模态回环检测。

Result: LXD-SLAM 支持多达 32 种传感器组合，在多个公开数据集和实际场景中验证了其性能，在保持实时运行的同时，位姿精度达到或优于当前主流算法，并能生成高保真的全局一致稠密网格。

Conclusion: LXD-SLAM 提供了一个模块化、高精度的多传感器融合框架，通过 GP 子网格建模和 ESC 描述符，实现了在复杂环境下实时、鲁棒的全局一致位姿估计与高质量稠密建图，超越了现有的专用里程计解决方案。

Abstract: Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) is essential for autonomous systems, yet achieving reliable, globally consistent pose estimation and dense mapping in complex environments remains challenging due to geometric degeneracy and sensor drift. While multi-sensor fusion addresses these issues, existing systems often lack the modularity to adapt to diverse platforms and rely on mathematically inconsistent fusion or suboptimal map representations. To address these limitations, we propose LXD-SLAM (LiDAR+X Dense SLAM), a highly versatile and unified multi-sensor fusion framework. Centered around 3D LiDAR, our system allows for the plug-and-play integration of LiDAR, Camera, IMU, Wheel Encoder, and GNSS, supporting up to 32 distinct sensor combinations. We employ a mathematically unified Iterative Error-Sate Kalman Filter with an adaptive hierarchical prediction strategy and an update step that minimizes point-to-mesh distances and visual reprojection errors. To support this, the environment is modeled using continuous multi-layered Gaussian Process (GP) sub-meshes, which enables efficient ray-to-mesh depth recovery for visual features. For global consistency, we introduce an Extended Scan Context (ESC) descriptor derived from the GP sub-meshes alongside a Bidirectional PnP optimization for robust multi-modal loop closure within a hybrid pose graph. Extensive evaluations on public datasets and real-world experiments demonstrate that LXD-SLAM matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized odometry solutions across various configurations while generating high-fidelity, globally consistent dense meshes in real-time. The relevant codes and data will be made available at https://github.com/peterWon/LXD-SLAM upon publication.

</details>


### [178] [Booster Lab: A Data-Centric Pipeline for Learning Deployable Humanoid Locomotion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27813)
*Penghui Chen,Tinglong Zheng,Yufeng Zhang,Mingguo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对高质量可行动作数据稀缺问题，提出了一种整合数据处理、AMP强化学习及Sim-to-Real部署的管线，实现在仿真与现实场景中生成自然的人形机器人运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人运动学习中高质量、物理可行且适配机器人形态的动作数据极其匮乏（人类动作与机器人形态不匹配、开源数据质量参差不齐、模拟轨迹需物理验证），限制了学习效率与部署效果。

Method: 提出了一个数据驱动的端到端管线：包括对动作数据的整理与清洗（Data Curation）、真实运动到仿真模型的适配（Real-to-Sim）、基于AMP（Adversarial Motion Prior）的强化学习训练，以及仿真到现实的部署（Sim-to-Real）。

Result: 在Booster T1机器人上验证了该框架的有效性，并成功在Booster K1上进行了跨平台初步验证，证明了该方法具备良好的泛化能力和实用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的以数据为中心的训练与部署流程（包括数据清洗、模型自适应、AMP强化学习等）能够有效地将多样化且非理想的动作数据转化为人形机器人的物理可行且自然的控制策略。

Abstract: Humanoid robot motion learning requires not only task-oriented control policies but also physically feasible and natural behaviors that can be transferred to real robots. However, robot-feasible motion data are often scarce: raw human demonstrations may be incompatible with the robot morphology, open-source clips vary in quality, and simulation-collected robot trajectories still require feasibility checking. To address these challenges, we propose a data-centric training and deployment pipeline that integrates motion data curation, real-to-sim model adaptation, AMP-based reinforcement learning, and sim-to-real deployment. We validate the framework on the Booster T1 robot and further provide preliminary cross-platform validation on Booster K1.

</details>


### [179] [PPO-EAL: Exact Augmented Lagrangian Proximal Policy Optimization for Safe Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27861)
*Jiatao Ding,Songqun Gao,Andrea Del Prete,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PPO-EAL 是一种新型安全强化学习框架，通过精确增广拉格朗日法提升了约束满足的精确度和学习稳定性，成功应用于复杂机器人控制及 sim-to-real 任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习算法在处理物理约束时，往往难以在任务性能与精确约束满足之间取得平衡，且常存在约束违反或效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出了一种结合精确增广拉格朗日（Exact Augmented Lagrangian）优化与近似策略优化（PPO）的框架，通过引入带二阶惩罚项的裁剪策略更新和动量调节的乘子更新策略，实现更稳健的约束满足。

Result: 在多种机器人仿真环境（如机械臂、四足机器人）中表现优于现有的第一阶安全强化学习基线；在真实的接触丰富齿轮装配任务中实现了零样本 sim-to-real 部署，有效降低了接触力并提高了成功率。

Conclusion: PPO-EAL 是一种通用且具有高实战性的安全强化学习框架，在保证约束满足的同时显著提升了任务性能和鲁棒性，具备良好的 sim-to-real 迁移能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising solution to accomplish complex robotic control tasks; however, most of the current work ignores the safety requirements. Safe RL seeks to maximize task performance while satisfying explicit physical constraints, but current algorithms struggle to learn the policy efficiently with precise constraint satisfaction. This work proposes PPO-EAL, a novel first-order constrained policy optimization framework that integrates exact augmented Lagrangian optimization into proximal policy optimization for safe robotic control. By combining clipped policy updates with exact quadratic penalty terms, PPO-EAL achieves theoretically grounded constraint enforcement without requiring impractically large penalty factors. A momentum-regulated multiplier update further improves dual-variable stability, reducing constraint oscillation and unsafe behavior while preserving task performance. We provide exactness and convergence analysis under standard stochastic approximation assumptions. Extensive validation across diverse GPU-accelerated robotic benchmarks-including cart-pole balancing, cart-double-pendulum stabilization, 7-DoF Franka end-effector reaching, and quadrupedal locomotion-demonstrates superior safety precision and reward performance compared with state-of-the-art first-order safe RL baselines. Finally, we demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real deployment in a contact-rich gear assembly task, where PPO-EAL substantially improves task success, reduces peak contact force, and enhances operational robustness. These results establish PPO-EAL as a general and practically deployable safe RL framework for diverse safety-critical robotic systems.

</details>


### [180] [LocalNav: Distilling Frontier VLMs and Embodied RL for On-Device Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27871)
*Nicolas Baumann,Liam Boyle,Pu Deng,Edoardo Ghignone,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys,Luca Benini,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对移动机器人的VLM轻量化策略，通过知识蒸馏和序列优化，使4B参数模型在机器人边缘端实现了近乎云端模型的效果，大幅提升了推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（VLM）计算开销巨大，通常依赖云端处理，限制了资源受限机器人（如嵌入式GPU设备Jetson Orin）的实时感知与低延迟推理能力。

Method: 1. 构建基于场景图（SG）和Claude Sonnet 3.5的SotA基准流水线；2. 基于500条前沿模型推理轨迹对Qwen3.5-4B进行微调；3. 提出E-RLVR算法，结合Token Generation（TG）正则化以优化输出序列长度；4. 结合模型量化实现推理延迟优化。

Result: 轻量化微调后的模型在HM3D OVON基准上达到34.5%的成功率（SR），接近大型云端模型水平；通过TG正则化和量化，推理延迟显著降低了82.8%。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了通过知识蒸馏将强大的前沿模型能力迁移至轻量级模型（4B）的可行性，并配合Token生成正则化及量化技术，成功实现高性能VLM在移动机器人边缘端的实时部署。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have emerged in the robotic domain as a powerful tool that enables environmental perception with language context, serving as a catalyst for open-vocabulary tasks like ObjectNav. Yet, their computational footprint typically confines them to cloud execution, hindering low-latency inference with local deployment on resource-constrained robots. To address this challenge, we present a distillation strategy that transfers complex spatial-semantic reasoning from large frontier models into a lightweight, 4B-parameter local VLM for edge execution on embedded GPU devices (e.g., Jetson Orin). We first establish a State of the Art (SotA), Scene Graph (SG)-based pipeline using Claude Sonnet 4.6, achieving a 39.7% Success Rate (SR) on the HM3D OVON benchmark. We then demonstrate that fine-tuning Qwen3.5-4B on just 500 frontier reasoning traces effectively enables navigation capabilities, yielding a SR of 34.5%, narrowing the gap to the performance of large cloud models. Finally, we introduce E-RLVR with Token Generation (TG) regularization to compress output sequence lengths for physical deployment while grounding the agent in its task. This downstream optimization reduces TG overhead by 72.1% and latency by 71.8%. Combined with quantization, this joint strategy yields a cumulative 82.8% reduction in overall inference latency without significantly sacrificing performance, presenting a viable paradigm for local, low-latency VLM execution on mobile robots.

</details>


### [181] [S$^2$-VLA: State-Space Guided Vision-Language-Action Models for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27872)
*Zhipeng Xie,Zongyi Han,Xiangyi Wei,Shiliang Sun,Yang Li,Jing Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究团队提出了S$^2$-VLA模型，通过引入SSGAA机制，实现了对视觉、语言及动作特征的动态自适应融合，有效解决了长程机器人任务中的误差累积问题，以更小的参数规模实现了更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在处理长程任务时，因采用静态特征融合机制导致误差累积严重，无法适应任务不同阶段的变动需求。因此，需要一种能够感知任务进度并动态调整跨模态特征关注度的模型。

Method: 提出了一种名为S$^2$-VLA的视觉-语言-动作架构，其核心是状态空间引导的自适应注意力（SSGAA）机制。该机制通过维护一个任务进展的“信念状态”（Belief State），生成动态门控权重，从而自适应地融合视觉特征（空间感知）、任务意图（高层规划）和时序动作序列（执行一致性）。

Result: S$^2$-VLA在仅有2B参数的情况下，性能超过了多种7B规模的VLA模型，并在LIBERO和SimplerEnv等长程操作基准测试中达到了SOTA（当前最佳）水平。

Conclusion: 通过引入状态空间引导的自适应注意力机制（SSGAA），S$^2$-VLA实现了更高效的跨模态特征融合。研究证明，在长程任务中，根据任务阶段动态调整关注点对于克服误差累积和提升机器人操作性能至关重要。该方法验证了模型参数量并非决定表现的唯一因素，架构的自适应设计更为关键。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, but their performance degrades significantly in long-horizon tasks due to cumulative error propagation. This limitation largely arises from static feature fusion mechanisms that rely on fixed weights to combine visual, language, and action representations, preventing the model from adapting to different phases of task execution. To address this limitation, we propose S$^2$-VLA, a framework that introduces a State-Space Guided Adaptive Attention (SSGAA) mechanism. SSGAA maintains a belief state that tracks task progression and generates dynamic gating weights to adaptively fuse information from three complementary sources visual features for spatial perception, task intents for high-level task planning, and temporal action sequences for execution consistency. This adaptive fusion allows the model to shift its focus throughout task execution, aligning with the evolving requirements of different task stages. Despite its compact 2B parameter size, S$^2$-VLA consistently outperforms larger 7B-scale models and achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon manipulation benchmarks, including LIBERO and SimplerEnv. highlighting the importance of adaptive feature fusion for long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [182] [Swarm sign language: motion-based communication between drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27883)
*Thomas Rey,Julien Moras,Alexandre Eudes,Antoine Manzanera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用无人机动态飞行轨迹作为视觉信号进行非主动通信的系统，通过专用的3DTrajDecoder神经网络模型，实现了在抗干扰、隐身场景下的可靠数据传输，并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐身约束下的蜂群机器人系统中，主动射频传输易受干扰。研究旨在探索一种不依赖射频、利用无人机自身运动特性进行“视觉通信”的替代方案，以提高通信的隐蔽性和鲁棒性。

Method: 1. 采用模组化、动态可行的平面轨迹作为视觉通信线索（运动编码）；2. 设计了3DTrajDecoder，利用位姿估计器提取信息，通过分类、时空序列分割及几何参数（大小、法向量）回归进行解码；3. 开发了可配置的在线程序化生成管线，用于生成和训练包含通信与非通信轨迹的数据。

Result: 通过仿真和真实世界实验验证了该系统的性能。消融实验分析证实了架构设计的有效性，并明确了系统的适用范围（操作域）及当前存在的局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了利用无人机动力学可行轨迹作为视觉通信载体的可行性。通过提出的3DTrajDecoder架构，系统实现了在隐身约束下非主动通信的高效解码，为蜂群无人机提供了一种抗干扰、低可探测性的通信替代方案。

Abstract: In stealth-constrained swarm robotics, visual communication provides a critical alternative to active radio transmissions, which might be jammed. This research investigates motion-based communication for non-active information exchange, utilizing modular, dynamically feasible planar trajectories as visual cues. On the receiver drone end, a pose estimator tracks the transmitting drone's pose, feeding it into our custom 3DTrajDecoder. The decoder is designed to classify and segment the spatiotemporal sequence while simultaneously regressing its size and normal vector. To robustly train the decoder on both communicative and non-communicative trajectories, we developed a configurable online procedural generation pipeline. We validate our system through real-world testing and simulation to define its operating domain, supported by an extensive ablation study detailing our architectural choices and system limitations.

</details>


### [183] [Drifting in the Future: Stabilizing Path Following Drifting on High-Latency Vehicle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27914)
*Frederik Werner,Till Heintzenberg,Markus Lienkamp,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于量产车（具有执行器延迟和机械耦合）的自主漂移控制框架，并通过实验验证了其在复杂动力学限制下的鲁棒性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前的自动漂移研究多局限于具有瞬时扭矩响应和独立驱动轮的科研平台，难以直接应用于具有执行器延迟和机械耦合车轴的量产乘用车。

Method: 开发了一种补偿动力总成延迟的预测器；修订了控制公式以适应更高的执行器延迟及驱动轴的差速耦合；引入了基于刹车的速度稳定技术。

Result: 在动力总成延迟超过250毫秒的情况下，受控量产跑车能够稳健地保持圆形和“8”字形漂移，横向误差限制在1.1米以内，侧滑超调限制在0.06弧度以内。

Conclusion: 自主漂移在量产车辆（包括内燃机和机械耦合传动系）上是可行的，这为开发能够在传统控制失效时稳定车辆的先进安全系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomously controlling and handling a vehicle at and beyond its stability limit is a mathematically and computationally demanding task. Prior demonstrations of automated drifting have been limited to research platforms with instantaneous torque delivery and independently actuated wheels, leaving their applicability to production vehicles with actuator latencies and mechanically coupled axles uncertain. To overcome these issues, we design a predictor to compensate for powertrain delays, develop a revised control formulation to accommodate higher actuation latencies as well as a differential coupling on the driven axle, and introduce brake-based velocity stabilization. This paper presents the controller framework, the model extensions, and real-world experimental results. We observe that our controller enables a production sports car with a combustion engine to robustly sustain circular and figure-eight drifts, limiting lateral error to 1.1 m and sideslip overshoot to 0.06 rad despite actuator delays exceeding 250 ms, while mitigating oscillations and maintaining stable path and sideslip tracking. In conclusion, our results establish that autonomous drifting is feasible on production-ready vehicles, opening pathways to advanced safety systems capable of stabilizing cars in scenarios where traditional control fails.

</details>


### [184] [When Multi-Robot Systems Meet Agentic AI:Towards Embodied Collective Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27929)
*Yuxuan Yan,Yuanyuan Jia,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了“具身集体智能”（ECI）架构，旨在推动多机器人系统从简单的任务协作转向基于共享世界记忆、经验与执行状态的深度协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单智能体具身智能正向代理化（agentic）演进，且机器人系统趋向多机协作；但现有多机器人系统多局限于基础的地图与任务共享，缺乏深层的智能状态共享，为此提出ECI框架以解决分布式多机协作的智能集成问题。

Method: 1. 综述具身智能代理化与多机器人协作的发展历程；2. 提出ECI的三个核心维度：共同感知（Co-Perception）、共同行动（Co-Action）与共同演化（Co-Evolution）；3. 通过导航任务案例研究，验证了共享世界记忆（Shared World-Memory Inheritance）在加速新机器人适应性方面的有效性。

Result: 案例研究表明，新加入的机器人通过利用团队共享的融合记忆，能够显著提升环境适应与导航表现，证明了共享世界记忆机制在ECI框架中的可行性。

Conclusion: 提出“具身集体智能”（ECI）作为具身多智能体协作的未来范式，通过共享世界背景、任务进度和技能经验，实现从简单的任务协作向深度智能共享的演进。

Abstract: Embodied AI is increasingly becoming agentic, shifting robots from perception--control pipelines towards closed-loop systems that can retrieve context, deliberate during execution, monitor feedback, and refine future behavior.
