In parallel, robotics research has also moved from single-robot autonomy towards multi-robot systems, driven by the need for wider sensing, distributed action, heterogeneous capabilities, and fault tolerance. As AI agents move from single-agent use towards multi-agent collaboration, robotics faces a parallel challenge: robot teams must move beyond sharing maps, task assignments, and datasets towards sharing the state produced by embodied agent loops. This article explores Embodied Collective Intelligence (ECI), a future multi-robot paradigm in which a robot team accumulates and uses world context, task progress, and skill experience as shared resources. Specifically, we first review how embodied AI is becoming agentic and how multi-robot cooperation has evolved. We then present Embodied Collective Intelligence through Co-Perception, Co-Action, and Co-Evolution. Finally, we use an illustrative navigation study to examine one concrete component of the concept: shared world-memory inheritance. The study shows that a newly added robot can benefit from merged team memory, but it is not intended as a full evaluation of the ECI framework. Taken together, the review and conceptual framework motivate Embodied Collective Intelligence as a direction for embodied multi-agent intelligence, while the case study grounds one measurable part of the concept.

</details>


### [185] [Building a Scalable, Reproducible, Evaluatable, and Closed-Loop Simulation Environment Foundation for Embodied Intelligence Cloud-Native Simulation Infrastructure for Embodied Intelligence Training, Evaluation, and Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27962)
*Junwu Xiong,Yongjian Guo,Mingxi Luo,Ning Qiao,Lei Kang,Song Wang,Yince Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种云原生具身智能仿真基础设施，通过四层架构整合仿真生成、任务处理、数据管理及评估，解决了传统数据收集的瓶颈，为具身智能大规模开发提供了高效底座。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现实世界机器人数据收集成本高、可扩展性差及可重复性低的问题。

Method: 构建四层云原生架构，整合弹性资源调度、容器化仿真、统一数据管理及面向服务的系统设计，并集成了D-VLA, RL-VLA3, Sword, Pre-VLA等关键系统。

Result: 实现了一个可扩展且可重复的云原生仿真平台，支持大规模训练、标准化评估及闭环数据优化，能够有效处理多模型与多任务工作负载。

Conclusion: 云原生仿真基础设施为具身智能提供了数据生成、模型训练、标准化评估及实际部署的统一底座，是推动具身智能发展的关键。

Abstract: This paper presents a cloud-native simulation infrastructure framework for embodied intelligence that supports large-scale training, standardized evaluation, and simulation-based data collection. The framework unifies simulation environment generation, task execution, trajectory collection, model evaluation, data management, and cloud services into a scalable and reproducible platform.
  To address the high cost, limited scalability, and poor reproducibility of real-world robotic data collection, the framework adopts cloud-native technologies including elastic resource scheduling, containerized simulation, unified data management, and service-oriented system design, enabling efficient large-scale simulation for multi-model and multi-task workloads.
  Built on a four-layer architecture, the framework provides standardized environment assets, automated task generation, trajectory collection, benchmark evaluation, and closed-loop data optimization. It further integrates representative systems including D-VLA, RL-VLA3, Sword, and Pre-VLA to support scalable simulation, dynamic scheduling, visual augmentation, and real-time data filtering.
  We argue that cloud-native simulation infrastructure provides a unified foundation for data generation, model training, standardized evaluation, and real-world deployment, and will play a key role in the future development of embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [186] [Translation as a Bridging Action: Transferring Manipulation Skills from Humans to Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28133)
*Sijin Chen,Kaixuan Jiang,Haixin Shi,Yanhui Wang,Weiheng Zhong,Haosheng Li,Bo Jiang,Yuxiao Liu,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种针对双臂机器人学习的“桥接动作”表示法（相对手腕位移），有效解决了人类动作与机器人夹爪差异带来的迁移困难，并通过多模态VLA模型实现了从人类数据到机器人技能的高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人学习方法在利用人类动作数据时，往往将人类视为简单的6DoF双臂实体，但由于手部姿态估计噪声大以及人类手指与机械夹爪在接触模式上的根本差异，直接迁移导致性能不佳。本研究旨在通过优化动作表示，更有效地利用人类动作数据来扩展机器人技能学习。

Method: 1. 提出“桥接动作表示”（Bridging Action Representation）：将动作表示为初始头戴式相机坐标系下的相对手腕平移，从而绕过手指接触模式和末端执行器的差异。2. 构建类似π0的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）：利用交错动作分词（Interleaved Action Tokens）和注意力掩码（Attention Masking）来处理不同实体间缺失的动作分量。

Result: 实验表明，在多项双臂操作任务中，该桥接动作表示比传统的6DoF人类动作迁移效果更佳，且随着人类数据量的增加，模型性能表现出良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过引入“桥接动作表示”（相对手腕平移）和基于掩码的多模态架构，本研究成功实现了从人类动作到双臂机器人的技能迁移，证明了该方法比传统的6DoF动作映射更具鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We study whether we can learn novel manipulation skills from human actions to a bi-manual robot with parallel grippers. Human action data is cheap, abundant, and diverse, making it one of the most promising resources for scaling up robot learning. Yet transferring skills from humans to robots remains hard: most prior work treats humans as just another bi-manual 6DoF embodiment, where hand-pose estimates are noisy and the contact patterns of human fingers differ fundamentally from those of a parallel gripper. We argue that learning rotation-inclusive action signals from human data is therefore sub-optimal, and instead propose a bridging action representation: the relative wrist translation within the initial head-camera frame, an action space shared by humans and robots. To handle the potential absence of certain action components in different embodiments, we build a $π_0$-like vision-language-action model with interleaved action tokens and attention masking. On a suite of novel bi-manual manipulation tasks, our bridging action transfers human manipulation knowledge to robots far more effectively than noisy 6DoF human actions and scales with the amount of human data.

</details>


### [187] [PA-BiCoop: A Primary-Auxiliary Cooperative Framework for General Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28192)
*Bai Qicheng,Wang Ziru,Ma Teli,Dai Guang,Wang Jingdong,Wang Mengmeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对双臂机器人缺乏交互与动态分工的问题，本文提出了 PA-BiCoop 框架，通过引入由任务阶段驱动的主从角色动态分配机制及主从关联的解码器架构，实现了显著高效的协同操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双臂操作方法通常将双臂视为功能对等的独立个体，缺乏有效的交互协作和动态的分工策略，限制了机器人处理复杂任务的能力。

Method: 提出了一种名为 PA-BiCoop 的单模型框架，核心机制包括：1. 动态角色分配模块，根据任务阶段自动分配主从臂；2. 共享全局特征编码器，配合主从双解码器设计（主臂输出基础位姿和核心任务热图，从臂输出相对主臂的坐标位姿）；3. 引入主从协同机制，实现跨臂知识共享。

Result: 在 RLBench2 模拟任务中平均超越 SOTA 基准 48%，在真实世界任务中平均性能提升超过 50%。

Conclusion: PA-BiCoop 通过引入主从动态分工和坐标系关联机制，打破了传统双臂机器人任务中双臂功能对等、缺乏交互的限制，显著提升了双臂协同操作的效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation is essential for advanced robotic systems because it offers higher efficiency and flexibility compared to single-arm configurations. However, existing approaches either lack inter-arm interaction or ignore the need for a dynamic division of labor, treating the arms as functionally equivalent. To address these limitations, this paper draws inspiration from human bimanual manipulation where one arm handles core operations and the other provides auxiliary support, and proposes PA-BiCoop, a new single-model bimanual cooperation framework with dynamic primary-auxiliary arm differentiation. PA-BiCoop categorizes robotic arms into primary and auxiliary arms with adaptively adjustable roles across task stages, employs two specialized decoders that share a global feature encoder: the primary decoder generates the primary arm's base-coordinate pose and core-task affordance heatmaps, and the auxiliary decoder outputs the auxiliary arm's relative pose in the primary arm's coordinate system. Moreover, we design a dynamic role assignment module to automatically map roles to left/right arms without manual pre-definition. This design facilitates inter-arm knowledge sharing and coordinated manipulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our PA-BiCoop achieves superior performance: it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by 48% on average in RLBench2 simulation tasks and by over 50% on average in real world tasks, thereby verifying its effectiveness and advancement in bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [188] [Learning Stable In-Grasp Manipulation in a Non-Dropping Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28196)
*Ha Thang Long Doan,Hikaru Arita,Kazuto Nakashima,Kenji Tahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将经典物理与控制理论融入强化学习的新框架，通过拆解任务与引入理论约束，解决了灵巧手操作技能学习中效率低与不稳定性的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解析控制方法受限于强假设，而现有端到端强化学习方法面临训练效率低、不稳定及学习目标冲突等问题，难以有效习得灵巧操作技能。

Method: 该研究提出了一种基于任务分解（Skill Decomposition）的强化学习框架。将灵巧操作技能拆解为多个简化且可分析的组件，并在每个组件的学习过程中引入经典物理学和控制理论的约束与引导，而非完全端到端的黑盒训练。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在抓取、重新定位/重定向任务中表现出更高的学习效率，并在处理传感器/电机噪声、系统延迟以及多种摩擦条件时展现出优秀的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 将经典物理和控制理论的先验知识融入强化学习（RL），能够显著提高灵巧操作任务在面对噪声、延迟及不同摩擦条件下的学习效率与稳定性，证明了理论指导下的任务分解是解决复杂灵巧手控制问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Traditionally, dexterous manipulation controllers are designed using analytic models constrained by strong assumptions about the hand and the objects being manipulated. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become another common approach in which skills are explored openly in an end-to-end manner but is inefficient because of unnoticeable instability and conflicts in learning objectives. This paper attempts to efficiently explore stable and accurate manipulation skills by decomposing dexterous skills into multiple simpler/analyzable components. Each skill component is subsequently learned with constraints and guidance from classical physics and control theory. Our work shows that for stable grasp, in-grasp reposition/reorientation with different objects, sensor/motor noise, latency, and frictional conditions, skill learning becomes efficient and stable with prior knowledge from theory.

</details>


### [189] [Unleashing Infinite Motion: Scaling Expressive Quadrupedal Motion via Generative Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28237)
*Youzhi Liu,Li Gao,Yifei Qian,Liu Liu,Yang Cai,Ziqiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-Mo 提出了一种摆脱动物数据依赖的四足机器人运动生成流水线，利用LLM和视频扩散模型自动生成多样化动作，并成功在Unitree Go2机器人上实现高成功率的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的四足机器人运动学习高度依赖真实动物数据，这种“类人路线”存在数据采集困难、跨物种重建脆弱以及形态学不兼容等局限性，限制了机器人表现出更丰富、更具表现力的动作行为。

Method: 1. 将数据匮乏问题转换为生成问题；2. 利用大语言模型（LLM）生成动作提示词；3. 通过视频扩散模型合成机器人行为视频；4. 引入“身份一致性损失（Identity Consistency Loss）”确保视频帧间外观一致性，并将视频提升（lift）为3D参考轨迹；5. 使用参考轨迹训练机器人跟踪策略。

Result: 1. 构建了包含7,488个带语言标注的动作数据集（Quad-Imaginarium），时长达18.5小时；2. 在Unitree Go2实机上验证了392个动作，部署成功率达96.7%；3. 模拟环境下的数据集整体成功率高达97.6%。

Conclusion: Uni-Mo 证明了无需依赖动物数据即可通过生成模型合成四足机器人复杂运动的能力。该方法成功打破了数据采集的物理限制，实现了从语言描述到真实机器人高成功率部署的闭环。

Abstract: Quadruped robots have achieved remarkable locomotion, yet their behavioral repertoire remains confined to a few gaits--far from the expressive, companion-like presence long envisioned for them. Attempts to import the humanoid recipe of large-scale motion data have inherited one tacit assumption: that robot motion must first pass through an animal body, making data collection dependent on cooperative animals, reconstruction fragile across species, and retargeting ill-posed across incompatible morphologies. We propose Uni-Mo, a fully automated pipeline that removes the animal from the loop by reframing data scarcity as a generation problem: an LLM proposes motion prompts, a video diffusion model synthesizes the corresponding robot behaviors, and the generated videos are lifted into 3D reference trajectories used to train tracking policies deployed on a real Unitree Go2. To make naively-drifting generations reliably extractable, we introduce an Identity Consistency Loss that enforces appearance coherence across frames. We release Quad-Imaginarium at https://github.com/GaoLii/Quad-Imaginarium.git, the resulting open-source dataset of 7,488 language-annotated quadruped motions (18.5 hours) spanning acrobatic and performative behaviors. We validate 392 randomly sampled motions on a real Unitree Go2 with a 96.7% deployment success rate, complemented by a 97.6% success rate across the full dataset in simulation.

</details>


### [190] [SimFoundry: Modular and Automated Scene Generation for Policy Learning and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28276)
*Nadun Ranawaka,Josiah Wong,Wei-Lin Pai,Wei-Teng Chu,Tianyuan Dai,Masoud Moghani,Hang Yin,Yunfan Jiang,Wesley Durbano,Brandon Huynh,Yu Fang,Linxi Fan,Danfei Xu,Ruohan Zhang,Li Fei-Fei,Bowen Wen,Ajay Mandlekar,Yuke Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimFoundry 是一种通过单一视频自动构建可编辑、可扩展真实感仿真场景的系统，显著提升了机器人策略在现实复杂任务中的零样本迁移能力和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界中训练和评估机器人策略成本高昂且难以扩展，而现有的仿真环境构建通常极其费时费力。

Method: 提出了一个模块化、自动化的系统 SimFoundry，用于从视频中进行零样本实转仿（Real-to-Sim）场景构建，该系统支持对象、场景和任务的编辑，能够自动生成多种保持可供性（Affordance-preserving）的“数字孪生”变体（Digital Cousins）。

Result: 1. 仿真表现与现实表现高度相关，皮尔逊相关系数达 0.911；2. 零样本策略迁移表现显著，引入对象、场景和任务变体后，任务成功率分别提升了 17%、21% 和 40%。

Conclusion: SimFoundry 有效地解决了现实世界机器人训练的高成本与扩展性难题。通过自动化构建逼真的仿真场景及其变体（Digital Cousins），显著提升了机器人在复杂任务中的零样本迁移性能，并验证了仿真性能与现实表现之间的高度相关性。

Abstract: Training and evaluating robot policies in the real world is costly and difficult to scale. We introduce SimFoundry, a modular and automated system for zero-shot real-to-sim scene construction from a video. SimFoundry generates sim-ready digital twins and supports object, scene, and task editing, enabling the automated generation of diverse digital cousins: affordance-preserving variations of reconstructed real-world scenes. Policies trained on SimFoundry data transfer zero-shot to challenging real tasks involving multi-step manipulation, articulated object interaction, and bimanual interaction, and its digital cousins (variations of the original scene, objects, and tasks) facilitate generalization to new real-world conditions. Across 7 manipulation tasks and 5 policy architectures, SimFoundry simulation evaluations strongly predict real-world performance, with mean Pearson correlation 0.911 and mean maximum ranking violation 0.018. When evaluating sim-trained policies zero-shot in the real world, policies trained with object, scene, and task cousins in simulation show average task success rate improvements of 17%, 21%, and 40%, respectively. Additional details at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/gear/simfoundry/ .

</details>


### [191] [CacheMPC: Certified Cached Model Predictive Control for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28300)
*Nimesh Khandelwal,Mehul Anand,Shakti S. Gupta,Mangal Kothari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对四足机器人 MPC 求解缓慢的问题，本文提出 Certified CacheMPC 系统，通过基于局部敏感哈希的缓存与后验证书验证，在确保稳定性的前提下，显著降低了硬件端 MPC 的求解计算延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人的层级控制器中，MPC 的 QP 求解过程较为耗时，限制了在嵌入式处理器上的更新频率；利用四足机器人步态在状态空间中的局部重叠特性，实现 MPC 解的缓存与复用以加速计算。

Method: 提出 Certified CacheMPC 框架：1) 利用局部敏感哈希（LSH）缓存接触力轨迹；2) 采用分段（按接触模式）存储与检索；3) 引入后验计算证书（基原可行性检查 + 拉格朗日对偶间隙界限）确保解的有效性；4) 实现了一个包含缓存检索、限时 QP 求解和备选方案的任务调度机制。

Result: 在 Unitree Go2 机器人上的实验显示，该方法在仿真中实现了 25 倍的中位数求解加速，在硬件上实现了 18.7 倍加速；在 $n=50$ 的试验样本中，其闭环稳定性与无缓存基准线无统计学差异。

Conclusion: Certified CacheMPC 提出了一种利用局部敏感哈希技术对 MPC 解进行缓存和复用的方法。实验表明该方法在保持闭环稳定性的同时，能显著降低计算延迟，实现了实时性能的大幅提升，为嵌入式受限系统提供了一种高效的 MPC 替代方案。

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) is the standard predictive layer in hierarchical quadruped controllers, but the per-cycle QP solve limits the update rate achievable on embedded processors. Because legged gaits revisit a bounded region of state space, MPC solutions admit caching and reuse. This paper proposes \emph{Certified CacheMPC}: a Locality-Sensitive-Hashed cache of horizon contact-force trajectories, partitioned by contact mode, retrieved at query time and accepted only when an a-posteriori per-query certificate confirms primal feasibility and a Lagrangian dual-gap upper bound on cost suboptimality. A bounded-budget controller schedule combines top-$K$ certified retrieval, a deadline-bounded QP solve, and a shifted last-certified fallback. The framework is evaluated on a Unitree Go2 across $2{,}038$ usable cold-controller MuJoCo trials, including a $600$-trial $n\!=\!50$ campaign at three failure-boundary cells, and a first-deploy session on the on-robot NVIDIA Orin NX. The un-gated cache delivers a $25\times$ median solve-time speedup in simulation and an $18.7\times$ median speedup on hardware. At $n\!=\!50$ no statistically significant difference in closed-loop stable rate is detected between the cache variants and the no-cache baseline at any tested cell. The certificate's contribution to closed-loop safety is not resolvable at the present sample size.

</details>


### [192] [WARP-RM: A Warp-Augmented Relative Progress Reward Model for Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28320)
*Justin Yu,Andrew Goldberg,Kavish Kondap,Karim El-Refai,Ethan Ransing,Qianzhong Chen,Mac Schwager,Fred Shentu,Philipp Wu,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WARP 是一种无需人工标注的自监督学习算法，通过对示范轨迹进行“时间扭曲”来提取进度信号，从而辅助行为克隆算法从包含大量次优轨迹的混乱数据中提取高质量动作，显著增强了机器人操作的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习通常依赖海量人类遥操演示，但此类数据往往包含大量冗余、错误或低效动作。现有的进度模型依赖昂贵的人工标注或不准确的绝对计时代理，难以在噪声数据中有效提取最优动作。

Method: 提出 WARP (Warp-Augmented Relative Progress) 框架：1. 通过时间扭曲增强（改变播放速度、反转）生成自监督进度标签；2. 训练 WARP-RM 模型预测帧间相对进度；3. 引入 WARP-BC 策略，使用进度信号加权高优势动作块（Action Chunks），抑制低质量样本（如犹豫、修正动作）的影响。

Result: 在T恤折叠任务中，随着数据集质量下降，WARP-BC 保持了 19/20 的成功率，远优于基准行为克隆（BC）的 2/20；在处理效率上提升了高达 18 倍。

Conclusion: WARP 通过自监督学习获取的高精度相对进度信号，能有效过滤低质量演示数据中的“噪音”，在长跨度、复杂操纵任务中显著提升了行为克隆的鲁棒性和成功率。

Abstract: Scaling imitation learning requires large datasets, yet human teleoperation inevitably produces mixed-quality demonstrations containing hesitations and recoveries. Prior frame-level progress reward models supervise on absolute temporal progress proxies that suffer from label noise, or require costly human annotations to define subtask boundaries. We present WARP (Warp-Augmented Relative Progress), a novel fully self-supervised algorithm for learning dense, signed relative progress magnitudes directly from successful demonstrations. WARP generates per-frame progress targets via time-warp augmentations of demonstrations (variable playback speeds and reversals) and we train WARP-RM to predict the normalized elapsed time between input frames. Aggregating these predictions across overlapping windows yields a dense frame-level progress signal. We then introduce WARP-BC, which leverages these scalar reward estimates to upweight high-advantage action chunks during behavior cloning, where chunk-level advantage is obtained by aggregating per-frame rewards. We evaluate our approach on a physical bimanual robot system performing a long-horizon deformable object manipulation task: folding T-shirts from a random crumpled start. To evaluate policy robustness against suboptimal data, we construct training datasets of varying quality using episode length as a proxy for teleoperation sub-optimality. As the dataset is widened to admit more inefficiencies, WARP-BC maintains a 19/20 success rate compared to vanilla BC's collapse to 2/20, improving throughput by up to 18x.

</details>


### [193] [DexCompose: Reusing Dexterous Policies for Multi-Task Manipulation with a Single Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28323)
*Dihong Huang,Zhenyu Wei,Zhuxiu Xu,Yunchao Yao,Sikai Li,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexCompose 是一个角色感知残差组合框架，通过解析手指级别的动作归属权，并结合双残差模块来复用预训练策略，实现了灵巧手多任务的无冲突组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手在处理多任务组合时，不同任务对手指和接触模式的需求常发生冲突，导致灾难性干扰，难以直接组合现有的独立技能。

Method: 提出了 DexCompose 框架：1. 使用“释放测试”识别维持原任务所需的关键手指掩码；2. 训练不对称的双残留模块：一个有界的残差稳定器（用于保持原任务）和一个上下文感知的残差模块（仅在分配给新任务的动作子空间内调整冷冻的下游策略）。

Result: 在包含 4 种物体保持技能和 4 种下游交互的 16 个复合任务中，平均成功率达到 77.4%。

Conclusion: DexCompose 通过显式的手指级动作归属权和双残留模块设计，为多任务灵巧手操作提供了一种高效的组合方案，克服了传统策略链式组合中的冲突干扰问题。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation policies can solve individual skills, but composing them to perform multiple tasks with a single hand remains challenging. Adding a new task on top of an existing manipulation skill often imposes conflicting demands on overlapping fingers and contact modes, causing destructive interference between preserving an existing manipulation outcome and executing a new one. We propose DexCompose, a role-aware residual composition framework that reuses pretrained dexterous policies for multi-task manipulation through explicit finger-level action ownership. Given two pretrained full-hand policies, DexCompose first collects successful post-task states from the first skill and performs release tests over candidate finger masks to identify which fingers are necessary for maintaining the established skill state. It then trains two asymmetric residual modules: a bounded residual stabilizer for task preservation, and a context-aware residual that adapts the frozen downstream policy only within the action subspace assigned to the new task. We evaluate the framework on 16 composite dexterous manipulation tasks spanning four object-retention skills and four downstream interactions. DexCompose achieves a 77.4% average composite success rate, demonstrating that structural action ownership with dual residuals offers a promising direction for composing dexterous skills beyond conventional policy chaining.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Incremental Dominating Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27469)
*Ilan Doron Arad,Jonathan Gal,Seffi Naor*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了增量控制集问题，通过修正在线算法的性能评估基准，提出了首个针对顶点加权图的有效算法（确定性O(Δ)，随机O(log²Δ)），并将其扩展至连通控制集问题，证明了这些结果在常数因子内是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的在线控制集（Dominating Set）问题在与离线最优解对比时存在极强的下界（Ω(Δ)），导致算法难以优化。研究者希望通过“增量”控制集模型（限制离线最优解的决策顺序）来建立更合理的性能基准。

Method: 1. 引入“增量”模型作为在线算法的基准（即要求离线最优解也遵循相同的顶点到达顺序约束）。
2. 针对顶点加权图，提出了O(Δ)竞争比的确定性算法和O(log²Δ)竞争比的随机算法。
3. 利用线性规划（LP）及其局部连通性约束，将结果扩展至连通控制集（Connected Dominating Set）问题。
4. 在已知邻居信息的变体中，证明了确定性算法可达到多对数级别的竞争比。

Result: 1. 实现了增量控制集问题 $O(Δ)$（确定性）和 $O(\log^2Δ)$（随机）的竞争比。
2. 成功将上述方法扩展至连通控制集问题。
3. 证明了在邻居信息预知的条件下，竞争比可进一步优化至多对数级别。
4. 给出了与上述结果相匹配的理论下界。

Conclusion: 本文为增量控制集（Incremental Dominating Set）与增量连通控制集问题提供了开创性的理论结果，证明了在增量模型下，通过合理的基准设定，可以实现超越传统在线算法界限的性能，并给出了匹配的上下界。

Abstract: Dominating Set is a fundamental problem in graph theory: given a graph, find a minimum-weight subset of vertices such that every vertex is either selected or adjacent to a selected vertex. In online settings where vertices arrive sequentially, comparing algorithms against an offline optimum with full knowledge of the input leads to extremely strong lower bounds, where even a simple star graph shows that any online algorithm must have competitive ratio $Ω(Δ)$, with $Δ$ the largest degree of any vertex in the graph, matching the trivial strategy of selecting all vertices.
  We study the incremental dominating set problem, where the optimal algorithm is constrained to the same choices available to online algorithms. This introduces a benchmark that enables a meaningful comparison between algorithms.
  We present the first results for vertex-weighted graphs and randomized algorithms in this model. For incremental dominating set, we give an $O(Δ)$-competitive deterministic algorithm and an $O(\log^2Δ)$-competitive randomized algorithm. We extend these results to the Connected Dominating Set problem using a linear-programming formulation that captures connectivity through local constraints. When the neighborhood of each arriving vertex is known \textit{in advance}, deterministic algorithms achieve similar polylogarithmic competitive ratios as their randomized counterparts. Finally, we establish matching lower bounds, showing that our results are optimal up to constant factors.

</details>


### [195] [Beating Trivial Time for Tricky Triangle Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27727)
*Neha Pant,Ryan Williams*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在Word RAM模型下，利用多种复杂性理论技术，首次打破了稀疏三角形检测、加权三角形检测及4-cycle检测问题的传统复杂度下限，并提供了一种高效的AC0排序算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对稀疏三角形检测（All-Edges Sparse Triangle, Sparse Monochromatic Triangle）和加权三角形检测（Exact Triangle）等问题，尽管存在基于3SUM猜想的最优性结论，但因3SUM本身存在非平凡改进，作者探究这些“棘手”问题是否存在超越平凡枚举的改进算法。

Method: 采用了随机算法、电路复杂度分析和通信复杂度分析等多种技术，针对Word RAM模型设计算法，特别是利用了多项式规模的AC0操作，并引入了针对4-cycle检测的优化技术。

Result: 实现了对多种稀疏及加权三角形检测问题的首次超越平凡算法的改进；给出了$n$节点图中4-cycle检测在$o(n^2)$时间内完成的算法（针对特定字长）；并提出了一种基于AC0操作的高效排序新方法。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在Word RAM模型下，通过利用随机算法、电路复杂度和通信复杂度等技术，可以突破多个经典三角检测及4-cycle检测问题的平凡算法复杂度上限，且这些算法在特定约束下高效可行。

Abstract: For several well-studied triangle detection problems in the literature, the trivial enumeration algorithms are known to be optimal (up to the exponent) assuming popular fine-grained conjectures. For example, All-Edges Sparse Triangle and Sparse Monochromatic Triangle where each node has degree $n^δ$ for some $δ< 1$, and the Exact Triangle where edges have arbitrary weights, all have this property under the 3SUM Conjecture. However, as there are slightly nontrivial algorithms for 3SUM, it is natural to wonder if the trivial algorithm for these tricky triangle tasks might also be improved.
  Applying a variety of techniques from randomized algorithms, circuit complexity, and communication complexity, we present the first improvements over the trivial algorithms for each of these problems in the Word RAM model. Moreover, our algorithms can be implemented with only polysize $AC0$ operations on words. Extending our techniques, we also show how to solve the notorious 4-cycle detection problem on $n$-node graphs in $o(n^2)$ time, in a Word-RAM model with word size $w > ω(\log^2 n)$. Along the way, we show how to sort $n$ items over a universe of size $2^u$ using only $AC0$ word operations in $O(n u \log n)/w$ time.

</details>


### [196] [Robust Shattering Arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27847)
*Mohsen Ghaffari,Magnús M. Halldórsson,Yannic Maus,Alexandre Nolin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文指出分布式计算领域常用的“图破碎”分析方法在处理长程依赖时存在根本性缺陷，并通过开发无需独立性假设的新分析工具及修复关键算法证明（如 LLL），为该领域奠定了更严谨的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式算法中常用的图破碎分析技术通常依赖于局部独立性假设。然而，作者发现当预破碎过程运行超过常数轮时，节点间的依赖会随时间累积，导致现有的基于独立性假设的分析框架在处理如最大独立集（MIS）、(Δ+1)-着色和分布式 Lovász 局部引理（LLL）等算法时出现逻辑漏洞，亟需修正。

Method: 1. 提出针对长程依赖的系统性分析修复方案；2. 针对 Fischer-Ghaffari 的 LLL 算法提供修正后的破碎分析证明；3. 开发捕捉现代算法通用模式的分析工具（如衰减界限，decay bounds），不再依赖独立性假设；4. 通过构建明确的计数反例证明了原有破碎引理的失效。

Result: 1. 证明了现有标准文献中针对复杂分布式算法的破碎论证存在不可忽视的完整性缺陷；2. 成功修复了 Fischer-Ghaffari 的 LLL 算法分析；3. 提供了一套通用的、可重用的分析工具，可在存在长程依赖的情况下建立破碎界的有效性。

Conclusion: 揭示了分布式图破碎（Graph Shattering）在长程依赖下的局限性，指出了现有文献在处理超常数轮（super-constant rounds）时的证明漏洞，并构建了无需依赖独立性假设的鲁棒性分析框架，确立了该领域新的分析基础。

Abstract: Graph shattering is a central technique underlying sublogarithmic-time distributed algorithms in the LOCAL model. Its analysis typically relies on bounding the probability that large sets of distant nodes remain unresolved, often via independence assumptions justified by locality.
  We show that these assumptions fail for pre-shattering procedures that run for super-constant rounds, where dependencies accumulate over time. As a result, several standard shattering arguments in the literature are incomplete, including those for maximal independent set, $(Δ+1)$-coloring, and the distributed Lovász Local Lemma (LLL).
  We provide a systematic repair of these analyses. Our main contribution is a corrected shattering analysis of the Fischer--Ghaffari LLL algorithm. In addition, we develop general tools that capture common patterns in modern algorithms and yield the required decay bounds without relying on independence. We also present explicit counterexamples to commonly used shattering lemmas.
  Overall, we establish a robust and reusable foundation for shattering arguments in the presence of long-range dependencies.

</details>


### [197] [An Exponential Lower Bound for Spectral Density Estimation on Unweighted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28188)
*Pan Peng,Yuyang Wang,Joy Qiping Yang,Yichun Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该研究证明了无权图谱密度估计的计算复杂性下界也是指数级的，关闭了此前关于加权与无权图复杂度对比的开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决之前留下的开放性问题：即针对加权图的谱密度估计指数级下界是否同样适用于无权图。

Method: 采用理论分析与复杂度证明方法，通过构建特定图结构揭示了算法在处理无权图谱密度估计时的信息瓶颈。

Result: 证明了对于无权图，任何利用均匀随机游走估计归一化邻接矩阵谱密度的算法，即便拥有 $2^{\Omega(1/\varepsilon^{1/6})}$ 条长度为 $2^{\Omega(1/\varepsilon^{1/6})}$ 的随机游走轨迹，也无法以常数成功概率获得 $\varepsilon$-近似。

Conclusion: 在无权图中，对于归一化邻接矩阵的谱密度估计，不存在能在次指数步数或次指数样本数内达到 $\varepsilon$-近似的算法，这确立了该问题的本质计算复杂性。

Abstract: We study lower bounds for estimating the spectral density of the normalized adjacency matrix of a graph. Previously, Cohen-Steiner et al. [KDD 2018] proposed an algorithm for $\varepsilon$-approximate spectral density estimation in the Wasserstein-1 distance, using $2^{O(1/\varepsilon)}$ random walks initiated from uniformly random nodes in the graph. Later, Jin et al. [COLT 2023] established a nearly matching exponential lower bound for \emph{weighted} graphs, assuming the algorithm has access to samples from random walks started at random nodes. It was left open whether this lower bound could be extended to \emph{unweighted} graphs.
  In this paper, we answer this question in the affirmative by proving an exponential lower bound for unweighted graphs. Specifically, we show that no algorithm can compute an $\varepsilon$-approximation to the spectrum of a normalized graph adjacency matrix with constant success probability, even when given the full transcripts of $2^{Ω(1/\varepsilon^{1/6})}$ random walks, each of length $2^{Ω(1/\varepsilon^{1/6})}$, started from uniformly random nodes.

</details>
